## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [Continuous Audio Thinking for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18273)
*Gyojin Han, Dong-Jae Lee, Changho Choi, Jongsuk Kim, Junmo Kim*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.CL, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (79.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Continuous Audio Thinking (CoAT) 框架，为大型音频语言模型（LALM）引入连续潜空间思维模块，通过音频专家蒸馏提供丰富的声学信息，使模型在生成文本响应前先在连续 latent 空间中组织音频信息。CoAT 可在单次 prefill 中完成，不增加自回归解码开销，在 Qwen2-Audio、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 和 Audio Flamingo 3 上均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LALM 的隐藏状态逐渐偏向文本生成，导致丰富的声学信息（如音素细节、韵律、音效、情感、音高）在生成过程中丢失，难以在响应中利用这些信息。

Method: 提出 CoAT 框架，在 LALM 中插入连续思维块（continuous thinking block），该块在连续潜空间中运作，通过音频专家蒸馏（audio expert distillation）将丰富的声学信息注入思维空间。模型在思维空间中组织信息后再生成文本响应。该思维块可在单次 prefill 中处理，不增加自回归解码成本。

Result: 在 Qwen2-Audio、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 和 Audio Flamingo 3 三个 LALM 上，CoAT 在音频推理、音频理解、音乐分类、语音情感和语音转录等广泛基准上均取得性能提升。分析表明辅助监督信号从思维位置传播到模型的文本响应中。

Conclusion: CoAT 通过连续潜空间思维有效保留了音频中的丰富声学信息，提升了 LALM 的音频理解能力，且不增加推理开销，是一种通用且高效的音频思维框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CoAT（Continuous Audio Thinking），为音频语言模型引入连续潜空间思维（continuous latent workspace）来组织音频信息后再生成文本响应。这属于多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning/thinking 的范式，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度吻合。同时论文涉及音频理解与生成，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent-space reasoning for audio, a novel extension of your priority continuous multimodal model direction. Signals: continuous latent thinking, audio expert distillation, single-prefill reasoning, zero extra decoding cost, strong gains on Qwen2-Audio and Qwen2.5-Omni-7B

Abstract: Large audio language models (LALMs) have shown impressive capabilities on diverse audio understanding tasks, ranging from speech transcription to music analysis. However, because LALMs are typically trained to produce text-aligned responses, their hidden states are progressively shaped for text generation rather than for preserving acoustic information. As a result, the diverse acoustic content that audio carries, such as phonetic detail, prosody, sound events, affect, and pitch, is lost along the way and difficult to leverage in the response. We introduce Continuous Audio Thinking (CoAT), a framework that equips audio language models with a continuous latent workspace for organizing acoustic information prior to response generation, grounded by distillation from audio experts. Within the thinking space, the model can utilize the rich acoustic information provided by expert distillation when generating its response. Furthermore, the proposed continuous thinking block can be processed in a single prefill, so CoAT does not require additional autoregressive decoding cost over the baseline. Across three LALMs, Qwen2-Audio, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, and Audio Flamingo~3, performance gains on a broad benchmark suite spanning audio reasoning, audio understanding, music classification, speech emotion, and speech transcription demonstrate the effectiveness of CoAT. Further analysis confirms that the auxiliary supervision propagates from the thinking positions to the model's textual responses.

</details>

### [2] [Sumi: Open Uniform Diffusion Language Model from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19005)
*Mengyu Ye, Keito Kudo, Wataru Ikeda, Ryosuke Matsuda, Keisuke Sakaguchi, Jun Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: Sumi 是一个完全开源的 7B 参数均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM），从零在 1.5T token 上预训练。它在知识、推理和编程基准上与同等 token 预算的自回归模型表现相当，但在常识基准上表现较弱。作者开源了模型权重、检查点和完整训练配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自回归模型和掩码扩散模型已有大规模开源模型，但均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）尚未有从零预训练的大规模模型。为了提供一个干净的参考点来研究 UDLM 的扩展行为、生成动态、可控性以及与自回归和掩码扩散模型的对比，作者从零预训练了 Sumi。

Method: 采用均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）范式，允许任意 token 在任意步骤被更新。模型规模为 7B 参数，在 1.5T token 上从零预训练。训练数据混合以教育类数据为主，全部来自公开语料库。模型权重、检查点和完整训练配方全部开源。

Result: Sumi 在知识、推理和编程基准上与同等 token 预算的自回归模型表现相当，但在常识基准上表现较弱，作者认为教育密集型数据混合是可能的原因。

Conclusion: Sumi 是首个从零预训练的大规模均匀扩散语言模型，为社区研究 UDLM 的扩展行为、生成动态和可控性提供了开源基础。其性能表明 UDLM 在部分任务上可与自回归模型竞争，但数据配比和常识推理方面仍有改进空间。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Sumi，一个从零预训练的 7B 均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM），属于连续潜空间中的语言建模。这与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: First open-source 7B uniform diffusion language model pretrained from scratch, directly advancing the user's top-priority continuous language model direction. Signals: first open-source 7B UDLM, pretrained from scratch on 1.5T tokens, competitive with autoregressive models on knowledge/reasoning/coding, full open-source release including training recipe, directly addresses user's priority subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Among these, uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) permit any token to be updated at any step, in principle enabling more flexible generation. However, no UDLM has yet been pretrained from scratch at both large parameter scale and large token budget. Both autoregressive modeling and masked diffusion modeling already have capable models at scale that the community can study and build on; uniform diffusion has none. A scratch-pretrained UDLM at scale would provide a clean reference point for studying scaling behavior, generation dynamics, controllability, and trade-offs against established autoregressive and masked diffusion models. To this end, we introduce Sumi ("ink" in Japanese), a fully open 7B uniform diffusion language model pretrained from scratch on 1.5T tokens. Sumi performs competitively with autoregressive models trained at comparable token budgets on knowledge, reasoning, and coding benchmarks, while under-performing on commonsense benchmarks, where our education-heavy data mixture is a likely contributor. We release our model weights, checkpoints, and full training recipe, including a complete specification of the data mixture over publicly available corpora. We hope this release enables the community to study native uniform diffusion at scale and catalyzes work on its as-yet poorly understood aspects.

</details>

## 具身世界模型与规划控制 [[Back]](#toc)

### [3] [Guava: An Effective and Universal Harness for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18363)
*Haowen Liu, Xirui Li, Shaoxiong Yao, Peng Shi, Tianyi Zhou, Jia-Bin Huang, Furong Huang, Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (61.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Guava是一个面向具身操作的通用框架，通过系统探索智能体工作流、动作空间和观测空间的设计空间，识别出三个关键要素：迭代感知-推理-动作循环、语义动作抽象和多模态观测。利用少于2000条仿真轨迹蒸馏出4B开源模型，在仿真和真实环境中达到与前沿闭源模型相当的性能，展现出对未见物体、新指令和长程任务的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体通常采用端到端的视觉-语言-动作系统，但这种方式难以泛化且需要大量真实数据。通过具身工具使用（harnessing models through embodied tool use）将高层推理与外部感知、规划、控制模块结合是一种有前景的替代方案，但什么样的框架设计对具身操作有效、以及这种框架能在多大程度上解锁各种推理模型的具身能力尚不清楚。

Method: Guava框架通过系统探索设计空间，识别出三个关键要素：(1) 迭代感知-推理-动作循环（iterative perception-reasoning-action loops），让智能体在每一步交替进行感知、推理和执行；(2) 语义动作抽象（semantic action abstractions），将底层控制命令抽象为高层语义动作；(3) 多模态观测（multimodal observations），融合视觉、语言等多种观测信息。基于这些设计原则，作者开发了端到端训练流水线，利用少于2000条仿真轨迹将具身操作能力蒸馏到4B开源模型中。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的实验表明，Guava框架的性能可与前沿闭源模型（如GPT-4V）相媲美，同时在未见物体、新指令和长程任务上展现出强大的泛化能力。使用仅4B参数的开源模型和少量训练数据即可获得显著的具身操作能力。

Conclusion: 一个设计良好的harness框架可以作为可扩展的、模型无关的具身操作接口，使紧凑的开源模型在极少训练数据下也能涌现出强大的具身能力。这为具身智能体的实用化部署提供了低成本、高泛化性的有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Guava框架，通过具身工具使用（embodied tool use）将VLM与外部感知、规划、控制模块结合，实现机器人操作任务。核心是具身智能体的感知-推理-动作循环、语义动作抽象和多模态观测，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。同时涉及VLM作为推理模块，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strong embodied manipulation framework but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: Embodied manipulation, iterative perception-reasoning-action loop, semantic action abstraction, multimodal observations, 4B model distillation

Abstract: Language models trained on large-scale vision-language data have demonstrated strong potential for embodied agents. Harnessing models through embodied tools use offers a promising alternative to end-to-end vision-language-action systems by combining high-level reasoning with external modules for perception, planning, and control. However, it remains unclear what makes an effective harness for embodied manipulation, and to what extent such a harness can unlock embodied capabilities in a wide range of reasoning models. In this work, we present Guava, a harness framework for embodied tool use developed through systematic exploration of the design space of agent workflows, action spaces, and observation spaces. Our study identifies three key ingredients for effective embodied agents: iterative perception-reasoning-action loops, semantic action abstractions, and multimodal observations. To understand whether these design principles are universal even to small models, we develop an end-to-end training pipeline that distills embodied manipulation capabilities into a 4B open-source model using fewer than 2K trajectories collected entirely in simulation. Experimental results in both simulation and real-world environments show performance comparable to frontier proprietary models while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen objects, novel instructions, and long-horizon tasks. Results suggest that a well-designed harness can serve as a scalable, model-agnostic interface for embodied manipulation, enabling strong emergent embodied capabilities in compact open-source models with minimal training data.

</details>


### [4] [VEGA: Learning Navigation VLAs from In-the-Wild Egocentric Video with Geometric Trajectory Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18426)
*Gershom Seneviratne, Yohan Abeysinghe, Jianyu An, Vaibhav Shende, Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VEGA提出从无标注的自我中心导航视频中训练导航VLA模型的方法，通过重建局部场景几何生成避障轨迹，并用流匹配训练策略。同时发布VEGA-Bench基准，实验表明该方法在碰撞减少和障碍物规避上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的自我中心视频提供了丰富的导航视觉观察，但缺乏机器人坐标系下的避障轨迹和显式导航目标标注，无法直接用于策略学习。VEGA旨在弥合这一差距，利用视频中的几何信息实现可扩展的导航策略训练。

Method: VEGA从单目视频重建局部场景几何，采样导航目标（文本、图像或空间路点），利用重建的几何生成避障轨迹。然后使用流匹配（flow matching）训练VLA导航策略。几何仅在训练阶段使用，将避障规划蒸馏到纯视觉策略中。同时构建VEGA-Bench基准，包含25万场景和约500万导航目标-几何对。

Result: 在VEGA-Bench上，VEGA在保持竞争性目标进展的同时，碰撞减少33.0%，障碍物规避提升17.9%；在真实世界实验中，成功率提升至少150.0%，碰撞减少至少66.7%，障碍物规避提升至少60.0%。

Conclusion: 视频导出的几何监督为训练避障导航VLA模型提供了一种可扩展且有效的信号，证明了从无标注自我中心视频中学习导航策略的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出VEGA方法，利用无标注的自我中心视频训练导航VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型，通过几何轨迹监督实现避障规划。核心是具身导航策略学习，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic 'embodied_world_model'。

Importance reason: Strong robotics navigation work but only tangentially touches user's priority on continuous multimodal models and world models. Signals: flow-matching VLA policy, world model via scene geometry, egocentric video training, collision reduction, VEGA-Bench benchmark

Abstract: We introduce VEGA, an approach for training navigation VisionLanguage-Action (VLA) models from unlabeled egocentric navigation videos. Internet-scale egocentric videos provide a scalable source of navigation-relevant visual observations, capturing cluttered scenes, close-range obstacles, and natural human motion through real-world spaces. However, these videos are not directly usable for policy learning because they do not provide obstacle-aware trajectories conditioned on explicit navigation goals in the robot's coordinate frame. VEGA addresses this gap by reconstructing local scene geometry from monocular video, sampling navigation goals (represented as text, image, or spatial waypoints) and generating obstacle-aware trajectories using the constructed geometry. The resulting trajectory distribution is then used to train a flow-matching VLA navigation policy. By using geometry exclusively during training, VEGA distills obstacle-aware planning directly into a vision-based policy. Furthermore, we introduce VEGA-Bench, a benchmark containing 250k scenes and approximately 5 million navigation goals paired with scene geometry, designed to evaluate goal progress, collision avoidance, and obstacle clearance of VLAs. Our evaluation shows that VEGA achieves competitive goal progress while reducing collisions by 33.0% and improving obstacle clearance by 17.9% over the strongest baseline on VEGABench, while improving success by at least 150.0%, reducing collisions by at least 66.7%, and improving obstacle clearance by at least 60.0% in real-world trials. Ultimately, we demonstrate that video-derived geometric supervision provides a scalable and effective signal for training obstacle-aware navigation VLAs. The code and benchmark will be released at the time of publication.

</details>


### [5] [As You Wish: Mission Planning with Formal Verification using LLMs in Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18519)
*Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui, Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (46.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的精准农业任务规划系统，通过引入线性时序逻辑(LTL)形式化验证反馈循环来解决自然语言歧义问题，使用两个不同的商业LLM分别负责规范生成和验证，实验展示了该全自动管线的能力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统操作门槛高，自然语言描述的歧义性导致任务规划不可靠，需要一种能自动验证规划是否满足用户规范的方法。

Method: 在LLM任务规划器中引入多个基于LTL的反馈循环，使用两个不同的商业LLM分别负责LTL规范生成和规范验证，通过形式化验证确保规划满足用户需求。

Result: 实验展示了该全自动管线在精准农业任务规划中的有效性，同时揭示了LLM生成有价值LTL公式的能力与局限。

Conclusion: 将形式化验证集成到LLM驱动的全自动任务规划管线中是可行的，但LLM生成LTL公式的质量仍有提升空间，双LLM架构有助于缓解偏差。

Classification reason: 该论文使用LLM结合线性时序逻辑(LTL)进行精准农业中的任务规划与形式化验证，属于机器人任务规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM-based planning with formal verification in agriculture, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: LLM mission planning, formal verification, precision agriculture, LTL feedback loops, low relevance to priority subtopics

Abstract: Though robotic systems are now being commercialized and deployed in various industries, many of these systems are highly specialized and often require an advanced skill set to operate and ensure they perform as instructed. To mitigate this problem, we recently introduced a mission planner leveraging LLMs to synthesize mission plans in precision agriculture based on mission descriptions provided in natural language. While the system demonstrates impressive performance, it also suffers from the inherent ambiguities of natural language. In this paper, we extend our system to address this issue by introducing multiple feedback loops in the planning architecture that leverage linear temporal logic (LTL) to ensure the mission planning system meets the specifications formulated by the user while still using natural language. To mitigate potential bias, this is achieved by using two different commercial LLMs in charge of the specification and verification subtasks. Through extensive experiments, we highlight the strengths and limitations of integrating mission verification into a fully autonomous pipeline, particularly regarding an LLM's ability to generate valuable LTL formulas, and show how our proposed implementation addresses and solves these challenges.

</details>


### [6] [DREAM-Chunk: Reactive Action Chunking with Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18589)
*Wenxi Chen, Kaidi Zhang, Chi Lin, Zhiyuan Zhang, Yu She, Yuejiang Liu, Raymond A. Yeh, Shaoshuai Mou, Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: DREAM-Chunk 提出一种测试时扩展方法，为基于 action chunking 的 VLA 策略附加轻量级潜空间世界模型，通过多候选动作 chunk 的潜空间 rollout 与状态匹配选择，在不微调策略的前提下提升长程动作执行在随机动力学下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Action chunking 是 VLA 模型的常见接口，允许低频策略推理驱动高频机器人执行。但一旦 chunk 被提交，其开环执行在随机动力学、硬件执行误差和部分可观测性下非常脆弱。现有方法需要重新训练或微调策略来提升鲁棒性，缺乏一种无需额外训练即可在测试时增强 chunking 策略的方法。

Method: DREAM-Chunk 在测试时采样多个候选 action chunk，利用一个轻量级潜空间世界模型（latent world model）对每个 chunk 的未来潜状态进行 rollout 预测，然后选择预测状态与实际 rollout 观测最匹配的 chunk 中的动作执行。该方法无需对原始策略进行任何微调，仅通过增加测试时计算量来覆盖多种可能的随机未来。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准上，DREAM-Chunk 在递增动作噪声下提升了鲁棒性，且受益于更大的候选样本量，尤其在演示包含纠正行为时效果更佳。在两种机器人平台、四种操作任务和两种 VLA 策略上，DREAM-Chunk 在仿真和硬件实验中均提升了 action-chunking 策略在随机动力学下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DREAM-Chunk 通过测试时计算扩展，利用轻量级潜世界模型有效提升了 action-chunking 策略在随机环境中的鲁棒性，无需额外策略微调，为机器人操作中的开环执行问题提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DREAM-Chunk，通过轻量级潜空间世界模型增强基于 action chunking 的 VLA 策略，在测试时进行多候选动作采样与潜空间 rollout 选择，提升机器人操作在随机动力学下的鲁棒性。这属于面向机器人动作规划与控制的世界模型工作，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes test-time scaling for VLA robustness via latent world model, but focuses on robotics control, not the user's priority subtopics. Signals: world model, action chunking, test-time scaling, robotics, latent rollout

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common interface for vision-language-action (VLA) models, enabling low-frequency policy inference to drive high-frequency robot execution. However, once an action chunk is committed, its open-loop execution can be brittle under stochastic dynamics, hardware execution errors, and partial observability. We propose DREAM-Chunk, a test-time scaling method that augments chunking-based policies with a lightweight latent world model, without requiring additional policy fine-tuning. At test time, DREAM-Chunk samples multiple candidate action chunks, rolls out their predicted latent futures, and selects actions from the chunk whose predicted state best matches the observed rollout. In this way, DREAM-Chunk uses additional test-time computation to cover multiple plausible stochastic futures and improve reactivity during long-horizon chunk execution. On the Kinetix benchmark, DREAM-Chunk improves robustness under increasing action noise and benefits from larger candidate sample sizes, especially when demonstrations contain corrective behaviors. We further validate DREAM-Chunk on four manipulation tasks across two robot platforms and two VLA policies under various sources of stochasticity. Across simulation and hardware experiments, DREAM-Chunk improves the robustness of action-chunking policies in stochastic dynamics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种动作空间（位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量、关节速度）在基于视觉的机器人抓取和推动任务中的表现，发现关节速度动作空间在平滑性和任务性能上最优，并为 sim-to-real 迁移提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界的强化学习中，动作空间的选择对运动平滑性、安全性和任务性能有重要影响，但缺乏系统的比较研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为基于视觉的机器人操作任务提供动作空间选择的实用指导。

Method: 在仿真环境中训练基于视觉的强化学习策略，使用四种不同的动作空间（pose increment、pose velocity、joint position increment、joint velocity），在物体抓取和推动两个任务上进行评估，并通过 sim-to-real 迁移部署到真实世界。

Result: 关节速度动作空间在视觉抓取和推动任务中在平滑性和最终任务性能方面表现最佳。动作空间表示显著影响 sim-to-real 迁移性能。

Conclusion: 动作空间的选择对基于视觉的机器人操作 RL 策略的 sim-to-real 性能至关重要，关节速度是推荐的默认选择。研究为 RL 实践者在仿真和真实实验中选择动作空间提供了实用指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于视觉的机器人操作任务中不同动作空间（位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量、关节速度）对强化学习策略性能的影响，涉及 sim-to-real 迁移。这属于具身智能/机器人领域的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation action spaces, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Robotics RL, action space benchmarking, sim-to-real transfer, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: In real-world reinforcement learning (RL), the choice of action space can play a key role in shaping motion smoothness, safety, and overall task performance. In this study, we evaluate pose increment, pose velocity, joint position increment, and joint velocity across two vision-based manipulation tasks: object picking and pushing. We train policies in simulation and deploy them to the real world using sim-to-real transfer. We find that action-space representation indeed significantly affects sim-to-real performance. In particular, we find that the joint velocity action space is best for the vision-based picking and pushing tasks in terms of smoothness and final task performance. We also provide practical guidance for RL practitioners in choosing action spaces for both simulation and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [8] [EffiNav: Fusing Depth and Vision-Language for Efficient Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18634)
*Zecheng Yin, Benedict Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: EffiNav提出一种融合深度信息与视觉语言模型的高效目标物体导航框架，通过深度感知探索策略和VLM语义理解，在HM3D、OVON和真实机器人上实现了高效导航，在SR和SPL指标上匹配或超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标物体导航方法中，基于训练的模型存在泛化性问题，而非训练框架则效率低下，容易导致重复探索和无效来回移动。如何同时提升导航的成功率和路径效率是核心挑战。

Method: EffiNav融合深度信息（Depth）和视觉语言模型（VLM）进行高效导航决策。深度信息用于感知环境几何结构、避免重复访问已探索区域；VLM提供语义理解，帮助识别目标物体相关区域。两者结合指导下一步探索方向，实现高效路径规划。

Result: 在HM3D和OVON两个标准仿真基准上，EffiNav在Success Rate (SR)和Success weighted by Path Length (SPL)指标上匹配或超越近期基线方法。在真实机器人上验证了有效性，并在GOAT-BENCH的记忆增强导航任务上展示了良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: EffiNav通过深度与VLM的融合，在目标物体导航中实现了更平衡、更泛化的高效导航能力，兼具成功率和路径效率，且易于扩展到记忆增强等更复杂的导航场景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究Object Goal Navigation（目标物体导航），属于具身智能中的导航与规划任务，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot navigation, not core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: object goal navigation, depth-vision fusion, efficient exploration, real-world validation, HM3D/OVON benchmarks

Abstract: To locate a target object while exploring the unknown environment is a fundamental capability for autonomous agents, with applications ranging from search-and-rescue to field robots. A simplified version of such task is Object Goal Navigation (ObjNav). In ObjNav, successful arrival at the target object provides a basic measure of performance; however, the efficiency of the navigation trajectory is equally important, as it indicates how intelligently the agent explores and how much time remains for subsequent tasks. In unknown environments, the key to efficient navigation lies in deciding where to explore next. While many prior works aim to address this core challenge and achieved promising performance in certain settings, recent training-based models and non-training frameworks still suffer from generalization and efficiency issues respectively, which in the worst cases can lead to excessive exploration of already-visited areas or redundant back-and-forth motion. We evaluate EffiNav on two widely used simulation benchmarks Habitat Matterport 3D (HM3D) and Open-Vocabulary Object goal Navigation (OVON), and further validate its effectiveness on physical robots in real-world settings. We conduct failure analysis on massive simulation episodes. With minimal modification, we also extend EffiNav to a memory-augmented ObjNav task on the GOAT-BENCH dataset, demonstrating its adaptability beyond standard ObjNav settings. Across two standard metrics--Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), EffiNav matches or outperforms recent baselines, reflecting its efficiency, robustness, and practical applicability. Recognizing the different emphases of the two datasets, the performances reveals this framework is more balanced and generalizable for efficient ObjNav.

</details>

*Yehang Zhang, Jianchong Su, Haojian Huang, Yifan Chang, Tianhao Zhou, Xinli Xu, Yingjie Xu, Yinchuan Li, Zexi Li, Ying-Cong Chen*

TL;DR: WorldLines 是一个面向长时域具身家庭助手的项目驱动基准，包含带对话、动作、反馈和状态变化的长时间家庭轨迹，并构建了 Memory QA 和具身任务规划样本。同时提出 ObsMem 观察者记忆框架，维护可见性感知记忆和动作原生状态轨迹，实验揭示了部分可观测性、状态覆盖和长期记忆到具身规划的转化等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准主要评估语言中心的检索和问答，而具身基准通常关注短时任务执行，未测试动态环境中长期记忆的使用。需要构建一个能评估具身智能体在真实家庭环境中长期记忆和状态感知能力的基准。

Method: 构建 WorldLines 基准：生成带时间戳的家庭轨迹，包含对话、动作、执行反馈、物体和设备状态变化，并转化为证据链接的 Memory QA 和 Embodied Task Planning 样本。提出 ObsMem 框架：维护可见性感知记忆（visibility-aware memories）和动作原生状态轨迹（action-native state trails），用于状态感知决策。

Result: 实验揭示了部分可观测性、被覆盖的世界状态、以及将长期记忆转化为具身规划等持续存在的挑战。ObsMem 框架为这一场景提供了更强的参考架构。

Conclusion: WorldLines 填补了长时域具身智能体评估的空白，ObsMem 展示了状态感知记忆在长时域家庭助手任务中的有效性，为未来研究提供了基准和参考架构。

Classification reason: 该论文提出长时域具身智能体的记忆基准与框架，核心关注具身智能体在动态家庭环境中的长期记忆、状态跟踪与任务规划，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。同时涉及多模态理解（Memory QA），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for long-horizon embodied agents with memory, but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Long-horizon embodied benchmark, Memory QA and task planning, Partial observability challenge, Observer memory framework, World model direction

Abstract: To assist humans over extended periods in real homes, embodied agents must remember user routines, world states, and past interactions. Existing long-term memory benchmarks mainly evaluate language-centric retrieval and question answering, while embodied benchmarks often focus on short-horizon task execution without testing long-term memory use in dynamic environments. We introduce WorldLines, a project-driven benchmark for long-horizon embodied household assistance. It constructs temporally extended household traces with dialogues, actions, execution feedback, object and device state changes, and converts them into evidence-linked samples for Memory QA and Embodied Task Planning. We further propose ObsMem, an observer-grounded memory framework that maintains visibility-aware memories and action-native state trails for state-aware decisions. Experiments reveal persistent challenges in partial observability, overwritten world states, and translating long-term memory into embodied plans, while ObsMem offers a stronger reference architecture for this setting.

</details>


### [10] [Generative-Model Predictive Planning for Navigation in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18888)
*Thomas Quilter, Yifan Zhu, Guorui Quan, Mingfei Sun, Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出 BeliefDiffusion，一种结合扩散模型与模型预测控制（MPC）的导航框架，用于部分可观测环境中的自主导航。该方法通过扩散模型显式建模多模态信念分布，并利用 MPC 进行前瞻规划，在合成地图实验中显著优于强化学习基线和其他生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中的导航对自主智能体构成重大挑战。基于信念的方法（尤其是用神经网络近似信念空间的方法）往往难以捕捉信念空间固有的多模态性，特别是在高维感知混淆场景中。生成模型虽有潜力，但通常需要大量数据或专家演示，且缺乏长期规划的显式机制。

Method: BeliefDiffusion 包含两个步骤：(1) 基于观测历史，利用扩散模型想象可能的环境配置（即生成多模态信念分布）；(2) 在聚合的配置上使用模型预测控制（MPC）规划高效的导航策略。扩散模型用于显式表征多模态信念，MPC 用于前瞻规划。

Result: 在合成地图环境中的大量实验表明，BeliefDiffusion 在导航成功率和路径效率上显著优于无模型强化学习基线和其他生成方法。结果验证了将显式多模态信念表征纳入规划能够实现更鲁棒的部分可观测导航。

Conclusion: 本文验证了将扩散模型生成的多模态信念分布与 MPC 规划相结合的有效性，为部分可观测环境中的导航提供了一种新的范式。该方法无需大量专家数据即可实现高效规划。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BeliefDiffusion 框架，利用扩散模型对部分可观测环境中的多模态信念分布进行显式建模，并结合模型预测控制（MPC）进行导航规划。这属于具身智能中的规划与控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and planning priorities, but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation subtopics. Signals: world model, diffusion model, model predictive control, partially observable navigation, belief distribution

Abstract: Navigation in partially observable environments presents a significant challenge for autonomous agents, requiring effective decision-making with limited sensory information in unknown environments. Belief-based methods, particularly those using neural networks to approximate the belief space, often fail to capture the inherent multimodality of belief spaces, especially in high-dimensional cases with perceptual aliasing. While generative models present a compelling alternative, they typically require substantial data or expert demonstrations and lack explicit mechanisms for long-term planning. In this paper, we introduce BeliefDiffusion, a novel framework that combines the benefits of both generation and planning. BeliefDiffusion leverages diffusion models to explicitly characterize multimodal belief distributions and utilizes Model Predictive Control (MPC) to simultaneously plan ahead. It consists of two steps: (1) Imagining plausible environment configurations based on observation history and (2) Planning efficient navigation strategies across an aggregated configurations. Through extensive experiments in synthetic map environments, we demonstrate that BeliefDiffusion significantly outperforms both model-free reinforcement learning baselines and other generative approaches in navigation success rate and path efficiency. Our results validate that explicitly incorporating multimodal belief representations into planning enables more robust navigation in partially observable settings.

</details>

TL;DR: ReSiReg提出一种特征重建方法，利用空间一致的VLM中间表征改善语言条件机器人任务中的密集语义检索，并提供了一个紧凑的25M参数密集VLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM的密集嵌入存在噪声且缺乏空间一致性，这阻碍了机器人同时进行语义和3D空间推理的能力。

Method: 将VLM中间层特征聚类为视觉原型，推导其语言描述符，然后将每个patch重建为原型级语言嵌入的软混合表示。

Result: 在OVSS和3D建图任务上定量评估显示密集检索性能提升；真实机器人操作场景中目标激活更空间一致；25M参数模型在性能上与ViT-B基线相当。

Conclusion: ReSiReg通过空间一致的VLM特征重建有效提升了语言条件机器人任务的语义-空间联合推理能力，且紧凑模型具有实际部署价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言条件机器人任务中的VLM语义空间一致性，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic VLM spatial consistency, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM spatial consistency, dense retrieval improvement, compact 25M model, robotic manipulation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable robots to follow open-language instructions. However, dense VLM embeddings have shown to be noisy and lack spatial consistency. This is problematic for robotic applications, which require simultaneous reasoning over semantics and 3D space. We examine spatial structure across recent VLMs and propose ReSiReg, a feature reconstruction method that uses spatially consistent VLM intermediates to improve dense language-grounded retrieval. ReSiReg clusters intermediates into visual prototypes, derives their language descriptors, and reconstructs each patch as a soft mixture of prototype-level language embeddings. We evaluate quantitatively on OVSS and 3D mapping across backbones, and qualitatively in real-world manipulation scenes. Quantitative results show improved dense retrieval; manipulation scenes show more spatially consistent target activations. We further provide a compact 25M dense VLM for robotic applications, substantially smaller than and competitive with ViT-B baselines. Available at

</details>

*Zanjia Tong, Wenlong Dong, Chengjie Zhang, Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 提出 GCNGrasp-VP 框架，结合 affordance 场预测与主动视角规划，实现高效的任务导向抓取，无需场景重建，仅需一次视角调整即可显著提升遮挡场景下的抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务导向抓取方法在物体视角存在遮挡时性能严重下降，而主动视角规划方法通常忽略任务语义且依赖耗时的场景重建，亟需一种高效且语义感知的视角规划方案。

Method: 提出 GCNGrasp-VP 框架，核心包括：(1) GCNGrasp-v2 模型，同时支持抓取评估与 affordance 场预测，实现常数时间推理复杂度；(2) Affordance-guided View Planner (Affordance-VP)，利用 affordance 场作为信息增益度量来引导相机观察任务相关区域，无需场景重建。

Result: 视角规划实验表明，仅需一次视角调整，该方法显著优于基于场景不确定性的基线方法。真实世界验证进一步证明，在单物体场景中抓取成功率大幅提升，同时保持毫秒级计算延迟。

Conclusion: GCNGrasp-VP 通过将 affordance 场预测与主动视角规划高效结合，在遮挡场景下实现了高效、语义感知的任务导向抓取，为机器人主动感知与操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Task-oriented grasping performance degrades significantly when object views suffer from occlusions. Existing task-oriented grasping methods typically assume task-relevant regions are visible in the initial frame, while view planning approaches enable active perception but often ignore task semantics and rely on time-consuming scene reconstruction. To address these limitations, we present GCNGrasp-VP, an efficient framework integrating affordance field prediction with active view planning. Central to this framework is GCNGrasp-v2, a task-oriented grasp model that simultaneously supports grasp evaluation and affordance field prediction, achieving constant-time inference complexity. Leveraging this capability, our Affordance-guided View Planner (Affordance-VP) utilizes the affordance field as an information gain metric to guide camera observation of task-relevant regions without requiring scene reconstruction. View planning results show that our method significantly outperforms scene-uncertainty-driven baselines with only one view adjustment. Real-world validation further confirms substantial improvements in grasp success rates for single-object scenarios while maintaining millisecond-level computational latency. Code and models are available at .

Result: HandTouch 在 HT-Bench 上全面超越基线：细粒度触觉相似性检索 Recall@5 从 74.65% 提升至 85.23%，掩码触觉修复 RMSE 从 0.022 降至 0.010，视觉到触觉合成 OOD cIoU 从 0.628 提升至 0.705。

Conclusion: HandTouch 在灵巧全手触觉表征学习上效果显著，表明大规模第一视角全手触觉数据为灵巧操作中的触觉表征学习评估与推进提供了可扩展的基础。

Classification reason: 论文关注灵巧手触觉表征学习，使用第一视角视觉与全手触觉数据，面向机器人灵巧操作任务，属于具身智能/机器人领域，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on tactile representation learning for robotics, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Robotics benchmark, tactile sensing, egocentric vision, vector-quantized encoder, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Establishing a universal benchmark for tactile representation learning in robotic manipulation remains challenging due to the diversity of tactile sensor designs, data formats, and robot embodiments. Rather than seeking to establish such, we explore a scalable and promising direction for future development: egocentric vision paired with full-hand tactile data. To this end, we introduce \textbf{HT-Bench}, a large-scale multi-task benchmark for dexterous full-hand tactile sensing, comprising 10M RGB frames and 7.8M tactile frames collected across 226 tasks. HT-Bench evaluates tactile representations from three key perspectives: whether they encode meaningful contact geometry, whether they can align tactile observations with visual information, and whether they generalize to unseen tasks. To assess these capabilities, HT-Bench includes four tasks: fine-grained tactile similarity retrieval, masked tactile inpainting, vision-to-tactile synthesis, and multimodal tactile frame prediction. We further propose \textbf{HandTouch}, a vector-quantized vision--tactile encoder that learns tactile representations through progressive spatial, cross-modal, and temporal training. Across HT-Bench, HandTouch consistently outperforms representative tactile encoder baselines, improving Recall@5 on fine-grained tactile similarity retrieval from 74.65\% to 85.23\%, reducing RMSE on masked tactile inpainting from 0.022 to 0.010, and increasing OOD cIoU on vision-to-tactile synthesis from 0.628 to 0.705. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of HandTouch and suggest that large-scale egocentric full-hand tactile data provides a scalable basis for evaluating and advancing tactile representation learning in dexterous manipulation.

</details>

Method: 提出可逆神经网络适配器（Invertible Neural Network Adapter），基于flow matching框架，通过可逆潜空间约束动作生成轨迹，实现单步去噪。该适配器以多模态观测（视觉、语言、本体感知）为条件，直接映射到高维动作空间，避免了传统迭代式flow matching的多步采样过程。

Result: 在多个仿真基准和真实机器人平台上，该方法在广泛的操作任务中达到最优或接近最优性能。真实世界实验中，VLA模型推理延迟从110ms降至61ms，同时保持强任务性能。

Conclusion: 可逆神经网络适配器能够有效实现单步flow matching动作生成，在保证动作质量的同时大幅提升推理效率，为实时机器人操作提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出用于机器人操作的可逆神经网络适配器，基于flow matching生成高维动作，属于机器人具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel invertible adapter for one-step flow matching, directly addressing efficient inference in robot manipulation, a key priority. Signals: efficient inference, flow matching, robot manipulation, VLA model acceleration, one-step generation

Abstract: This paper presents an invertible neural network adapter for general robotic manipulation, designed to generate precise high-dimensional actions conditioned on multimodal observations, including visual, linguistic, and proprioceptive inputs, through a one-step denoising process. Built upon a flow-matching formulation, the proposed adapter effectively constrains the action generation trajectory within an invertible latent space, thereby enabling efficient and high-quality dexterous action synthesis with only a single inference step. Compared with conventional iterative flow-matching policies, the proposed framework substantially reduces inference complexity while maintaining strong action prediction accuracy and stability. Extensive experiments are conducted across a diverse set of simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms to evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Across simulation benchmarks, the proposed adapter consistently demonstrates superior or near state-of-the-art performance on a wide range of manipulation tasks. Furthermore, real-world experiments reveal a significant improvement in inference efficiency for vision-language-action (VLA) models, reducing the average inference latency from 110 ms to 61 ms while maintaining strong task performance.

</details>

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法在3D定位精度和执行可靠性上优于单视图RGB-D定位和微调VLA基线。成功实现了零样本长时程操作，包括对未见物体和工具使用任务的操作。

Conclusion: 零样本框架通过多视图3D VLM推理可以有效实现长时程灵巧操作，无需端到端训练即可泛化到新场景和新物体，闭环重规划进一步提升了任务成功率。

Classification reason: 论文提出零样本长时程灵巧操作框架，利用多视图3D VLM推理生成可执行任务规划，属于具身智能中的机器人操作与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong zero-shot 3D grounding for dexterous manipulation, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: zero-shot dexterous manipulation, multi-view 3D grounding, VLM for task planning, closed-loop replanning, real-world robot experiments

Abstract: We present a zero-shot framework for long-horizon dexterous manipulation that grounds language instructions into executable 3D task plans from calibrated multi-view RGB images. Rather than training an end-to-end policy, our system uses a vision-language model (VLM) to produce reference-frame task grounding and primitive-level 2D keypoints, then lifts them into 3D via multi-view fusion. This lifting combines triangulation of view-wise VLM groundings with reference-view ray voting, which searches along a semantic camera ray for geometrically consistent candidates across neighboring views. The resulting 3D keypoints support both pick-and-place and tool-use: for tool-use, we retrieve an object-centric atomic action corresponding to the inferred skill category and align its stored 6D tool trajectory to the scene; for dexterous execution, we expand the lifted grasp keypoint into a task-conditioned grasp affordance region and generate feasible grasp-motion pairs with an arm-hand motion generator. Real-world experiments show improved 3D grounding accuracy and execution reliability over single-view RGB-D grounding and fine-tuned VLA baselines. We further demonstrate long-horizon manipulation through closed-loop status verification and replan, enabling zero-shot execution on unseen objects and tool-use tasks in novel scenes.

</details>


### [16] [Stealthy World Model Manipulation via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18697)
*Yibin Hu, Xiaolin Sun, Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CR, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWAAP，首个针对学习型世界模型的两阶段数据投毒攻击框架。第一阶段通过双层优化识别一个有害的目标世界模型（使规划产生低回报行为但保持接近干净模型），第二阶段通过梯度匹配和预测误差正则化，仅修改少量微调轨迹的转移目标，使受害者模型向目标模型偏移。实验表明该方法在连续控制任务中能显著降低性能，同时逃避多种防御检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的学习智能体依赖世界模型预测未来状态、规划动作和适应新环境。然而，世界模型从收集的经验中更新的过程存在训练时攻击面：被恶意投毒的微调轨迹可以操纵学习到的动力学，进而破坏下游规划。现有工作缺乏针对世界模型微调阶段的数据投毒攻击研究。

Method: SWAAP 包含两个阶段：(1) 目标模型识别阶段，通过一阶双层优化（利用 transition-gradient theorem）寻找一个有害的目标世界模型，该模型在规划时诱导低回报行为，同时保持与干净模型接近；(2) 目标实现阶段，通过 stealth-constrained gradient matching，仅修改少量微调转移目标，使诱导的训练梯度将受害者模型推向对抗目标，同时用预测误差正则化使投毒目标接近世界模型的自然近似误差。论文还设计了跨三阶段的检测评估框架。

Result: 在多种连续控制任务（continuous-control tasks）上，SWAAP 能造成显著的性能下降，同时保持投毒转移数据接近干净数据，并能逃避非自适应 residual/CUSUM/TRIM 风格的防御检测。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了世界模型自适应微调流程中的实际安全漏洞，强调了需要同时保护世界模型训练数据和所学动力学的鲁棒性方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于数据投毒攻击世界模型（world model）的训练过程，属于世界模型的安全与鲁棒性方向。虽然现有 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 主要关注正向的规划与控制，但该论文攻击的目标正是具身/连续控制任务中的世界模型，且论文实验在连续控制任务（continuous-control tasks）上进行，与 embodied world model 紧密相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Novel attack on world models but focuses on security, not core generative or multimodal priorities. Signals: world model security, data poisoning, continuous control, adversarial robustness, not generative

Abstract: Model-based learning agents use learned world models to predict future states, plan actions, and adapt to new environments. However, the process of updating world models from collected experience creates a training-time attack surface: adversarially poisoned fine-tuning trajectories can manipulate the learned dynamics and thereby corrupt downstream planning. In this paper, we propose SWAAP, the first two-stage data poisoning framework for learned world models. In the first stage, SWAAP identifies a harmful target world model that induces low-return behavior under planning while remaining close to clean dynamics, using first-order bilevel optimization enabled by a transition-gradient theorem. In the second stage, SWAAP realizes this target through stealth-constrained gradient matching, modifying only a limited fraction of fine-tuning transition targets so that the induced training gradients steer the victim model toward the adversarial target, while a prediction-error regularizer encourages the poisoned targets to remain close to the world model's natural approximation error. To assess attack stealthiness, we evaluate defenses and detectability across three stages of the poisoning pipeline: pre-training detection of poisoned transitions, robust training during fine-tuning, and test-time monitoring of the resulting world model. Across diverse continuous-control tasks, SWAAP causes substantial performance degradation while keeping poisoned transitions close to clean data and evading the evaluated non-adaptive residual/CUSUM/TRIM-style defenses. These results reveal a practical vulnerability in world-model adaptation pipelines and highlight the need for robustness methods that protect both world-model training data and learned dynamics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用VLA模型训练需要大量带有精确动作标注的机器人数据，而人类第一人称操作视频虽然丰富且环境多样，但缺乏动作标签，难以直接用于训练。因此，如何从无标注的人类视频中提取可迁移的动作先验，是降低VLA模型对标注数据依赖的关键问题。

Method: 提出一个潜动作框架：(1) 设计混合解耦VQ-VAE，通过物理掩码将运动动态与环境背景解耦，构建跨具身动作码本；(2) 在人类视频上预训练VLM，利用动作码本学习动作意图的深层表征；(3) 引入意图-感知解耦策略，VLM预测动作意图，冻结的视觉编码器提供状态特征给动作专家，减少动作幻觉。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，仅使用无标注人类视频预训练，下游仅需50条轨迹微调，即可与在大规模标注数据集上训练的SOTA VLA模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 证明了从无标注人类视频中提取跨具身动作先验是可行的，为降低VLA模型对标注数据的依赖提供了有效路径，展示了人类视频作为机器人学习数据源的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从无标注人类第一人称视频中提取动作先验，通过混合解耦VQ-VAE构建跨具身动作码本，预训练VLM学习动作意图，并适配到具体机器人本体进行动作规划与控制。这属于具身智能中从人类视频学习动作先验并用于机器人规划控制的典型工作，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal representation learning via latent action extraction from unlabeled human video, aligning with top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: world model, continuous multimodal representation, cross-embodiment action codebook, VLA training from human video, motion-decoupled VQ-VAE

Abstract: Training generalist Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models typically requires massive, diverse robotic datasets with high-fidelity action annotations. While egocentric human manipulation videos are abundant and capture significant environmental diversity, the absence of action labels makes them difficult to use in conventional training paradigms. To address this, we propose a latent-action-based framework designed to extract general action priors from unlabeled human videos. The architecture features a Hybrid Disentangled VQ-VAE that decouples motion dynamics from environmental backgrounds through physical masks, enabling the construction of a cross-embodiment action codebook. By pre-training on human videos with the codebook, the VLM backbone learns deep representations of action intent. For adaptation to specific embodiments, we introduce an intent-perception decoupling strategy where the VLM predicts the action intent while a separate frozen visual encoder provides state-specific features to the action expert, thereby reducing action hallucinations. Results in simulation and real-world environments show that our method, pre-trained exclusively on unlabeled human videos, performs competitively with state-of-the-art VLA models trained on massive annotated datasets, requiring only 50 trajectories for downstream adaptation.

</details>


### [18] [Mem-World: Memory-Augmented Action-Conditioned World Models for Persistent Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18960)
*Zirui Zheng, Jiaqian Yu, Xiongfeng Peng, jun shi, Mingyi Li, Chao Zhang, Weiming Li, Dong Wang, Huchuan Lu, Xu Jia*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: Mem-World 提出了一种记忆增强的多视角动作条件世界模型，通过 4D 腕部视角中心曲面索引记忆（W-VMem）显式建模场景元素何时何地被观测，实现几何感知的历史帧检索，从而在复杂操作场景中生成持久化 rollout，提升策略评估与策略改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型在机器人操作任务中面临持久化建模挑战：末端执行器频繁遮挡和腕部相机快速运动导致当前观测不足以预测未来视角，模型容易遗忘或幻觉早期帧中看到的场景细节。现有记忆检索策略在动态操作场景中难以识别有信息量的历史帧。

Method: 提出 Mem-World，核心是 W-VMem（4D 腕部视角中心曲面索引记忆），将历史观测锚定到随时间演化的曲面元素上，显式建模场景元素被观测的时间和位置。通过基于曲面的渲染和评分，选择相关历史帧作为生成上下文，提供信息丰富且非冗余的预测条件。

Result: 在复杂操作场景中生成持久化 rollout；相比 Ctrl-World，与真实世界性能的 Pearson 相关性提升 14.5%；通过合成数据生成进行策略改进，在长时任务上成功率从 58% 提升至 72%。

Conclusion: Mem-World 通过记忆增强有效解决了操作场景中世界模型的持久化问题，提升了策略评估的可靠性和策略改进的效果，验证了合成数据在机器人学习中的价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Mem-World，一个面向机器人操作的动作条件世界模型，通过记忆增强实现持久化 rollout 生成，用于策略评估与改进。这直接属于世界模型方向下的 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 子主题。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot learning, offering a scalable alternative to costly real-world experimentation by generating action-consistent video rollouts. However, persistent world modeling remains challenging in manipulation: frequent end-effector occlusions and rapid wrist-camera motion make the current observation insufficient for predicting future views, causing models to forget or hallucinate scene details seen in earlier frames. Existing memory retrieval strategies often fail to identify informative history in dynamic manipulation scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose Mem-World, a memory-augmented multi-view action-conditioned world model. At its core, we present W-VMem, a 4D wrist-view-centered surfel-indexed memory that anchors historical observations to temporally evolving surface elements. By explicitly modeling when and where scene elements are observed, W-VMem enables geometry-aware retrieval of relevant history frames conditioned on future actions. During generation, relevant history frames are selected via surfel-based rendering and scoring, providing informative and non-redundant context for prediction. Extensive experiments show that Mem-World generates persistent rollouts in complex manipulation scenarios, enables more reliable policy evaluation than Ctrl-World, improving the Pearson correlation with real-world performance by 14.5\%, and supports effective policy improvement through synthetic data generation, increasing success rates from 58\% to 72\% on long-horizon tasks.

</details>

Conclusion: VLA模型在具身适应过程中存在知识保留问题，Act2Answer协议能有效评估这一问题，为未来VLA训练策略改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Importance reason: Focuses on preference-based RL for robotics, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Preference-based RL, Model-based planning, Uncertainty balancing, Sample efficiency, Meta-World benchmark

Abstract: Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.

</details>

<details>

Method: 提出Encoder-Router网络，通过三个互补参数组（帧参数定向生成器、调制参数控制空间传播、基本系数决定强度）经共享的半群叠加机制生成黎曼度量场。训练时仅使用单个双障碍物场景，推理时通过度量场的测地线计算路径。

Conclusion: 将智能置于空间本身是一种可行的范式，黎曼度量场方法能够以紧凑架构实现场景复杂度自然缩放，并具备强大的零样本泛化能力，为机器人运动规划提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出将智能置于空间本身——场景诱导配置流形上的黎曼度量，动作遵循该度量的测地线而非调用独立规划器。这本质上是一种物理世界建模方法，将环境几何编码为度量场以实现零样本泛化，与"可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel geometric approach to world modeling but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, Riemannian metric, zero-shot generalization, robotics, geometric intelligence

Abstract: Traditional approaches place intelligence in the agent, whether as a learned policy or a search procedure. We instead place intelligence in the space itself: a scene induces a Riemannian metric on the configuration manifold, and action reduces to following the geodesics of that metric rather than invoking a separate planner or collision checker. A single Encoder-Router network realizes this idea through three complementary parameter groups -- frame parameters that orient the generators, modulation parameters that govern their spatial propagation, and basic coefficients that determine their strength. These groups combine through a shared semigroup-superposition mechanism to produce a single Riemannian metric field, yielding a compact architecture whose geometry scales naturally with scene complexity. Trained on a single two-obstacle scene, the model demonstrates robust zero-shot generalization across unseen obstacle configurations, with orders-of-magnitude separation between collision-free and obstacle-penetrating path costs.

</details>


### [22] [Dual-Channel Grounded World Modeling (DCGWM): Structural Prevention of Objective Interference Collapse via Heterogeneous External Grounding with Inward-Only Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18688)
*Akshay Hazare*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (86.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DCGWM，一种双通道接地世界模型架构，通过分区潜空间（物理子空间 Z_p 和行为子空间 Z_b）与内向梯度流，结构性地防止 JEPA 世界模型在同时学习物理动力学和社会行为动力学时出现的"目标干扰坍缩"（OIC）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 世界模型在同时接地两种性质不同的外部信号（物理动力学：稀疏、高幅度、约束满足的梯度修正；社会行为动力学：弥散、分布匹配的修正）时，共享潜空间会导致主导通道系统性坍缩从属通道的表征子空间，且无法通过简单的损失加权解决。

Method: 提出 Dual-Channel Grounded World Modeling (DCGWM)，包含：(1) 分区潜空间：物理子空间 Z_p 和行为子空间 Z_b；(2) 物理接地通道：通过 VICReg 风格对齐仅更新 Z_p；(3) 社会行为接地通道：通过多智能体仿真轨迹对齐仅更新 Z_b；(4) 通道间接口模块：在任务层面耦合子空间但不传递跨子空间梯度；(5) 非对称接地遵守损失：物理违反用硬铰链损失，行为偏离用软 KL 散度；(6) 生成渲染层与潜世界模型架构隔离。

Result: 论文提供了三个理论结果：(1) 分区消除了 OIC 的梯度干扰路径；(2) 每个接地子空间从其对齐目标继承抗坍缩保证；(3) 在生成目标几何的假设下，生成隔离是必要的。实验验证正在进行中，将在未来版本中报告。

Conclusion: DCGWM 通过结构设计而非损失加权来防止多目标世界模型学习中的表征坍缩，为同时建模物理和社会动力学提供了理论保证的架构方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Dual-Channel Grounded World Modeling (DCGWM)，关注世界模型表征学习中的物理动力学与社会行为动力学双通道接地问题，属于世界模型方向。其核心是物理世界建模与交互式环境建模，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a structural solution to a fundamental world model failure mode, directly relevant to your priority on world models and continuous representations. Signals: world model, JEPA, objective interference collapse, partitioned latent space, inward-only gradient flow

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are a leading approach to world model representation learning. We identify a failure mode in JEPA-based world models grounded against two qualitatively distinct external signals: physical dynamics (sparse, high-magnitude, constraint-satisfying gradient corrections) and social-behavioral dynamics (diffuse, distribution-matching corrections). We term this Objective Interference Collapse (OIC): we argue that joint learning in a shared latent space causes the dominant channel to systematically collapse the subordinate channel's representational subspace, in a manner not resolvable by loss weighting alone. We propose Dual-Channel Grounded World Modeling (DCGWM), designed to structurally prevent OIC through a partitioned latent space (physical subspace Z_p, behavioral subspace Z_b) with inward-only gradient flow. A Physical Grounding Channel updates only Z_p via VICReg-style alignment to physical measurements; a Social-Behavioral Grounding Channel updates only Z_b via alignment to trajectories from an emergent multi-agent simulation. An Inter-Channel Interface Module couples the subspaces at the task level without cross-subspace gradients. An Asymmetric Grounding Adherence Loss penalizes rollout drift with a hard hinge for physical violations and a soft KL for behavioral divergence. A Generative Rendering Layer is architecturally isolated from the latent world model. We present three theoretical results: the partition removes the gradient-interference pathway implicated in OIC; each grounded subspace inherits anti-collapse guarantees from its alignment objective; and generative isolation is necessary under a stated assumption on the generative objective's geometry. This manuscript establishes the problem formulation and architecture; experimental validation is ongoing and will be reported in a future revision.

</details>

Result: 在 CAMUS 和 u-RegPro 数据集上的实验表明，DreamReg 相比现有方法具有更好的鲁棒性和有竞争力的配准精度，适用于实时手术导航。

Conclusion: 信念驱动的世界模型框架能够有效利用时序信息和内部想象来逐步精化 2D-3D 超声配准，为实时手术导航提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于信念驱动的世界模型 DreamReg 用于 2D-3D 超声配准，通过学习探针运动动力学和内部想象来模拟候选运动并预测观测结果，属于可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Medical ultrasound registration is a niche application with negative keywords, and the world model framing is tangential to the user's priority on video generation and continuous multimodal models. Signals: world model for registration, medical domain, negative keywords present, no autoregressive or video generation, no continuous language/multimodal

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) is widely used for surgical navigation, yet real-time registration between intraoperative 2D slices and preoperative 3D volumes remains challenging due to partial observability, speckle noise, and the action-dependent US acquisition. Existing methods are one-shot or short-horizon, making it hard for them to gather evidence over time or capture how surgeons adjust probe motion based on on-screen feedback. We propose DreamReg, a belief-driven world-model framework that formulates 2D-3D registration as belief updating over rigid transformations. DreamReg maintains a latent belief state that summarizes past observations and poses information, and continuously refines the transformation through learned dynamics as new slices arrive. During training, DreamReg is exposed to probe-motion trajectories that mimic clinical scanning behavior and learns to update its belief by conditioning pose refinement on the current US observation. During inference, DreamReg refines registration via internal imagination: it rolls out the learned world model to simulate candidate probe motions and their predicted observations, and integrates these imagined outcomes to converge to an accurate rigid transformation. Experiments on CAMUS and u-RegPro datasets demonstrate improved robustness and competitive registration accuracy for real-time guidance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>

*Wei-Cheng Tseng, Gashon Hussein, Yuzhu Dong, Allen Z. Ren, Lucy X. Shi, XuDong Wang, Sergey Levine, Zhaoshuo Li, Jinwei Gu, Florian Shkurti, Ming-Yu Liu, Quan Vuong*

TL;DR: SC3-Eval提出一种自一致视频生成方法，将预训练视频基础模型适配为机器人策略评估器，通过前向-逆动力学一致性、跨视角一致性和测试时一致性三种机制，在无需真实世界部署的情况下准确评估机器人操作策略，在7种策略上达到0.929的Pearson相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中评估通用机器人操作策略成本高、速度慢且难以规模化。虽然动作条件视频世界模型提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，但自回归 rollout 会累积误差、多视角观测需保持一致性，且评估器需泛化到分布外的策略行为。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: SC3-Eval通过三种一致性机制将预训练视频基础模型适配为策略评估器：(1) 前向-逆动力学一致性——联合训练模型从动作预测帧和从帧恢复动作，将生成 rollout 锚定在物理合理的动作流形上；(2) 跨视角一致性——训练模型从其他视角修复每个相机视角，无需显式记忆机制即可保持多相机观测在长 rollout 中的一致性；(3) 测试时一致性——在推理时利用逆动力学模型作为每动作块的置信度信号，当生成帧偏离请求动作时终止 rollout。

Result: 在7种真实世界视觉-语言-动作策略上，SC3-Eval达到0.929的闭环Pearson相关性和0.119的MMRV，优于三种强基线方法，并能泛化到新任务。SC3-Eval的rollout能够复现策略在真实世界rollout中表现出的失败模式，支持细粒度诊断性比较。

Conclusion: SC3-Eval通过自一致视频生成有效解决了基于视频世界模型的策略评估中的累积误差、多视角一致性和分布外泛化三大挑战，为机器人基础策略的规模化评估提供了一种高效、准确的替代方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SC3-Eval，利用动作条件视频世界模型评估机器人基础策略，属于世界模型方向下的视频世界模型（video_world_model）[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及视频生成技术，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model evaluation for robot policies via self-consistent video generation, aligning with top-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model for policy evaluation, self-consistent video generation, forward-inverse dynamics consistency, cross-view consistency, test-time consistency

Abstract: Evaluating generalist robot manipulation policies in the real world is expensive, slow, and difficult to scale. Action-conditioned video world models offer a scalable alternative by simulating policy rollouts. Autoregressive rollouts accumulate compounding errors, observations across multiple camera views must remain mutually consistent, and the evaluator must generalize to policies whose behaviors lie outside the training distribution. We address these challenges with SC3-Eval, a self-consistent video generation recipe that adapts a pre-trained video foundation model into an accurate policy evaluator by enforcing three complementary forms of consistency. First, forward-inverse dynamics consistency jointly trains the model to predict frames from actions and to recover actions from frames, anchoring generated rollouts to a physically plausible action manifold and counteracting the drift a forward-only model cannot penalize. Second, cross-view consistency trains the model to inpaint each camera view from the other, keeping the multi-camera observation coherent over long rollouts without any explicit memory mechanism. Third, test-time consistency reuses the inverse dynamics mode at inference as a per-action-chunk uncertainty signal that terminates rollouts whose generated frames drift away from the requested actions. We also demonstrate SC3-Eval rollouts reproduce the failure modes that policies exhibit in real-world rollouts, supporting fine-grained diagnostic comparison rather than aggregate ranking alone. Across seven real-world vision-language-action policies, SC3-Eval attains a closed-loop Pearson correlation of $0.929$ and MMRV of $0.119$, outperforming three strong prior video-model-based baselines, and generalizes to new tasks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动预测是视觉智能的核心能力，但现有方法通常依赖类别特定的3D边界框或2D像素坐标，缺乏通用性。作者认为3D世界坐标中的点轨迹是一种类别无关、视角稳定、紧凑且可直接用于下游任务的通用表示，因此提出语言指令引导的3D点运动预测任务。

Method: (1) 构建MolmoMotion-1M数据集：从116万无约束视频中标注动作描述、物体锚定的3D点轨迹；(2) 设计PointMotionBench基准：覆盖111类物体和61种运动类型的人工验证评测集；(3) 提出MolmoMotion模型：支持两种轨迹预测范式——自回归坐标预测和基于flow-matching的轨迹生成，以语言指令为条件预测未来3D轨迹。

Result: MolmoMotion在PointMotionBench上显著优于现有运动预测基线方法；学到的3D运动先验可有效迁移到机器人操作任务（提升训练效率和泛化能力），并为视频生成模型提供运动引导以合成更真实的物体运动。

Conclusion: 3D点轨迹是一种通用且有效的运动预测表示，语言指令引导的运动预测框架具有良好泛化能力，可服务于机器人操作和视频生成等下游应用。

Classification reason: 论文提出3D点轨迹运动预测任务，核心是预测物体未来运动轨迹，属于世界模型中的未来预测与动作条件生成。同时其flow-matching轨迹生成也涉及多模态生成方向。最佳匹配的规范子主题为"视频世界模型"（video_world_model），因为该子主题涵盖未来预测、动作条件视频生成等核心内容。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal representation via 3D point trajectory forecasting with language instruction, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, continuous multimodal representation, flow matching, autoregressive prediction, video generation downstream

Abstract: Motion forecasting is central to visual intelligence: agents must anticipate how objects will move in order to plan actions, reason about physical interactions, and synthesize realistic futures. We argue that 3D points in world coordinates provide a general representation that is class-agnostic, view-stable, compact, and directly useful for downstream tasks. We formalize the task of goal-conditioned 3D point motion forecasting: given a short visual history, a set of 3D query points on an object of interest, and a language description of the intended goal, the model predicts the future 3D trajectory of each point. We introduce a full stack to study this task at scale: (1) MolmoMotion-1M is a large corpus of action-described, object-grounded 3D point trajectories annotated from 1.16M unconstrained videos; (2) PointMotionBench is a human-verified benchmark spanning 111 object categories and 61 motion types; and (3) MolmoMotion is a general motion forecasting model that supports both autoregressive coordinate prediction and flow-matching-based trajectory generation. MolmoMotion accurately predicts diverse motion patterns with different language instructions, and significantly outperforms existing motion prediction baselines on PointMotionBench. Finally, we show that the learned 3D motion prior transfers well to downstream applications: it improves training efficiency and generalization for robot manipulation, and its predicted trajectories provide effective motion guidance for generative models to synthesize videos with more realistic object motion.

</details>


### [26] [Where Will They Go? Modelling Multimodal Pedestrian Manoeuvres from Ego-centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18824)
*Yuxuan Xie, Nicolas Pugeault, Chongfeng Wei, Hubert P. H. Shum, Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 普通相关 (56.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPM框架，从自我中心视频中预测行人多模态轨迹。通过行为感知的行人交互模块（PIM）捕捉行人-车辆和行人-环境交互，并利用CVAE-based模式感知轨迹预测器（MTP）分别建模"过马路"和"不过马路"两种模式的未来轨迹分布，使用查询解码器保证模式一致性。在PIE和JAAD数据集上超越SOTA，且MTP模块可即插即用集成到现有框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机轨迹预测器通常从单一单峰分布中采样多个未来轨迹，容易产生"混合模式"轨迹——即位于不同运动模式之间的不合理轨迹。行人轨迹具有多模态性（如过马路与不过马路是两种截然不同的模式），需要分别建模才能得到语义合理且符合真实场景的预测结果。

Method: 提出MMPM框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 行为感知行人交互模块（PIM），通过引入行人注视、头部和手势姿态，联合建模行人-车辆和行人-环境交互；(2) CVAE-based模式感知轨迹预测器（MTP），分别对"过马路"和"不过马路"两种模式独立建模未来轨迹分布，并使用查询解码器在解码过程中保持模式一致性。MTP模块是模型无关的，可集成到BiTrap-NP、SGNet-ED等现有框架中。

Result: 在PIE和JAAD数据集上的实验表明，MMPM超越现有SOTA基线方法。引入的数据驱动验证协议将预测与时空一致的真实轨迹匹配，在逐帧位移误差指标上优于先前工作。MTP模块集成到现有框架后进一步提升了轨迹预测性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了对行人轨迹进行显式的模式感知建模（区分过马路与不过马路）能够有效避免混合模式轨迹问题，提升预测的语义合理性和准确性。提出的MTP模块具有通用性，可作为即插即用组件增强现有轨迹预测框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究行人轨迹预测（pedestrian trajectory prediction），本质上是未来预测/动作条件视频生成问题，属于世界模型方向中的视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题。论文从自我中心视频中建模行人多模态轨迹分布，与视频世界模型的未来预测和动作条件生成高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on pedestrian trajectory prediction, not core continuous multimodal or video generation world models. Signals: pedestrian trajectory prediction, ego-centric video, multimodal prediction, CVAE-based, PIE/JAAD datasets

Abstract: Pedestrian trajectory prediction from an ego-centric camera is challenging since it depends on complex interactions with vehicles and scene context, as well as the intention of the pedestrian. By modelling correlation and intent from the historical and future trajectories of the pedestrian, it will usually result in a multimodal (i.e. multiple modes) distribution. Existing stochastic predictors often sample multiple futures from a single unimodal distribution, which can yield sub-optimal 'mixed-mode' trajectories that lie between distinct motion patterns and become implausible in real scenes. In this paper, we propose MMPM, a mode-aware framework that separately models future trajectory distributions into semantically meaningful modes based on the pedestrian's crossing behavior. MMPM consists of two modules: behavior-aware Pedestrian Interaction Module (PIM) that jointly captures pedestrian-vehicle and pedestrian-environment interactions by introducing gaze, head and hand gesture, and a CVAE-based Mode-aware Trajectory Predictor (MTP) module to model the future trajectory distributions on two modes, crossing and non-crossing the road, separately. A query-based decoder further enforces mode consistency during decoding. Experiments on PIE and JAAD datasets show that our method surpasses state-of-the-art baselines. Our proposed MTP is model-agnostic, which can be integrated into existing frameworks such as BiTrap-NP and SGNet-ED to further improve future trajectory prediction performance. We additionally introduce a data-driven validation protocol that matches predictions to spatio-temporally consistent ground-truth trajectories, demonstrating improved frame-wise displacement errors over previous work.

</details>

TL;DR: CAOA 提出了一种结合点云补全与对称感知姿态估计的方法，用于将 CAD 模型精确对齐到室内 RGB-D 扫描中的物体，在 Scan2CAD 基准上相比 SOTA 提升 17% 的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内 RGB-D 扫描存在噪声、不完整以及分割误差导致的几何畸变，使得 CAD 模型与扫描物体的精确对齐（9-DoF 姿态估计）极具挑战性。现有方法受限于合成数据与真实场景的域差距，且缺乏针对真实室内场景的点云补全基准。

Method: CAOA 包含两个核心模块：(1) 语义与上下文感知的点云补全模块，用于修复扫描物体的几何缺失；(2) 对称感知的相对姿态估计算法，利用对称性损失函数处理对称歧义。同时提出了一种面向室内场景的合成数据生成策略以缩小合成到真实的域差距，并发布了 S2C-Completion 数据集（8500+ 对物体-CAD 对）作为真实室内单物体补全的基准。

Result: 在 Scan2CAD 基准上，CAOA 相比现有最先进方法实现了 17% 的精度提升。通过定量对比验证了合成数据生成策略的有效性，显著缩小了合成到真实的域差距。

Conclusion: CAOA 通过结合点云补全与对称感知姿态估计，有效解决了 CAD 模型与真实扫描物体的对齐难题，为 3D 语义重建提供了更精确的解决方案。所提出的合成数据策略和 S2C-Completion 数据集为后续研究提供了有价值的资源。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 CAD 模型与 RGB-D 扫描物体的对齐（3D 语义重建），属于 3D 物体/场景重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D CAD alignment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong overlap with user's key interests in continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3D reconstruction, point cloud completion, CAD alignment, Scan2CAD benchmark, synthetic-to-real domain gap

Abstract: Accurately aligning CAD models to their corresponding objects in indoor RGB-D scans is a central challenge in 3D semantic reconstruction. The task requires estimating a 9-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) pose-position, rotation, and scale along three axes-but is hindered by noisy and incomplete scans, as well as segmentation errors that cause geometric distortions. We present Completion-Assisted Object-CAD Alignment (CAOA), a method that integrates a semantically and contextually aware point cloud completion module with a symmetry-aware relative pose estimation algorithm, enabling precise alignment of CAD models to scanned objects. Existing completion methods are typically trained and evaluated on synthetic datasets, which often fail to generalize to real-world scans. To bridge this gap, we introduce a synthetic data generation strategy tailored to indoor scenes, significantly reducing the synthetic-to-real domain gap-validated through quantitative comparisons with widely used completion datasets. In addition, we release S2C-Completion, an expert-annotated dataset of over 8,500 object-CAD pairs from Scan2CAD, created for real-world indoor single-object completion and intended as a new benchmark for this task. For object-CAD alignment, we incorporate symmetry information via a symmetry-aware loss, improving robustness to symmetric ambiguities. On the Scan2CAD benchmark, CAOA achieves a 17% accuracy improvement over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>

*Yuchen Rao, Xuqian Ren, Yinyu Nie, Sayan Deb Sarkar, Biao Zhang, Vincent Lepetit, Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowObject 提出一种无训练引导框架，通过双空间引导策略控制 Flow-Matching 模型的 ODE 轨迹，结合 3DGS 精炼阶段，在稀疏视角 3D 重建中同时实现生成先验驱动的未观测区域补全和观测一致性保持，显著优于现有生成模型和优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视角 3D 重建方法存在两难：生成模型（如 Flow-Matching）能合成高质量纹理资产但存在"合成偏差"，覆盖观测证据；优化方法（如 3DGS）在可见表面保真度高但无法推理未观测几何。本文旨在弥合生成先验与重建保真度之间的鸿沟。

Method: FlowObject 将稀疏视角 3D 重建重新表述为无训练的引导逆问题。采用双空间引导策略，在 Flow-Matching 模型的 ODE 轨迹中施加约束，利用生成先验补全未观测区域，同时强制与真实观测严格一致。最后通过 3DGS 精炼阶段将"合成感"输出转化为逼真重建。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的综合基准测试表明，FlowObject 在几何完整性和视角相关外观保真度上显著优于现有生成模型和优化方法，尤其在严重遮挡场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: FlowObject 通过双空间引导策略有效桥接了生成先验与重建保真度之间的差距，为稀疏视角 3D 物体重建提供了一种无需额外训练的高效框架，在几何完整性和观测一致性上均取得最优结果。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FlowObject，利用 Flow-Matching 生成先验与 3DGS 优化相结合，从稀疏视角图像恢复完整 3D 物体表征，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel training-free guidance for flow-matching in 3D reconstruction, but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: flow-matching guidance, 3D reconstruction, generative priors, sparse-view, 3DGS refinement

Abstract: Recovering complete 3D representations of objects from few casual image captures remains a significant challenge. Recent 3D generative models, particularly those based on Flow-Matching (FM), can synthesize high-quality textured assets; however, they often suffer from ''synthetic bias'' where learned priors override observational evidence, alongside a lack of alignment with the observed instance. Conversely, optimization-based methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provide high fidelity on visible surfaces but fail to reason about unobserved geometry. In this paper, we present FlowObject, a framework that reformulates sparse-view 3D reconstruction as a training-free, guided inverse problem. Our approach applies a dual-space guidance strategy to steer the Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) trajectory of a flow-matching model, enabling the completion of unseen regions through learned generative priors while enforcing strict consistency with real-world observations. By integrating a 3DGS refinement stage, FlowObject further bridges the gap between ''synthetic-looking'' generative outputs and photorealistic reconstructions. Comprehensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that current state-of-the-art methods often struggle to achieve geometric completeness and observational consistency simultaneously, especially under severe occlusions. In contrast, our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art generative models and optimization-based frameworks in both geometric completeness and view-dependent appearance fidelity.

</details>

Result: 在 H2O 和 ARCTIC 两个具有挑战性的第一人称数据集上，Hand-4DGS 显著优于基线方法，实现了约60 FPS的快速推理速度，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Hand-4DGS 证明了前馈式网络结合3D高斯泼溅可以有效解决第一人称4D手部重建难题，为AR/VR等应用提供了高效且泛化性强的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的前馈式动态4D手部重建方法，属于3D生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel feed-forward 4D hand reconstruction from egocentric video, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, egocentric video, dynamic hand reconstruction, feed-forward, ~60 FPS inference

Abstract: Dynamic 3D hand reconstruction from egocentric videos is essential for next-generation computing platforms such as AR/VR and AI glasses. Despite its importance, most prior works focus either on multi-view 3D hand reconstruction or on 4D human body reconstruction. Egocentric 4D hand reconstruction remains challenging due to fast head motion, rapid hand dynamics, severe occlusions, and inherent ambiguity from single-view observations. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand-4DGS, the first feed-forward framework for reconstructing dynamic 4D hands directly from egocentric videos, enabling both fast (~60 FPS) inference and strong generalization. Our approach incorporates a mesh-guided representation for structural priors and temporal convolutions to model dynamic motion. We evaluate our framework on two challenging egocentric datasets, H2O and ARCTIC, and demonstrate significant improvements over baselines. Our method benefits from the generalization capability of feed-forward networks and effective 2D image supervision through Gaussian splatting, without requiring expensive 3D hand pose ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [30] [Splaxel: Efficient Distributed Training of 3D Gaussian Splatting for Large-scale Scene Reconstruction via Pixel-level Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18588)
*Wenqi Jia, Zhewen Hu, Ying Huang, Yu Gong, Stavros Kalafatis, Yuke Wang, Wei Niu, Chengming Zhang, Ang Li, Sheng Di, Yuede Ji, Bo Fang, Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.DC

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Splaxel 提出了一种基于像素级通信的高效分布式 3DGS 训练框架，通过局部渲染与全局组合替代高斯级同步，在大规模场景重建中实现高达 7.6 倍加速，同时保持重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式 3DGS 方法要么将场景分割为孤立区域导致全局不一致，要么依赖全局高斯级交换导致通信量随场景规模急剧增长，难以扩展到大规模场景（上亿高斯体）。

Method: 提出 Splaxel 框架：每个 GPU 渲染其局部高斯子集，仅交换部分像素值（而非高斯体）实现全局组合，保持数学一致性且通信量稳定；通过几何与透射率可见性预测减少像素级冗余；通过无冲突的相机视图合并提高 GPU 利用率。

Result: 在包含多达 1.2 亿高斯体的大规模数据集上，Splaxel 相比最先进的分布式 3DGS 框架实现最高 7.6 倍加速，同时保持高重建质量。

Conclusion: Splaxel 证明了像素级通信策略可以有效替代高斯级同步，在保持重建质量的同时大幅提升分布式 3DGS 训练的可扩展性和效率，为大规模场景重建提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大规模3D场景重建中的分布式3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)训练，属于3D生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on distributed training efficiency for 3DGS, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Distributed training, 3D Gaussian Splatting, Large-scale scene reconstruction, Pixel-level communication, Efficiency improvement

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity and real-time 3D scene reconstruction, but scaling training to large-scale scenes requires optimizing hundreds of millions of Gaussians across multiple GPUs. Existing distributed approaches either partition scenes into isolated regions, causing global inconsistency, or rely on global Gaussian-level exchanges, which lead to substantial growth in inter-GPU communication and quickly dominate iteration time. We propose Splaxel, a communication-efficient distributed 3DGS training framework based on pixel-level local rendering and global composition. Instead of synchronizing Gaussians, each GPU renders its local subset and exchanges only partial pixel values, maintaining mathematical consistency while keeping communication cost stable as the scene size increases. Splaxel further reduces pixel-level redundancy through geometric and transmittance visibility prediction and improves GPU utilization via conflict-free camera-view consolidation. Evaluated on large-scale datasets with up to 120M Gaussians, Splaxel achieves up to 7.6$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art distributed 3DGS framework while preserving high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [31] [EDoF-NeRF: extended depth-of-field neural radiance fields using a coded aperture camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18826)
*Yoshiyuki Shirasaki, Ryoichi Horisaki*

Main category: cs.CV, physics.optics, eess.IV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 EDoF-NeRF，通过在相机光瞳处放置编码光圈，将编码光圈相机模型融入 NeRF，直接输入编码图像以生成具有扩展景深的新视角渲染，在仿真和实验中优于传统光圈相机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机存在景深与进光量之间的固有权衡，这一权衡也传递给了基于这些相机采集数据训练的 NeRF 模型。现有 NeRF 在景深受限的数据集上难以重建高质量场景，因此需要一种方法在保持进光量的同时扩展有效景深。

Method: 在 NeRF 的相机模型中引入编码光圈（coded aperture），通过编码光圈保留离焦条件下的空间频率成分。将编码图像直接作为 NeRF 输入，训练隐式神经表示，从而在渲染新视角时获得扩展的景深效果。

Result: 通过仿真和真实实验验证，EDoF-NeRF 相比使用传统光圈相机的 NeRF 方法，在景深扩展和渲染质量上均表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: 编码光圈可以有效缓解 NeRF 中的景深-进光量权衡问题，将编码光圈相机模型集成到 NeRF 框架中是提升 NeRF 在受限景深条件下重建质量的有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种使用编码光圈相机扩展景深的 NeRF 方法，属于 NeRF 神经渲染与重建方向，与 [CANONICAL] 3D 生成与重建 (three_d_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: NeRF with coded aperture for extended depth-of-field is a niche optics+CV contribution, not aligned with user's priority on autoregressive/diffusion video/image generation or continuous multimodal models. Signals: NeRF, coded aperture, extended depth-of-field, novel view synthesis, not autoregressive

Abstract: We propose a method for extending the depth-of-field (DoF) to construct high-fidelity neural radiance fields (NeRF) -- an emerging technique for rendering photorealistic novel views from a dataset of images captured at different viewpoints, based on implicit neural representations. The trade-off between DoF and light quantity is inherent not only in conventional cameras but also in NeRF, since the datasets used by NeRF are captured by these cameras. To address this issue, we introduce a coded aperture placed at the camera pupil, preserving spatial frequency components under defocused conditions. We develop a camera model incorporating coded apertures into NeRF, allowing direct input of coded images and enabling the generation of novel views with an extended DoF. We validate the proposed method, termed extended DoF-NeRF (EDoF-NeRF), through simulations and experiments, demonstrating its superior performance compared to conventional aperture cameras.

</details>
