### [32] [Intrinsic 4D Gaussian Segmentation from Scene Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18623)
*Hasan Yazar, Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi, Erchin Serpedin, Mehmet Tuncel, Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV, eess.IV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Intrinsic-GS，一种无需训练、无需掩码的4D高斯分割方法，通过构建高斯原语间的稀疏亲和图并利用Leiden社区检测进行分割，在Neu3D和HyperNeRF基准上取得了与SAM监督方法可比的性能，且速度提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D高斯分割方法依赖SAM等基础模型生成2D掩码并提升到3D/4D表示中，这在动态场景中需要在多帧多视角下生成掩码，成本高昂且结果受外部掩码质量影响。作者探究能否仅从高斯原语自身恢复物体级结构信息，从而实现快速、鲁棒且无需外部掩码的分割。

Method: Intrinsic-GS从高斯原语的外观、朝向、尺度、形变轨迹和非学习的渲染边界线索中构建稀疏亲和图，然后使用Leiden社区检测算法对图进行划分，无需任何基础模型或学习到的特征场。

Result: 在Neu3D上达到0.746 mIoU，在HyperNeRF上达到0.575 mIoU；在Neu3D上仅使用几何线索的变体达到0.902 mIoU，与SAM监督的TRASE方法相当。在HyperNeRF上，运行速度比掩码生成和特征渲染阶段快12.5倍。

Conclusion: 大量分割信号已编码在高斯原语自身中，Intrinsic-GS提供了一种快速、无需掩码的4D高斯分割方向，在外部掩码不可靠或成本高昂的场景中具有更好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态4D高斯泼溅场景中的分割方法，属于4D动态场景处理范畴，与[多模态生成]方向下的[4D与动态场景生成]（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel training-free 4D segmentation method, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Training-free 4D Gaussian segmentation, Leiden community detection, 12.5x speedup vs SAM-supervised, geometry-only variant matches SAM-supervised on Neu3D, no foundation model needed

Abstract: Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs deforming scenes with high fidelity and is increasingly adopted as a representation for dynamic 3D scenes. Putting such a scene to use, for editing, manipulation or motion analysis, first requires segmenting it: grouping the Gaussian primitives into coherent objects. Current pipelines obtain this grouping by importing 2D masks from foundation models such as SAM and lifting or distilling them into the Gaussian representation. In dynamic scenes these masks must be generated across many frames and views, which is costly, and the resulting segmentation can depend strongly on the quality and consistency of those external masks. We ask how much object-level structure can instead be recovered from the Gaussians themselves, and propose Intrinsic-GS, a training-free, mask-free method that builds a sparse affinity graph over Gaussian primitives from appearance, orientation, scale, deformation-trajectory and non-learned rendered-boundary cues. The graph is partitioned with Leiden community detection, requiring no foundation model and no learned feature field. On the standard 4D Gaussian segmentation benchmarks, Neu3D and HyperNeRF, Intrinsic-GS recovers substantial object structure without mask supervision, reaching 0.746 mIoU on Neu3D and 0.575 on HyperNeRF; on Neu3D, a geometry-only variant reaches 0.902 mIoU, matching SAM-supervised TRASE. On HyperNeRF, Intrinsic-GS runs 12.5x faster than the mask-generation and feature-rendering stages used by mask-supervised pipelines. These results suggest that much of the segmentation signal is already encoded in the Gaussians themselves, offering a fast, mask-free direction for 3D and 4D Gaussian segmentation that may also point toward more generalizable, robust segmentation in settings where external masks are unreliable or expensive.

</details>

*Denis Savytski, Aiden Lei, Heding Liu, Warren Yang, Sihan Liang, Alexander Liu, Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: 本文提出 CHIEF，一个以创作者为中心的人机协作视频生成框架，通过迭代式精炼和基于多模态 LLM 的 agent 反馈循环，使无电影制作经验的学生也能创作出从1分钟短片到10分钟完整短片的叙事连贯视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 视频生成工具生成的长视频缺乏叙事连贯性和创意方向，且视频生成不像代码生成那样有可靠的自动反馈机制，需要主观的叙事、场景和情节评估。因此需要一种将人类创意方向融入迭代视频精炼的方法。

Method: 提出 CHIEF 框架，核心是 human-in-the-loop 迭代视频精炼：创作者驱动每次迭代，提供创意方向；专门的 refiner agent 将创作者的修改意见融入视频；同时使用 persona-conditioned 多模态 LLM 作为评审 agent，从观众视角对生成视频提供主观批评反馈，弥补自我评估的不足。

Result: 与高中和大学生（无电影制作经验）合作，成功创作了从1分钟短片到10分钟完整短片的视频，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: CHIEF 框架通过将人类创意方向与自动主观反馈相结合，有效提升了 AI 生成视频的叙事连贯性和创意质量，使非专业人士也能创作较长的叙事视频。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CHIEF 框架，通过人机协作的迭代式视频精炼（human-in-the-loop iterative video refinement），结合 agent 反馈循环进行视频生成与编辑。核心是视频的迭代式编辑与改进，属于视频编辑范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）。同时涉及多模态 LLM 作为评审 agent，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-in-the-loop video generation with agentic feedback, which is not aligned with the user's priority on autoregressive or diffusion video generation models. Signals: human-AI co-creation, iterative video refinement, agentic feedback loops, narrative coherence, no filmmaking experience

Abstract: Generative AI has made content creation increasingly accessible, but many AI-generated videos lack narrative coherence and creative direction, issues that become more substantial at longer durations. Unlike coding, where AI generation benefits from reliable feedback and techniques such as recurrent self-improvement, video generation requires subjective feedback about plot, scenes, and narrative, which naturally motivates approaches that incorporate human creative direction. We introduce CHIEF, a human-AI co-creation video generation framework that places the creator at the center of human-in-the-loop iterative video refinement, and supports them by providing automatic subjective feedback. The creator incorporates their creative direction by driving each iteration, while their revisions are incorporated by a specialized refiner agent. The feedback loop is generated by persona-conditioned multimodal LLMs that watch generated videos and produce subjective critique from the audience perspectives, providing feedback that self-evaluation alone cannot capture. To test the effectiveness of our proposed framework, we work with high school and college students with no prior filmmaking experience to create videos, from short 1-minute videos to a complete short 10-minute film with a complicated plot.

</details>


### [34] [BindEdit: Taming Attention Leakage for Precise Multi-Object Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18906)
*Chaewon Park, Soyoon Lee, Naeun Lee, Minjung Shin, Seogkyu Jeon, Kibeom Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (80.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BindEdit 通过约束注意力泄漏（Edit-Token Leakage 和 Source Dominance Leakage），在单次扩散轨迹中实现精确的多目标图像编辑，并提出了新的多目标编辑基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法在复杂多目标场景中常出现语义混合、物体重复或编辑不完整等问题，原因是去噪过程中空间区域与文本 token 之间的注意力信号发生纠缠泄漏。

Method: 提出 BindEdit，在单次扩散轨迹中施加注意力级约束：(1) 联合正则化交叉注意力和自注意力，将每个目标 token 组绑定到对应空间区域并保持实例级分离，抑制 Edit-Token Leakage；(2) 交叉注意力重平衡机制放大目标 token 影响、减弱可编辑区域内的残留源语义，抑制 Source Dominance Leakage；(3) 区域保真项确保每个目标概念在整个编辑掩码内连贯表达。

Result: 在单目标和多目标编辑场景中，BindEdit 均一致优于现有方法，在提出的多目标基准上表现稳健。

Conclusion: BindEdit 通过注意力级约束有效解决了多目标图像编辑中的注意力泄漏问题，在单次扩散轨迹中实现了精确、鲁棒的编辑效果。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多目标图像编辑方法 BindEdit，属于图像编辑方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing (图像与视频编辑)" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses attention leakage in multi-object editing, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: attention leakage, multi-object editing, diffusion trajectory, cross-attention regularization, single-trajectory editing

Abstract: Real image editing enables precise manipulation of visual content, yet existing methods often fail in complex multi-object scenarios, causing semantic blending, object duplication, or incomplete edits. We attribute these failures to attention leakage, where signals across spatial regions and text tokens become entangled during the denoising process. Specifically, we identify two distinct forms of leakage: Edit-Token Leakage, where ambiguous token-region alignment leads to object blending, and Source Dominance Leakage, where tokens of unchanged source objects overwhelm the attention intended for target entities. To resolve these leakages, we propose \textbf{BindEdit}, which enforces attention-level constraints within a single diffusion trajectory. To suppress Edit-Token Leakage, BindEdit jointly regularizes cross- and self-attention so that each target token group is bound to its corresponding spatial region while maintaining instance-level separation. To suppress Source Dominance Leakage, a cross-attention re-balancing mechanism amplifies target token influence and attenuates residual source semantics within editable regions. Moreover, a region fidelity term ensures that each target concept is expressed coherently across the entire editing mask. Additionally, we propose a comprehensive multi-object benchmark encompassing diverse object counts and categories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BindEdit consistently outperforms existing methods within a single diffusion trajectory, maintaining robust performance across both single- and multi-object editing scenarios.

</details>


### [35] [Taming I2V models for Image HOI Editing: A Cognitive Benchmark and Agentic Self-Correcting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19073)
*Jiayi Gao, Qingchao Chen, Yuxin Peng, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (80.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HOI-Edit 基准和 SCPE 自纠正框架，利用 I2V 模型的时序生成能力解决复杂的人物-物体交互（HOI）图像编辑问题，通过迭代式提示优化实现动态交互关系的准确编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法在静态属性编辑上表现良好，但在复杂的人物-物体交互（HOI）编辑上失败。现有基准将 HOI 与静态属性混为一谈，缺乏能同时评估动态交互有效性和人物-物体对保持性的指标。

Method: 1) 构建 HOI-Edit 基准，包含三个递进认知层级，并设计 HOI-Eval 自动评估指标（利用 VLM 对含有人物-物体对的图像进行思维链问答来评估实例级交互）。2) 提出 SCPE（Self-Correcting Process Editing）框架，利用 I2V 模型的时序生成能力进行动态编辑，通过迭代式提示优化约束生成过程，从生成视频中提取帧作为最终编辑结果。

Result: 在 HOI-Edit 基准上，SCPE 在交互编辑方面达到了与 SOTA 编辑模型（如 Nano Banana）竞争的性能。

Conclusion: I2V 模型因其时序生成能力天然适合动态 HOI 编辑，且能提供"失败过程回放"用于错误诊断；SCPE 自纠正框架通过迭代提示优化有效提升了 HOI 编辑质量。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于图像中人物-物体交互（HOI）编辑任务，属于图像编辑的细分方向。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 涵盖图像编辑、可控编辑等，与该论文核心任务高度匹配，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Novel HOI editing benchmark and self-correcting framework, but focuses on image/video editing, not core priority subtopics like continuous models or world models. Signals: HOI editing benchmark, I2V model for dynamic editing, agentic self-correcting framework, cognitive evaluation levels, VLM-based automated metric

Abstract: Current image editing methods excel at static attributes but fail at complex Human-Object Interactions (HOI), a critical challenge unaddressed by existing benchmarks that conflate HOI with static attributes, relying on global metrics incapable of simultaneously assessing dynamic interaction validity and entangled human-object pair preservation. Thus, we first introduce HOI-Edit, a comprehensive benchmark with three progressive cognitive levels, which features an automated metric HOI-Eval that reliably evaluates instance-level interaction by letting VLM Q&A after thinking with images containing grounded Human-Object pairs. Considering the task's essence of remodeling dynamic relationships, we benchmark Image-to-Video (I2V) models, finding them inherently suited for dynamic editing due to their temporal generation capabilities. Crucially, beyond superior performance, this capability provides a "replay of the failure process," offering unique diagnosability into why errors occur. We thus propose SCPE (Self-Correcting Process Editing), a novel, agentic self-correcting framework that constrains the generation of I2V models through iteratively refined prompts, enabling the generated videos to more accurately present the target HOI. Extracted frames from these videos are the final editing results. On HOI-Edit, SCPE achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art (SOTA) editing models like Nano Banana on interaction. Code is available at .

</details>


### [36] [ProductConsistency: Improving Product Identity Preservation in Instruction-Based Image Editing via SFT and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19103)
*Mukund Khanna, Raj Singh Yadav, Kunal Singh*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (69.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProductConsistency 数据集和方法，通过监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）提升指令式图像编辑中产品身份（品牌、文字、视觉特征）的保持能力。构建了87k SFT样本、869张产品图像的RL数据集及评测基准，并提出循环一致性奖励（Cyclic Consistency reward）用于RL训练。在Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev上微调，OCR字符错误率降低5倍，感知质量和MLLM评估均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑模型在处理产品图像时，难以保持产品特征、品牌标识和文字元素等细粒度对象身份。缺乏专门针对产品图像编辑的数据集和评测基准，导致该能力被当作隐式能力而非显式优化目标。

Method: 1) 构建 ProductConsistency 数据集：87k SFT样本用于产品编辑训练，869张独特产品图像的RL数据集，以及标准化评测基准。2) 提出 Cyclic Consistency reward：利用原始产品描述与编辑后图像生成描述之间的字幕相似度，在RL训练中强制保持产品身份语义。3) 在 Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 和 Flux.1-Kontext-dev 两个模型上进行SFT+RL微调。

Result: 微调后的模型在OCR指标（字符错误率降低5倍）、感知质量指标和MLLM评估上均优于基线模型，产品一致性、文字渲染和整体视觉质量显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过专门的数据集和循环一致性奖励的RL训练，可以有效提升指令式图像编辑模型在产品图像场景下的身份保持能力，为产品级图像编辑应用提供了可行方案。

Signals: Instruction-based image editing, Product identity preservation, SFT and RL for editing, Cyclic consistency reward, OCR improvement

Abstract: Recent advances in instruction-based image editing have enabled models to perform complex visual edits from natural language instructions. However, in product-centric scenarios where preserving product features, branding, and textual elements are critical, current open and closed source models often struggle to maintain this fine-grained object identity. This issue is further compounded by the lack of datasets for instruction-based product image editing with text fidelity constraints, leaving it largely treated as an implicit capability of instruction-based image editing models. In this work, we introduce the ProductConsistency dataset which is designed to improve product-centric image editing. Our approach includes a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) dataset of 87k samples for product editing, a reinforcement learning (RL) dataset with 869 unique product images, and a new benchmark dataset, the ProductConsistency Benchmark, to allow rigorous and standardized evaluation of editing models. To guide RL training, we propose a Cyclic Consistency reward that enforces semantic preservation of product identity by using caption similarity between the original product description and captions generated from the edited image. We fine-tune both Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 and Flux.1-Kontext-dev using our dataset and demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models in OCR and Perceptual metrics, and MLLM-based evaluations as well, indicating stronger product consistency, text rendering, and overall visual quality; with the Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 model achieving a 5x reduction in the character error rate. The code and pipeline is available at

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Moebius 系统性地重构了扩散骨干网络，提出 Local-λ Mix Interaction (LλMI) 模块，包含 Local-λ 和 Interactive-λ 两个子模块，将空间上下文和全局语义先验压缩为固定大小的线性矩阵，在保留复杂潜空间交互的同时大幅减少参数。同时，采用自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，在潜空间内动态平衡多个梯度损失，实现高保真对齐。

Result: 在自然图像和人像修复基准上，Moebius (0.22B) 在生成质量上媲美甚至超越 FLUX.1-Fill-Dev (11.9B)，参数量仅为后者的 1.8%，推理速度提升 15 倍以上。

Conclusion: Moebius 证明了通过精心设计的紧凑架构与蒸馏策略的协同，轻量级专用模型可以在图像修复任务上达到工业级大模型的性能，为高效高保真图像修复设定了新的效率标准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出轻量级图像修复（inpainting）框架，属于图像编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 直接匹配。

Importance reason: Strong efficiency gains in image inpainting via extreme parameter reduction and distillation, but subtopic is outside user's priority list. Signals: 0.22B parameters vs 11.9B, 15x inference acceleration, Local-λ Mix Interaction block, adaptive multi-granularity distillation, diffusion backbone compression

Abstract: While 10B-level industrial foundation models have pushed the boundaries of image inpainting, their prohibitive computational costs severely hinder practical deployment. Constructing a highly optimized task-specific specialist offers a promising solution; however, extreme structural compression inevitably triggers a severe representation bottleneck. To conquer this, we propose Moebius, a highly efficient lightweight inpainting framework. We systematically reconstruct the diffusion backbone by introducing the Local-$\lambda$ Mix Interaction ($L\lambda MI$) block. Comprising Local-$\lambda$ and Interactive-$\lambda$ modules, it elegantly summarizes spatial contexts and global semantic priors into fixed-size linear matrices, preserving complex latent interactions while drastically shedding parameters. Furthermore, to unlock the full representational capacity of this highly compact architecture, we synergistically pair it with an adaptive multi-granularity distillation strategy. Operating strictly within the latent space to avoid expensive pixel-space decoding, this strategy dynamically balances multiple gradient-based losses to achieve high-fidelity alignment. Extensive experiments across natural and portrait benchmarks demonstrate that this optimal synergy enables Moebius to rival or even surpass the generation quality of the 10B-level industrial generalist FLUX.1-Fill-Dev. Remarkably, Moebius achieves this using less than 2\% of the parameters (0.22B vs. 11.9B) while delivering a $>15\times$ acceleration in total inference time, setting a new efficiency standard for high-fidelity inpainting. Project page at .

Conclusion: NeuMesh++ 通过解耦的神经网格隐式场表示，实现了多功能且高效的3D场景体积编辑，为神经渲染在编辑领域的应用提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently neural implicit rendering techniques have evolved rapidly and demonstrated significant advantages in novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, existing neural rendering methods for editing purposes offer limited functionalities, e.g., rigid transformation and category-specific editing. In this paper, we present a novel mesh-based representation by encoding the neural radiance field with disentangled geometry, texture, and semantic codes on mesh vertices, which empowers a set of efficient and comprehensive editing functionalities, including mesh-guided geometry editing, designated texture editing with texture swapping, filling and painting operations, and semantic-guided editing. To this end, we develop several techniques including a novel local space parameterization to enhance rendering quality and training stability, a learnable modification color on vertex to improve the fidelity of texture editing, a spatial-aware optimization strategy to realize precise texture editing, and a semantic-aided region selection to ease the laborious annotation of implicit field editing. Extensive experiments and editing examples on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method on representation quality and editing ability. Project page:

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成高质量的AD特异性结构MRI数据面临挑战，因为神经退行性病变导致的解剖变化微妙、区域特异且渐进。现有生成模型难以捕捉这些细微变化，而高质量合成数据对数据增强、隐私保护和模型泛化至关重要。

Method: 基于Med-DDPM条件扩散模型，以ADNI数据集的解剖分割掩码为条件，将AD相关脑区结构信息注入生成过程，实现3D结构MRI的AD特异性合成。

Conclusion: 条件扩散模型能够生成解剖精确的AD特异性合成MRI，混合真实与合成数据可显著提升分割模型性能，为神经影像学研究中的数据增强和诊断准确性提升提供了有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文使用条件扩散模型（Med-DDPM）生成3D结构MRI图像，属于图像生成/扩散模型范畴。虽然应用领域是医学影像，但核心方法是条件扩散生成，与[文生图与图像扩散]这一CANONICAL subtopic在方法论上高度匹配（扩散模型、条件生成），因此复用该分类。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical imaging for Alzheimer's, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords. Signals: Medical imaging, Alzheimer's disease, Conditional diffusion, Anatomical masks, Med-DDPM

Abstract: Recent advances in generative machine learning models have significantly improved medical imaging, offering promising solutions for data augmentation, privacy preservation, and improved model generalization. However, synthesizing high-quality structural MRI data for Alzheimer's Disease (AD) remains challenging due to the subtle, region-specific, and progressive anatomical changes associated with neurodegeneration. In this paper, we extend the Med-DDPM conditional diffusion model -- originally designed for brain tumor synthesis -- to generate 3D structural MRIs specifically tailored to AD. We adopted Med-DDPM due to its established stability and structural fidelity compared to other generative models, which makes it particularly suitable for capturing the subtle anatomical changes characteristic of AD. Our approach conditions the diffusion process on anatomical segmentation masks derived from the ADNI dataset, incorporating key AD-relevant brain structures into the generation process. We systematically evaluate the quality and utility of the synthetic images by training segmentation models on real, synthetic, and hybrid (mixed) datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that segmentation models trained exclusively on synthetic data achieve comparable Dice scores (0.6532) to those trained on real data (0.6513), while exhibiting significantly enhanced recall. Notably, models trained on hybrid datasets (mixing real and synthetic images) outperform both real and synthetic-only baselines, achieving a Dice score of 0.7244. These findings underscore the successful use of conditional diffusion models for generating anatomically accurate, AD-specific synthetic MRIs, and highlight their potential for enhancing training data availability, improving diagnostic accuracy, and promoting research reproducibility in neuroimaging studies.

### [40] [UniTemp: Unlocking Video Generation in Any Temporal Order via Bidirectional Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18702)
*Lin Zhang, Sicheng Mo, Zefan Cai, Jinhong Lin, Zihao Lin, Jiuxiang Gu, Krishna Kumar Singh, Yuheng Li, Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniTemp 提出双向蒸馏框架，通过块级锚点潜变量解决因果 3D VAE 在反向生成时的上下文缺失问题，使单个自回归视频扩散模型支持任意时序方向的视频生成（前向、后向、中间插帧），并实现双向扩展、循环视频、场景过渡和视觉故事生成等多种工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型仅支持前向时序生成，而实际视频创作常需要灵活生成顺序（如基于未来帧向后扩展、基于过去和未来帧进行插帧生成）。现有方法受限于因果 3D VAE 的编码结构，无法直接支持反向生成。

Method: 提出 UniTemp 双向蒸馏框架：(1) 引入块级锚点潜变量（blockwise anchor latents），在反向生成时恢复因果 VAE 缺失的过去上下文；(2) 训练单个自回归学生模型，通过双向蒸馏从前向教师模型学习任意方向生成能力；(3) 推理时支持以任意过去和/或未来帧为条件进行生成。

Result: 实验表明 UniTemp 在短/长视频生成上与前向方法性能相当，同时支持双向视频扩展、中间插帧、循环视频生成、场景过渡和视觉故事生成等多种灵活工作流。

Conclusion: UniTemp 成功突破了自回归视频扩散模型仅支持前向生成的限制，通过双向蒸馏和锚点潜变量设计，实现了灵活可控的任意时序方向视频生成，拓展了视频生成的应用场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniTemp，一种支持任意时序方向（前向、后向、中间插帧）的视频扩散生成方法，属于视频生成范畴。虽然涉及自回归视频扩散，但核心是视频扩散模型在时序方向上的扩展，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances autoregressive video generation with bidirectional flexibility, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: autoregressive video generation, video diffusion, bidirectional generation, continuous visual token, flow matching

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have emerged as a promising approach for long video generation, achieving strong performance in streaming settings. However, existing methods are restricted to forward temporal generation, whereas practical video creation often requires flexible generation order, e.g., conditioning on future context to extend backward, or on both past and future context for inbetween generation. We bridge this gap by training an autoregressive model that supports generation in arbitrary temporal directions. A key technical challenge arises from the Causal 3D VAE widely used in video diffusion models, which encodes latents strictly conditioned on past context. While suited for forward generation, this causal structure causes inter-block discontinuities when generation proceeds backward. To address this, we introduce blockwise anchor latents, a set of auxiliary latents that restore the missing past context at block boundaries during backward generation. Built on this design, we propose UniTemp, a bidirectional distillation framework that trains a single autoregressive student model for any-direction video generation. At inference time, UniTemp conditions on arbitrary past and/or future frames, improving controllability for both bidirectional and inbetween generation. Experiments show that UniTemp maintains competitive performance on short and long video generation compared to forward-only methods, while enabling diverse workflows such as bidirectional video extension, inbetween generation, looping video generation, scene transition, and visual story generation. Project website:

### [41] [Data-Forcing Distillation: Restoring Diversity and Fidelity in Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18478)
*Siyi Chen, Shaowei Liu, Yixuan Jia, Zian Wang, Huan Ling, Qing Qu, Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出 Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD)，一种简单的后训练框架，通过教师分数差异（teacher score discrepancy）引导少步视频生成学生模型恢复多样性和保真度，仅需一行代码改动和100-300步微调即可解决DMD方法中的模式坍塌和过饱和问题，在Wan2.1和Cosmos-Predict2.5模型上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）方法在将多步视频扩散模型蒸馏为少步学生模型时，由于反向KL目标函数的固有缺陷，存在两个严重问题：样本多样性大幅下降和输出过饱和（偏离真实视频外观）。需要一种简单有效的方法来恢复多样性和保真度。

Method: 提出 Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD)，核心思想是利用教师分数差异（teacher score discrepancy）来引导学生模型朝向真实数据分布：一方面将学生拉向缺失的模式（缓解模式坍塌），另一方面推离真实数据中不存在的有问题的模式（避免过饱和）。该方法仅需一行代码改动，在DMD训练后额外进行100-300步微调。

Result: 在文本到视频、图像到视频和自回归视频生成任务上验证，DFD有效恢复了Wan2.1-1.3B和Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B模型的多样性和保真度，解决了过饱和伪影，显著改善了视频动态和外观，甚至超越了教师模型的表现。

Conclusion: DFD是一种简单有效的后训练框架，通过教师分数差异引导，能够在不增加推理成本的前提下，显著提升少步视频生成模型的多样性和视觉保真度，为扩散模型蒸馏提供了一种实用的改进方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD)，是一种针对视频扩散模型的蒸馏后训练框架，旨在将多步扩散模型蒸馏为少步学生模型，属于生成模型加速与高效化（蒸馏、少步采样）方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses diversity and fidelity collapse in few-step video generation distillation, a critical bottleneck for efficient video generation. Signals: video generation, efficient inference, diffusion distillation, mode collapse, over-saturation

Abstract: Recent progress has shown promise in distilling multi-step video diffusion models into efficient few-step students. Among them, Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) and its successor DMD2 achieved strong generation quality and fast convergence. However, due to the nature of the reverse Kullback--Leibler (KL) objective, these methods exhibit two persistent failure modes: a substantial drop in sample diversity, and visibly over-saturated outputs that deviate from real-video appearance. In this work, we propose Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD), a simple post-training framework that restores diversity and fidelity in DMD with only a single-line of code change. At its core is the teacher score discrepancy to guide the student toward the real-data distribution, pulling it to missing modes (mitigating mode collapse) and away from problematic modes absent in real data (avoiding over-saturation). We provide an in-depth theoretical analysis of our framework and validate our approach on text-to-video, image-to-video, and autoregressive video generation. With only 100--300 steps of finetuning, DFD effectively restores diversity and fidelity on both Wan2.1-1.3B and Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B model, resolving the over-saturation artifacts with significantly better video dynamics and appearance, and even outperforms the teacher model.

</details>


### [42] [SpectralDiT: Timestep-Conditioned Spectral Residual Correction for Flow-Matching DiTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18765)
*Jiayu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (87.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpectralDiT 在 Flow-Matching DiT 的 MLP 残差分支中引入时序条件谱残差校正模块，将残差更新分解为低频和高频分量并通过零初始化的可学习门控进行校正，在 CIFAR-10 和 ImageNet-100 上以极小的额外计算开销（0.6% FLOPs）显著提升了生成质量（FID 降低 8.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 在 flow-matching 框架下虽然性能优异，但其 MLP 残差分支对高低频信息的处理缺乏显式区分，导致高频细节生成不够精确。作者希望通过在频域上对残差进行分解和校正，以极小的额外代价提升生成质量。

Method: SpectralDiT 在每个 DiT block 的 MLP 残差分支后添加一个时序条件谱残差校正模块。该模块首先将 patch-token 网格上的残差通过 2D FFT 分解为低频和高频分量，然后使用时序嵌入（timestep embedding）作为条件，通过零初始化的可学习门控网络对两个分量分别进行加权校正，最后通过逆 FFT 将校正后的信号加回残差。零初始化保证模型初始状态与基线 DiT 一致，便于稳定训练。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 像素空间生成上，SpectralDiT 将 FID 从 20.78 降至 19.71（patch size=1），并缩小了径向傅里叶谱差距。在 ImageNet-100 潜空间扩散上，仅增加 0.6% 理论 FLOPs 和 1.36% 参数，在 CFG 2.0 下实现了 8.7% 的相对 FID 降低。所有结果均基于 5 个种子平均。消融实验和门控可视化揭示了稳定的逐块谱校正模式。

Conclusion: SpectralDiT 证明了在频域中对 DiT 残差进行显式分解和时序条件校正是一种轻量且有效的改进方式，能够以极小的额外开销提升 flow-matching DiT 的生成质量。该方法具有通用性，可推广到其他基于 DiT 的生成任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SpectralDiT，通过对 Flow-Matching DiT 的 MLP 残差分支添加时序条件谱残差校正模块，以极小的额外计算开销（0.6% FLOPs）提升生成质量。这属于生成模型效率优化（轻量级改进提升性能），因此归入 [CANONICAL] 生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）。

Importance reason: Proposes a lightweight spectral correction for flow-matching DiTs, directly improving generation efficiency and quality with minimal overhead. Signals: flow-matching DiT, spectral residual correction, efficient inference, 0.6% FLOPs, FID reduction

Abstract: We propose SpectralDiT, a lightweight modification to flow-matching Diffusion Transformers that adds timestep-conditioned spectral correction to the MLP residual branch. The module decomposes each residual update into low- and high-frequency components on the patch-token grid, then learns a zero-initialized additive gate so the model initially matches the baseline DiT. On CIFAR-10 pixel-space generation, SpectralDiT improves FID from 20.78 to 19.71 at patch size 1 and reduces the radial Fourier spectrum gap. Furthermore, we scale our method to latent diffusion on ImageNet-100. With 0.6% additional theoretical FLOPs and 1.36% additional parameters, SpectralDiT improves latent flow-matching, achieving an 8.7% relative FID reduction under classifier-free guidance (CFG 2.0). All reported results are averaged over five seeds. Ablations and gate visualizations on CIFAR-10 reveal stable block-specific spectral correction patterns.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在 Qwen-Image 后训练上，Spotlight 达到相同目标验证分数的速度比基线快 4 倍，总成本降低 1.4-6.4 倍，并在 DeepSeek-OCR 和 Geneval 数据集上（512×512 和 1280×1280 分辨率）实现了更优的图像质量。

Conclusion: Spotlight 证明了 spot GPU 可以高效用于 DiT 的 RL 后训练，通过巧妙的系统设计（种子探索解耦、弹性 SP、抢占感知调度）大幅降低了训练成本，为大规模扩散模型的后训练提供了实用的系统级解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 的强化学习后训练加速，通过利用 spot GPU、种子探索和弹性序列并行等技术大幅降低训练成本，属于生成模型训练/推理效率优化，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Novel system for efficient DiT RL post-training using spot GPUs, directly addressing model efficiency and acceleration priorities. Signals: DiT RL post-training, spot GPU utilization, seed exploration, elastic sequence parallelism, training acceleration

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) is prohibitively expensive, requiring thousands of high-end GPUs. Existing works explore two directions to reduce cost: seed exploration improves training convergence by selecting high-contrast samples, yet adds compute to the critical path; spot GPUs offer 69--77\% lower cost, yet sit idle during training because DiT rollouts finish nearly simultaneously, which prevents LLM-style pipelining of rollout with training. Spot preemptions further break Sequence Parallelism (SP) groups, fragmenting GPU topology. We present Spotlight, the first system that harvests spot GPUs for DiT RL post-training. Spotlight rests on two key insights we devise: (1)~we show that exploration can tolerate stale model weights because exploration that uses the model weights from the previous iteration preserves the relative ranking of random seeds, allowing exploration to run on idle spot GPUs during training. (2)~SP reconfiguration can reuse on-node state, reducing group recovery from minutes to sub-second launches. Built on these insights, Spotlight introduces three techniques: a bandit-based exploration planner that maximizes reward variance within the training time budget, elastic sequence parallelism that reconfigures SP groups on the fly via persistent schedulers and intra-node weight copying, and a preemption-aware pull-based request scheduler that balances load and commits in-flight state upon preemption. We implement Spotlight on the open-source RL platform ROLL and evaluate it on Qwen-Image post-training. Spotlight reaches the same target validation score $4\times$ faster than baselines, reducing total cost by $1.4$-$6.4\times$ while achieving superior image quality on DeepSeek-OCR and Geneval datasets with resolution $512\times512$ and $1280\times1280$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型的 ℓ2 回归损失与推理时的视觉/语义质量之间存在结构性失配，而基于人类偏好的 RL 虽然能缓解这一问题，但依赖昂贵且混杂了标注者主观偏好的数据。作者希望在不依赖人类偏好的情况下获得一个能直接反映数据分布质量的奖励信号。

Method: DRL 在预训练表征空间（如 DINOv2）中训练一个判别器来区分真实数据与基模型生成样本，将其 logit 作为奖励信号，结合 KL 正则化 RL（类似 DPO 的变体）对流匹配模型进行微调。预训练空间限制了判别器只关注感知上有意义的维度，而 logit 恰好估计了数据与模型之间的对数似然比，这是指向数据分布的最优奖励。

Result: 在 SiT、JiT、REPA、RAE 等多个流匹配骨干上，DRL 显著降低了无引导 FID（如 SiT 上从 9.38 降至 2.62）和语义空间 FD（DINOv3 上从 88.2 降至 19.3），并提升了人类偏好奖励。在后续偏好对齐中，DRL 提供了更好的 Pareto 前沿，在提升对齐的同时减少了过饱和、过亮等低层伪影。

Conclusion: DRL 证明无需人类偏好数据，仅通过判别器引导的 RL 就能有效纠正流匹配模型的结构性失配，显著提升生成质量，并可作为偏好对齐的更好起点。

Classification reason: 该论文提出用判别器引导的强化学习（DRL）来对齐流匹配生成模型，核心是奖励建模与偏好优化，属于生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel RL method to fix flow-matching structural mismatch using discriminator logits as reward, directly improving generation quality without human preferences. Signals: discriminator-guided RL, flow matching correction, no human preference needed, strong FID gains on SiT/JiT/REPA/RAE, aligns with continuous language/multimodal model priorities

Abstract: Score- and flow-matching models often rely on preference-based reinforcement learning for two purposes: aligning with subjective preferences and, surprisingly, recovering properties such as visual realism and coherent object structure that matching-based training is intended to learn from the data itself. We argue that this reflects a structural mismatch. Matching losses measure $\ell_2$ regression error on the velocity or score field under training-time marginals, a proxy poorly aligned with the visual and semantic properties that determine sample quality at inference. Given a reward aligned with these properties, RL sidesteps the mismatch by evaluating the model on its own samples and following the reward landscape directly. The challenge is to obtain such a reward without relying on human preferences, which are expensive and conflate data realism with annotator inclinations. We propose Discriminator-Guided RL (DRL). DRL trains a discriminator to separate data from base-model samples in a pretrained representation space and uses its logit as the reward in KL-regularized RL. The pretrained space restricts the discriminator to perceptually meaningful directions, and the logit estimates the log-likelihood ratio between data and model, which is the optimal reward for targeting the data distribution. Across SiT, JiT, REPA, and RAE, DRL reduces guidance-free FID (e.g., $9.38 \to 2.62$ on SiT) and semantic-space FD (e.g., $88.2 \to 19.3$ on DINOv3 for SiT), with consistent gains across all backbones, and improves human-preference rewards without training on them. It also yields a better Pareto frontier between preference reward and image fidelity under subsequent preference-based post-training, increasing alignment while reducing low-level artifacts such as oversaturation and excessive brightness.

</details>

Abstract: Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) integrate multimodal understanding, future-state reasoning, and executable robot actions. Yet their safety alignment for human-injury prevention remains underexplored, primarily because real-world data of robots harming humans or creating hazardous household situations cannot be safely or ethically collected. To address this challenge, we propose a safety-critical data construction pipeline for human-injury prevention in from real DROID observations, our construction pipeline proceeds through scene understanding, hazard-aware image editing, temporal prompt generation, and single-pass rollout synthesis. The temporal prompts specify the expected scene evolution, while Wan2.7 synthesizes realistic robotic rollouts from the edited hazardous states in a single pass. Using this pipeline, we construct ROBOSHACKLES, a 10,000-clip robotic video dataset derived from real DROID observations, spanning two direct-harm and four indirect-harm categories. To ensure dataset quality, we assess task completion and visual quality with automatic metrics, and evaluate six representative EFMs under a refusal-based safety criterion. Results show that all evaluated models produce unsafe actions in the tested safety-critical scenarios, yielding a 100% unsafe action generation rate. ROBOSHACKLES serves as a scalable benchmark and training resource for refusal learning and hazard anticipation before robot action dataset is publicly available at .

Abstract: The rapid advancement of text-to-image diffusion models has enabled the creation of highly photorealistic synthetic images that closely resemble real photographs, making it increasingly difficult to distinguish authentic content from AI-generated fabrications. This poses challenges for cybersecurity, digital forensics, and disaster response, where fake imagery of floods, fires, or earthquakes can spread misinformation or disrupt emergency operations. To address this, we introduce Forged Calamity, a benchmark dataset for synthetic disaster detection containing 30,000 images, including 6,000 real and 24,000 synthetic samples generated by four diffusion models. Comprehensive experiments across fine-tuned and zero-shot settings reveal consistent weaknesses in current forensic approaches. Fine-tuned detectors perform well in-distribution but lose up to 50\% accuracy on unseen generators or disaster types, showing overfitting to model-specific artifacts. Zero-shot generalized detectors also struggle to maintain stable accuracy, with only limited resilience in a few representation-robust models. These findings highlight persistent generalization gaps and the urgent need for domain- and model-agnostic detection methods to ensure visual authenticity in the diffusion era.

Abstract: In the realm of computer vision, indoor image recognition presents challenges due to the intricate interplay of lighting conditions, occlusions, and diverse object arrangements within confined spaces. To address the lacks of training indoor images, we introduce a novel approach leveraging Stable Diffusion (SD) for the generation of synthetic images, which serve as a powerful data augmentation tool. The utilization of SD offers a principled framework for synthesizing diverse and realistic indoor scenes, thereby enriching the training data pool for robust indoor image recognition models. Experimental findings on the MIT Indoor Scene dataset reveal the potential of our proposed approach in enhancing the training of deep models when authentic data is limited. Furthermore, to prevent the misuse of SD synthetic images, we introduce a counter measure based on DIffusion Reconstruction Error (DIRE). The powerful DIRE presentation enables training robust classifiers only using lightweight deep models. Experiments show that our approach can perfectly recognize SD generated images with the accuracy of 100% using MobilenetV3.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的VLM评测协议用于单图3D网格质量评估，包含固定渲染管线、双VLM法官族和位置偏差校正。实验表明廉价代理指标（几何有效性、CLIP相似度）无法替代该协议，几何有效性仅在视觉缺陷明显时有效，而CLIP相似度基本随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图到3D生成模型发展迅速，但缺乏公认的、无需人工的网格质量评估方法。现有研究常使用廉价代理指标（如渲染空间CLIP相似度、网格几何有效性统计），但这些指标与人类感知质量的相关性尚未被系统验证。

Method: 提出VLM法官评测协议：固定24视角无头渲染管线、两个独立VLM法官族（如GPT-4o等）、强制位置偏差校正（两种呈现顺序，仅保留一致判决）。以该协议为基准，评估几何有效性和渲染CLIP相似度等廉价代理指标的表现，并训练Bradley-Terry模型学习特征权重。

Result: 两个VLM法官族之间一致性较高（Cohen's kappa=0.66）。几何有效性仅在视觉缺陷明显时高于随机水平，在模糊对比中为随机；渲染CLIP相似度整体为随机水平。Bradley-Terry模型退化为仅依赖单一流形统计量，与纯几何指标表现相同。

Conclusion: VLM法官协议在测试条件下（Google Scanned Objects上的两个前馈生成器，面片退化机制）是可靠、可复现的评估方法。廉价代理指标不能替代该协议，不建议将其作为优化目标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于VLM的3D网格质量评测协议，并评估了现有廉价代理指标（CLIP相似度、几何有效性）的可靠性，属于生成模型评测方法。这与[multimodal_generation]方向下的[generation_benchmark]（生成模型评测与数据集）高度匹配，该规范子主题涵盖评测基准、自动评测指标等。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation protocol for 3D mesh quality, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: VLM judge protocol, 3D mesh quality, cheap proxy failure, position bias correction, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Single-image-to-3D generators are improving quickly, but there is no agreed, human-free way to tell whether one generated mesh is better than another. Practitioners commonly rely on cheap automatic proxies (render-space CLIP similarity and mesh geometry-validity statistics), yet how well these track perceived quality is unestablished. We make two contributions. First, we propose and validate a reproducible VLM-judge evaluation protocol: a fixed 24-view headless render rig, two independent vision-language judge families, and a mandatory position-bias correction that queries both presentation orders and keeps only order-consistent verdicts. The two judge families agree substantially with each other (Cohen's kappa = 0.66), well above the chance-agreement floor. Second, using this protocol as the reference, we show the cheap proxies do not substitute for it. Geometry validity is only a weak signal on average (because, as we show, it is bimodal) and stays below our pre-registered target, while render-CLIP is at chance. A learned Bradley-Terry head collapses onto a single manifoldness statistic (giving render-CLIP a negative weight) and matches geometry-only exactly, so learning the feature weights buys nothing. The proxy is also bimodal: it is significantly above chance on contrasts with visible geometric defects but at chance on ambiguous contrasts, consistent with geometry validity tracking the judge only when the defect is visually salient. We therefore recommend the VLM-judge protocol as a reliable, reproducible evaluator under the conditions tested (two feed-forward generators on Google Scanned Objects, with a face-drop degradation regime) and advise against geometry/CLIP proxies as optimization targets.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建了包含113个主题和7种演示场景的语料库，设计了8,133个去重且锚定源文档的探针，并为每个探针分配受众特定的效用权重。提出四个互补指标：Audience Coverage（受众必要信息覆盖率）、Domain-wise Coverage（信息类型覆盖）、Efficiency（单位注意力成本的效用）、Correctness（幻灯片声明是否被源文档支持）。

Result: 在 DeepPresenter、SlideTailor 和 NotebookLM 上的实验表明，当前系统能恢复大部分但非全部的受众必要信息。在 τ_A=0.7 时，DeepPresenter 的最佳 Audience Coverage 为 0.714，SlideTailor 为 0.594，NotebookLM 消融版本达到 0.853，但存在明显的源文档锚定差异。

Conclusion: 视觉质量和广泛的主题覆盖不应被视为没有源文档锚定评估的证据支持。X+Slides 揭示了现有幻灯片生成系统在受众条件生成方面的不足，为未来研究提供了更全面的评估框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向受众条件的幻灯片生成评测基准 X+Slides，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on slide generation benchmarking, not on core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: Slide generation benchmark, audience-conditioned evaluation, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Automatically generating slide decks from source documents is an important application of large language models (LLMs). Existing benchmarks primarily assess slide completeness and technical depth, while overlooking the target audience as a critical real-world factor. For instance, specialists demand rigorous proofs, whereas decision-makers prioritize actionable conclusions. To bridge this gap, we introduce X+Slides, a benchmark specifically designed for audience-conditioned slide generation. Built on a diverse corpus spanning 113 topics and seven presentation scenes, X+Slides employs a dynamic evaluation framework constructed from 8,133 deduplicated, source-grounded probes. By assigning audience-specific utility weights to the same source-grounded probes, X+Slides reports four complementary metrics: Audience Coverage measures how much audience-essential information is conveyed, Domain-wise Coverage shows which information types are covered, Efficiency measures delivered utility per unit of attention cost, and Correctness verifies whether slide claims are supported by the source. Experiments on DeepPresenter, SlideTailor, and NotebookLM show that current systems can recover a substantial but still incomplete part of audience-essential information: at $\tau_A=0.7$, DeepPresenter reaches a best Audience Coverage of 0.714, SlideTailor reaches 0.594, and the NotebookLM ablation reaches 0.853 while showing clear grounding differences. These results indicate that visual quality and broad topic coverage should not be treated as evidence support without source-grounded evaluation.

</details>

Importance reason: Provides a refined benchmark for evaluating physical understanding in video generation, but is a meta-evaluation rather than a new generative model or method. Abstract: Video generative models ( VGMs) have become a new frontier that can be used not just for video generation but for a multitude of downstream tasks, including world modeling. To advance these tasks, a good video model must understand the physical reality of the world. Evaluating this understanding is an emerging field and has led to the Physics-IQ benchmark, which quantifies this explicitly by comparing model-generated videos to real-world videos of physical experiments. In this work, we present a systematic audit of the Physics-IQ benchmark, expose shortcomings and propose three solutions that sharpen how we can measure physical understanding of VGMs. Specifically, we improve prompt and ground-truth quality to reduce the influence of confounding factors and further introduce a sample-level scoring system that weights each sample and metric equally. Our resulting benchmark, Physics-IQ Verified, refines 57.6\% of all samples and improves over 34.8\% of prompts. In a comparison study using six image-to-video generative models, we observe moderate but meaningful ranking changes (Kendall's $\tau = 0.46$). We hope Physics-IQ Verified advances the community by providing a more reliable signal toward physically accurate VGMs. The code for the benchmark can be accessed at

Abstract: Text-rich images often contain privacy-sensitive, transactional, or decision-relevant information. As recent multimodal image generation models become increasingly capable of synthesizing realistic textual content and structured visual designs, detecting AI-generated text-rich images has become an important challenge for digital trust and content authenticity. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on object-centric images and provide limited coverage of scenarios where textual semantics and layout organization are central. In this paper, we introduce a multi-domain benchmark for detecting text-rich images generated by OpenAI's GPT Image 2. The benchmark contains 8,602 images across six representative categories: commercial posters, infographics, academic posters, receipts, tables, and UI screenshots. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five representative AI-generated image detectors in a zero-shot setting and analyze their overall, category-wise, and post-processing robustness. Our results show that detector performance is highly domain-dependent: methods that perform well in some categories often fail on others, and even the strongest conventional detector exhibits severe sensitivity to JPEG compression. We further conduct an exploratory evaluation with a multimodal vision-language model, revealing both its promise and its limitations on structured formats. These findings highlight the need for text- and layout-aware detection methods for modern AI-generated images. Our dataset is released at XXX.

*Subhankar Ghosh, Jason Li, Paarth Neekhara, Shehzeen Hussain, Ryan Langman, Xuesong Yang, Roy Fejgin*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.SD, cs.AI

TL;DR: MagpieTTS-LF 提出了一种无需重新训练的推理时方法，通过软注意力先验、状态推理和历史感知文本编码，使 MagpieTTS 能够生成长文本连贯语音，显著改善韵律漂移、说话人一致性和句边界伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经 TTS 系统在短句上表现优异，但在长文本语音生成中存在韵律漂移、说话人不一致和句边界伪影等问题。现有方法要么压缩序列、增加上下文长度，要么简单拼接独立合成的片段，效果有限。本文旨在不重新训练模型的前提下，实现高质量的长文本语音生成。

Method: 提出 MagpieTTS-LF，包含三项关键创新：(1) 软注意力先验（soft attention priors），引导单调对齐同时保留过去和未来上下文；(2) 状态推理算法（stateful inference），跨句子块维护上下文，确保韵律连续性；(3) 历史感知文本编码（history-aware text encoding），利用过去文本进行语篇级韵律规划。该方法完全在推理阶段实现，无需模型重训练。

Result: 在长文本上的实验表明，与多种基线方法相比，MagpieTTS-LF 在长距离可懂度、韵律连贯性、说话人一致性和边界自然度方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: MagpieTTS-LF 通过推理时策略有效解决了长文本语音生成中的关键问题，无需额外训练即可提升长文本 TTS 质量，为长文本语音合成提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长文本语音生成（Long-Form TTS），属于音频生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS long-form generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: Inference-time method, no retraining needed, soft attention priors, stateful inference, history-aware encoding

Abstract: Neural Text-to-Speech (TTS) systems achieve remarkable quality on short utterances but long-form speech generation shows prosodic drift, speaker inconsistencies and sentence boundary artifacts. Existing approaches either compress sequences, increase context length or naively concatenate independently synthesized chunks. We present an inference-time approach called MagpieTTS-LF that enables MagpieTTS to produce coherent long-form speech without model retraining. Our method introduces three key innovations: (1) soft attention priors to guide monotonic alignment while preserving past and future context; (2) a stateful inference algorithm that maintains context across sentence chunks, ensuring prosodic continuity; (3) history-aware text encoding that uses past text for discourse-level prosodic planning. Experiments on long texts show significant improvements in long-range intelligibility, prosodic coherence, speaker consistency, and boundary naturalness compared to other baselines.

</details>


### [53] [Reference-Driven Multi-Speaker Audio Scene Generation from In-the-Wild Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19325)
*Michael Finkelson, Daniel Segal, Eitan Richardson, Shahar Armon, Nani Goldring, Poriya Panet, Nir Zabari, Benjamin Brazowski, Or Patashnik, Yoav HaCohen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScenA，一种基于参考语音驱动的多说话人音频场景生成方法。它利用预训练的文本到音频 flow-matching 基础模型，通过将参考语音潜变量拼接进 token 序列并加入身份感知位置编码，实现多说话人控制。针对训练中出现的"参考捷径"问题（模型通过声学相似性绕过文本提示），提出高噪声偏置的时间步分布来强制模型依赖文本提示进行说话人分配。在 CoVoMix2-Dialogue 基准上，ScenA 在说话人绑定指标上超越现有系统，同时生成包含重叠语音、情感发声和环境音的丰富对话音频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多说话人对话系统依赖结构化监督（每轮标签、多流转录或可学习说话人嵌入），且仅在纯语音管道中生成干净的语音序列，缺乏真实对话中的环境纹理（背景噪声、房间声学、重叠对话、自发副语言事件）。作者希望利用大规模野外数据预训练的通用音频基础模型，在保持多说话人控制能力的同时，生成更自然、丰富的对话音频场景。

Method: ScenA 基于预训练的文本到音频 flow-matching 基础模型。将多个参考语音通过模型的编码器得到潜变量，拼接进模型的 token 序列中，并引入轻量级的身份感知位置编码来区分不同说话人。训练时发现"参考捷径"问题：模型通过声学相似性匹配参考语音与带噪目标，绕过文本提示。为此提出高噪声偏置的时间步分布（high-noise-biased timestep distribution），迫使模型依赖文本提示进行说话人分配。

Result: 在 CoVoMix2-Dialogue 基准上，ScenA 在说话人绑定指标上超越现有多说话人系统，同时能够生成包含重叠语音、情感发声和环境音的丰富对话音频，展示了使用通用音频模型配合自由形式场景描述的优势。

Conclusion: ScenA 证明了利用通用音频基础模型（而非纯语音管道）进行多说话人音频场景生成的可行性。通过参考语音驱动和自由形式文本提示，可以生成更自然、更具环境纹理的对话音频，为多说话人音频生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多说话人音频场景生成方法 ScenA，使用文本到音频 flow-matching 基础模型，以多参考语音和自然语言提示为条件生成包含背景噪声、重叠对话等自然音频场景。这属于音频生成与多模态条件生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 的 "音频与多模态联合生成" 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: While technically solid, the paper's focus on multi-speaker audio scene generation is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: flow-matching foundation model, reference shortcut problem, multi-speaker audio scene generation, in-the-wild priors, high-noise-biased timestep distribution

Abstract: Existing multi-speaker dialogue systems bind speakers to utterances through structured supervision: per-turn tags, multi-stream transcriptions, or learnable speaker embeddings. These systems operate within speech-only pipelines that produce clean vocal sequences without the ambient texture of real conversations. We take a different approach. Our method, ScenA, conditions a text-to-audio flow-matching foundation model, pretrained on large-scale in-the-wild data, directly on multiple reference voices and a free-form natural language prompt that describes an entire multi-speaker audio scene. Leveraging such a foundational model allows us to inherit its capacity for natural, non-studio audio: background noise, room acoustics, overlapping dialogue, and spontaneous paralinguistic events, while adding multi-speaker control without any per-turn structure. Concretely, reference latents are concatenated into the model's token sequence and distinguished by lightweight identity-aware positional encodings. However, we identify a critical obstacle to this approach: the \textit{Reference Shortcut}. During training under standard noise schedules, the model can identify the matching reference by acoustic similarity to the noisy target, bypassing the text prompt entirely. We address this with a high-noise-biased timestep distribution that forces the model to rely on the text prompt for speaker assignment. We evaluate ScenA on the CoVoMix2-Dialogue benchmark, showing that it outperforms existing multi-speaker systems on speaker-binding metrics while generating rich conversational audio with overlapping speech, emotional vocalizations, and ambient sound. Our results demonstrate the advantage of using a general-purpose audio model conditioned on a free-form scene description, rather than passing structured dialog scripts through a speech-only pipeline.

</details>


### [54] [Reliable Neural-Codec Text-to-Speech by ASR Self-Verification and Distillation: Near-Zero Catastrophic Failures Across Models and Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18323)
*Ali Asaria, Tony Salomone, Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 值得关注 (74.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于ASR自验证与蒸馏的方法，有效消除开放自回归神经编解码TTS模型中的灾难性失败（静音、提前终止、重复/幻觉输出）。通过ASR往返的best-of-N自验证可将失败率降至接近零，并将该行为蒸馏回模型，在单次解码中恢复大部分鲁棒性，且无额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放自回归神经编解码TTS模型在典型输入上表现优异，但在相当比例的输入上会出现随机灾难性失败（静音、提前终止、重复或幻觉内容），严重影响实际部署的可靠性。现有方法缺乏针对此类失败的系统性消除方案。

Method: 提出一种格式鲁棒的ASR往返度量（ASR round-trip）来检测灾难性失败；使用best-of-N ASR自验证选择最佳输出；将自验证行为通过监督蒸馏（supervised distillation）回传至模型，使单次解码也能获得接近自验证的鲁棒性。对比了DPO/IPO等偏好优化方法。

Result: 在LibriSpeech上N=2时失败率降至零，在困难提示集上N=4时降至零。该结果在4种编解码TTS系统和3种神经编解码器（XCodec2, SNAC, Mimi）上复现。蒸馏在困难输入上消除了约52-58%的失败，且不损害已可靠的输出质量。DPO/IPO未优于简单监督蒸馏。

Conclusion: ASR自验证与蒸馏是一种低成本、高效的方法，可近乎消除神经编解码TTS的灾难性失败，且蒸馏可在零额外推理成本下实现。该方法对大多数模型和编解码器有效，但无法解决罕见词能力上限问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究神经编解码自回归文本到语音（TTS）生成，属于音频生成方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS reliability, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: TTS robustness, ASR self-verification, distillation, not in priority subtopics, no video/image generation

Abstract: Open autoregressive neural-codec text-to-speech (TTS) models sound excellent on typical inputs yet suffer stochastic catastrophic failures: on a meaningful fraction of utterances they emit silence, terminate early, or collapse into repetitive or hallucinated content. We show this failure mode is cheap to remove. Under a single format-robust metric (a catastrophic-failure rate via an ASR round-trip), best-of-N ASR self-verification drives failures to near-zero: no observed failures remain by N=2 on a standard corpus (LibriSpeech) and by N=4 on a hard prompt set. This is not an artifact of one model: the reduction replicates across four open codec-TTS systems and three neural codecs (XCodec2, SNAC, Mimi), reaching the near-zero floor by N=2 on three of the four. We then make the fix free at inference time by distilling the self-verified behaviour into the model, which recovers much of the robustness in single-shot decoding, closing ~52-58% of the failure mass on hard inputs at no test-time cost. The distillation gain concentrates where it is needed (hard inputs); on already-reliable prose there is no headroom and no detectable change. A controlled comparison adds a clean negative: offline direct preference optimization (DPO/IPO) does not beat plain supervised distillation, and an online iterative variant is promising but not statistically separable at our evaluation size. We report honestly the one model that resists (a larger Llasa where scale did not obviously help) and a rare-word capability ceiling that no self-distillation method overcomes

</details>

### [55] [Semantic Robustness Certification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18839)
*Peiyu Yang, Paul Montague, Feng Liu, Andrew C. Cullen, Amardeep Kaur, Christopher Leckie, Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对视觉语言模型（VLM）在语义级变换（如形状、大小、风格）下的鲁棒性认证框架。利用VLM的开放词汇能力，以文本提示作为语义代理构建参数化变换，通过闭式表征VLM决策边界，定量认证预测类别在语义变换下保持不变的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性认证框架主要研究几何或像素级变换，但实际应用中VLM常面临语义级分布偏移（如形状、大小、风格变化），缺乏针对语义级变换的鲁棒性认证方法。

Method: 利用VLM的开放词汇能力，用文本提示作为语义代理（semantic proxies）构建参数化语义变换；通过闭式表征VLM决策边界，定量认证预测类别在语义变换下保持不变的extent区间。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该框架能够在多种语义变化场景下认证VLM的鲁棒性，且无需为每种变化额外收集数据。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了VLM在语义级变换下的鲁棒性认证，为VLM的安全部署提供了实用工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在语义级变换下的鲁棒性认证（robustness certification），属于多模态模型的防御与安全性验证，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel certification framework for VLM semantic robustness, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: First VLM semantic robustness certification, closed-form decision boundary, no additional data needed, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are now widely used in downstream tasks. However, real-world applications often expose VLMs to distribution shifts induced by semantic variation (e.g., shape, size, and style). Robustness certification determines if a model's prediction changes when transformations are applied to its input. While most certification frameworks study geometric or pixel-level transformations over inputs, this work proposes a novel framework that enables certifying VLM robustness under semantic-level transformations. Leveraging the open-vocabulary capability of VLMs, we use text prompts as semantic proxies to construct transformations parameterized by an extent that controls the degree of semantic variation. By characterizing the VLM decision boundary in closed form, our framework quantitatively certifies extent intervals for which the predicted class remains unchanged under the semantic transformation. Our framework is the first to certify VLM robustness under semantic-level variations without requiring additional data for each variation, making it practical to apply. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data show that our framework enables certifying robustness under diverse semantic variations across scenarios.

</details>

### [56] [CaVe-VLM-CoT: An Interpretable Vision-Language Model Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18385)
*Sneha Rao, Shaina Raza, Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CaVe-VLM-CoT 是一个基于反思式 agentic-RAG 的五阶段闭环框架，通过提取器、检索器、求解器、引用注入器和验证器协同工作，在推理过程中检测无依据声明并触发定向重新检索，从而增强 VLM 的引用忠实性和跨模态证据基础。同时提出包含 23 个组件级指标和 CaVeScore 的综合评测体系，在 ScienceQA 和 MMMU 上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 仍存在幻觉问题，产生流畅但视觉上不忠实的输出。已有的 CoT 和 RAG 方法仅部分缓解该问题，因为它们既没有强制步骤级别的引用基础，也没有将验证失败反馈回检索进行修正。

Method: 提出 CaVe-VLM-CoT 框架，包含五个模块：(1) Extractor 从问题中提取关键概念；(2) Retriever 检索相关证据；(3) Solver 基于证据进行推理；(4) Citation Injector 将引用注入推理步骤；(5) Verifier 检测无依据声明，若发现问题则向 Extractor 发送结构化反馈触发定向重新检索。同时提出包含 CaVeScore 在内的 23 个组件级指标用于全面评估。

Result: 在 ScienceQA 上达到 87.1% 准确率和 56.6% CaVeScore，在 MMMU（30 个科目）上达到 55.2% 准确率和 35.7% CaVeScore，无需任何架构或提示修改。

Conclusion: CaVe-VLM-CoT 通过闭环反思式 RAG 机制有效提升了 VLM 的推理忠实性和证据基础，验证了将验证反馈融入检索过程对于缓解幻觉的有效性。提出的多维度评测体系为未来 VLM 可靠性研究提供了更全面的评估工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CaVe-VLM-CoT 框架，通过反思式 agentic-RAG 和引用验证来缓解 VLM 幻觉问题，属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性提升方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM hallucination and interpretability, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: VLM hallucination, agentic-RAG, citation grounding, CaVeScore, ScienceQA

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain prone to hallucinations, producing fluent but visually unfaithful outputs. Existing chain-of-thought and retrieval-augmented methods only partially address this, as they neither enforce step-level citation grounding nor route verification failures back to retrieval for correction. We present CaVe-VLM-CoT, a modular reflection-based agentic-RAG framework that enforces evidence-grounded reasoning through a five-stage closed-loop pipeline: Extractor, Retriever, Solver, Citation Injector, and Verifier, in which detected ungrounded claims trigger structured feedback to the Extractor for targeted re-retrieval. Since no existing framework jointly measures retrieval quality, step-wise citation faithfulness, and cross-modal grounding, we propose a suite of 23 component-wise metrics across all stages, anchored by CaVeScore, a composite metric weighting accuracy, citation precision and recall, attribution, and evidence grounding. Without any architectural or prompt modifications, CaVe-VLM-CoT achieves 87.1\% accuracy and 56.6\% CaVeScore on ScienceQA , and 55.2\% accuracy and 35.7\% CaVeScore on MMMU (30 subjects).

</details>


### [57] [Hallucination Detection and Correction in Medical VLMs via Counter-Evidence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18609)
*Nan Zhou, Ke Zou, Meng Liu, Linchao He, Jiaqi Zhu, Yi Zhang, Hu Chen, Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (45.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出CoEV（Counter-Evidence Verification），一种无需训练的即插即用框架，通过文本断言与视觉证据之间的双向验证来检测和纠正医学VLM中的幻觉，在四个医学数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法主要关注生成文本与参考数据之间的事实一致性，但很少验证模型对图像的注意力是否真正反映了支持生成文本的视觉证据。医学诊断中VLM的可靠性至关重要，需要更可靠的幻觉检测与纠正方法。

Method: 提出CoEV框架，进行文本断言与视觉证据之间的双向验证，判断每个陈述是否被其对应的证据区域支持，并将每个陈述映射到四象限诊断图（结合文本事实性和视觉基础性），实现幻觉检测与后处理纠正。

Result: 在四个医学数据集上，CoEV在幻觉检测中PR-AUC和ROC-AUC平均提升3.0%和3.9%，特定VQA场景提升达18.5%；幻觉纠正中Micro-F1提升达12.5%，医学报告生成的幻觉率降低超11.9%，同时提升医学VQA准确率。

Conclusion: CoEV通过基于证据的事实一致性验证，实现了可靠的幻觉检测与纠正，为临床诊断提供可信赖的、基于证据的线索。

Classification reason: 该论文研究医学VLM中的幻觉检测与纠正，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM hallucination, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Medical VLM, Hallucination detection, Training-free, Counter-evidence verification, Multimodal understanding

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) reliability in medical diagnosis is challenged by trust-undermining hallucinations. Existing hallucination detection approaches mainly focus on identifying factual inconsistencies between generated text and reference data. While some studies analyze where models attend in images, they seldom verify whether such attention truly reflects the visual evidence supporting the generated text. To address this gap, we propose Co}unter-Evidence Verification (CoEV), a training-free plug-and-play framework that detects and corrects hallucinations through evidence-based factual consistency verification. CoEV performs bidirectional verification between textual assertions and visual evidence, testing whether each statement is supported by its corresponding evidence region, and assigns each statement into a four-quadrant diagnostic map capturing combinations of text factuality and visual grounding. CoEV detects hallucinated content and serves as a post hoc refinement tool, correcting hallucinations without retraining. Extensive experiments on four medical datasets show that CoEV combats hallucinations in hallucination detection, CoEV consistently outperforms existing methods, improving average PR-AUC and ROC-AUC by 3.0% and 3.9% absolute points respectively, with notable gains of up to 18.5% in specific VQA scenarios. For hallucination correction, it improves Micro-F1 by up to 12.5%, reduces hallucination rates by over 11.9% on medical report generation, and also boosts medical VQA accuracy. These results show that CoEV enables reliable detection and correction of hallucinations, providing clinicians with dependable, evidence-based cues for diagnosis. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

### [58] [Steerable Cultural Preference Optimization of Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18606)
*Minsik Oh, Advit Deepak, Sophie Wu, Douwe Kiela, Ekaterina Shutova*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCPO（Steerable Cultural Preference Optimization），一种可引导的文化偏好优化算法，用于训练奖励模型以平衡地表示不同文化子社区的偏好，避免对任何群体的过度偏见，在 PRISM 和 GlobalOpinionQA 数据集上提升了少数群体奖励模型性能达 7 个点，训练数据效率提升最高 280%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 对齐研究主要关注预测来自特定地区标注者的统一响应偏好，缺乏对全球不同文化子社区多样化偏好的建模能力，导致模型可能对某些文化群体存在偏见。本文旨在开发能够准确表示子社区偏好且不表现出过度偏见的对齐模型。

Method: 提出 SCPO 算法，通过加权方式在训练奖励模型时平衡不同文化子社区的偏好数据，使模型能够学习到多样化的文化偏好，同时避免对任何群体的过度偏向。该方法支持在推理时通过调整权重引导模型偏向特定文化偏好。

Result: 在 PRISM 和 GlobalOpinionQA 两个数据集上，跨越 7 个国家，SCPO 使少数群体奖励模型性能提升最高达 7 个点；训练数据效率比全数据微调奖励模型高出最多 280%；通过分别评估子社区偏好，验证了该方法有效缓解了过度偏见。

Conclusion: SCPO 是一种有效的文化偏好对齐方法，能够在奖励模型训练中平衡多样化的文化偏好，提升少数群体表现并减少偏见，为构建更具全球包容性的 LLM 对齐提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究奖励模型的文化偏好对齐与优化，属于多模态/语言模型对齐（RLHF/偏好学习）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on cultural preference alignment for reward models, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Cultural preference optimization, Reward model training, Bias mitigation, Data efficiency, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: It is essential for large language model (LLM) technology to serve many different cultural sub-communities in a manner that is acceptable to each community. However, research on LLM alignment has so far predominantly focused on predicting a unified response preference of annotators from certain regions. This paper aims to advance the development of alignment models with a more global outlook, that are able to accurately represent the preferences of subcommunities and do not exhibit excessive bias towards any of them. We focus on the development of reward models for this purpose and present a novel reward model training algorithm (SCPO) that can incorporate diverse cultural preferences in a balanced manner. Our method results in performance increases of the minority reward model of up to 7 points over the baseline model across two datasets, PRISM and GlobalOpinionQA, and across 7 countries. SCPO is up to 280% more training data-efficient than full-data finetuning of reward models. In addition, we perform analysis of bias by separately evaluating on the preference of subcommunities and show that excessive bias is mitigated via our weighting method. Our code is available at

</details>


### [59] [Domain Generalizable Adaptation of 3D Vision-Language Models via Regularized Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18472)
*Sneha Paul, Zachary Patterson, Nizar Bouguila*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReFine3D，一种正则化微调框架，用于3D大语言模型（LMM）的域泛化适应。通过选择性层微调、多视角一致性正则化、LLM生成的同义词文本多样性、点云渲染视觉监督以及测试时增强等策略，在多个3D域泛化基准上显著提升了基类到新类泛化、跨数据集迁移、鲁棒性和少样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D视觉语言模型在域适应时面临过拟合和灾难性遗忘问题，尤其是在下游域数据有限的情况下。现有方法缺乏有效的正则化策略来平衡泛化能力与任务性能。

Method: ReFine3D框架包含：(1) 选择性层微调，仅更新部分网络层；(2) 多视角一致性正则化，对增强后的点云保持特征一致性；(3) 文本多样性正则化，利用LLM生成同义词提示；(4) 点云渲染视觉监督，引入2D视觉先验；(5) 测试时增强与置信度聚合机制。

Result: 在多个3D域泛化基准上，ReFine3D在基类到新类泛化提升1.36%，跨数据集迁移提升2.43%，鲁棒性提升1.80%，少样本准确率提升最多3.11%，超越先前最优方法且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: ReFine3D通过多种正则化策略有效缓解了3D LMM在域适应中的过拟合和灾难性遗忘问题，为3D多模态模型的域泛化微调提供了一种高效且通用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D视觉语言模型的域泛化微调，属于多模态对齐与适应问题，映射到现有规范子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment），因为其核心是正则化微调框架以提升模型在目标域上的泛化能力，属于多模态对齐与适应范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D domain generalization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: 3D vision-language models, domain generalization, regularized fine-tuning, multi-view consistency, test-time augmentation

Abstract: Domain adaptation remains a central challenge in 3D vision, especially for multimodal foundation models that align 3D point clouds with visual and textual data. While these models demonstrate strong general capabilities, adapting them to downstream domains with limited data often leads to overfitting and catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we introduce ReFine3D, a regularized fine-tuning framework designed for domain-generalizable tuning of 3D large multimodal models (LMMs). ReFine3D combines selective layer tuning with two targeted regularization strategies: multi-view consistency across augmented point clouds and text diversity through synonym-based prompts generated by large language models. Additionally, we incorporate point-rendered vision supervision and a test-time augmentation mechanism with confidence-based aggregation to further enhance robustness. Extensive experiments across different 3D domain generalization benchmarks show that ReFine3D improves base-to-novel class generalization by 1.36%, cross-dataset transfer by 2.43%, robustness to corruption by 1.80%, and few-shot accuracy by up to 3.11%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods with minimal added computational overhead.

</details>


### [60] [Learning from Own Solutions: Self-Conditioned Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18810)
*Yingyu Shan, Yuhang Guo, Zihao Cheng, Zeming Liu, Xiangrong Zhu, Xinyi Wang, Jiashu Yao, Wei Lin, Hongru Wang, Heyan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出SC-GRPO，利用自条件化轨迹产生的KL散度作为GRPO梯度的乘性权重，实现token级别的信用分配，在数学、代码和智能体任务上超越GRPO和DAPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（如GRPO）对所有token分配均匀信用，浪费梯度在常规token上而对关键推理步骤信用不足。现有的token级信用分配方法需要过程奖励模型或外部教师，限制了在纯RLVR场景中的适用性。

Method: 观察到模型在其自身已验证轨迹上做条件化会产生可测量的逐token KL散度，证明从已验证轨迹构建的自教师进行蒸馏在多条验证轨迹存在时会导致不可行的加权平均解。提出SC-GRPO，将上述KL散度作为GRPO梯度的乘性权重，实现无需外部资源的token级信用分配。

Result: 在数学、代码和智能体任务的五个基准上，SC-GRPO比GRPO平均提升8.1%，比DAPO提升5.9%，且具有更强的分布外泛化性能，同时超越了On-Policy Distillation方法。

Conclusion: SC-GRPO通过自条件化KL散度实现了有效的token级信用分配，无需外部模型或过程奖励，在纯RLVR场景中显著优于现有方法，为LLM推理训练提供了更高效的强化学习方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SC-GRPO，一种针对可验证奖励的强化学习方法，用于训练LLM的推理能力。虽然论文主要关注LLM推理，但RLVR方法（如GRPO）广泛用于多模态推理任务的对齐训练，属于多模态强化学习与对齐范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL credit assignment for LLM reasoning, not on the user's priority continuous multimodal or video generation topics. Signals: RLVR credit assignment, self-conditioned KL divergence, math/code/agent tasks, outperforms GRPO/DAPO, no direct multimodal or video generation application

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has driven substantial progress in training LLMs for reasoning tasks, but representative methods such as GRPO assign uniform credit across all tokens, wasting gradient on routine tokens while under-crediting pivotal reasoning steps. Existing token-level credit assignment methods require resources beyond the model's own rollouts. GRPO variants rely on process reward models or ground-truth answers. Knowledge distillation assigns credit through per-token divergence but requires external teachers (On-Policy Distillation) or privileged information (On-Policy Self Distillation). However, these dependencies limit applicability in the pure RLVR setting. We observe that conditioning the model on its own verified trajectories induces a measurable per-token KL divergence between the original and conditioned distributions, and prove that distilling from a self-teacher constructed by verified trajectories leads to infeasible weighted-average solutions when multiple verified trajectories exist. We propose SC-GRPO (Self-Conditioned GRPO), which uses KL divergence mentioned before as a multiplicative weight on GRPO gradients. Across five benchmarks spanning math, code, and agentic tasks, SC-GRPO consistently outperforms 8.1% over GRPO and 5.9% over DAPO with stronger OOD performance. Moreover, SC-GRPO achieves higher performance than OPD.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Consensus Frame GRPO (CF-GRPO)，一种无需时序标注的过程级奖励框架，通过构建共识帧先验并与模型帧使用分数对齐，为视频 MLLM 提供高对比度的视觉焦点奖励信号，从而提升视频推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果奖励（outcome-only reward）在视频 MLLM 推理中无法提供关于"哪些视觉证据应支持答案"的细粒度指导，导致模型缺乏证据感知能力。受多感官整合中一致性线索增强感知显著性的启发，作者希望设计一种无需人工时序标注的过程级奖励机制，引导模型关注正确的视觉帧。

Method: 提出 CF-GRPO 框架，包含：(1) 共识帧先验构建——从时序覆盖、场景切换线索和查询条件视觉相关性三个内在视频线索中生成先验；(2) 模型侧帧使用分数——从视觉和响应表征中计算；(3) Consensus Frame Reward (CFR)——通过显著性感知稀疏聚合和分布锐化，优化先验与模型分数之间的一致性，提供高对比度奖励信号。整个过程无需人工时序标注。

Result: 在多个复杂视频推理基准上，VideoCFR 取得了具有竞争力的性能，在多项指标上优于代表性 Video-MLLM 和 RL 基线方法。共识先验还提供了训练过程中被强调的证据帧的可解释性视图。

Conclusion: CF-GRPO 通过过程级奖励有效引导视频 MLLM 关注正确的视觉证据，无需人工时序标注即可提升推理质量，同时提供了可解释的视觉焦点分析能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CF-GRPO，一种面向视频 MLLM 的时序无标注过程级奖励框架，用于增强视频推理中视觉证据的选择能力。核心贡献在于视频多模态推理中的过程奖励与视觉焦点对齐，属于多模态推理范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Focuses on video MLLM reasoning with process-level rewards, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Video-MLLM reasoning, process-level reward, consensus frame alignment, no temporal annotation, reinforcement learning for vision

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but applying outcome-only rewards to video multimodal large language models (Video-MLLMs) provides limited guidance on which visual evidence should support the answer. Inspired by multisensory integration, where consistent cues can enhance the salience and reliability of perceptual estimates, we introduce Consensus Frame GRPO (CF-GRPO), a temporal-annotation-free process-level reward framework for evidence-aware video reasoning. CF-GRPO constructs a consensus frame prior from intrinsic video cues, including temporal coverage, scene-transition cues, and query-conditioned visual relevance. It then computes a model-side frame-use score from visual and response representations and optimizes their agreement through the Consensus Frame Reward (CFR). With salience-aware sparse aggregation and distribution sharpening, CFR provides a high-contrast reward signal without requiring human temporal annotations. Experiments show that VideoCFR achieves competitive performance across complex video reasoning benchmarks and improves several metrics over representative Video-MLLM and RL baselines, while the consensus prior provides an interpretable view of the evidence frames emphasized during training. The implementation is available at .

</details>

*Jinhao Song, Shan Liang, Yiqun Yue, Zhuhuayang Zhang, Tianqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI
