TL;DR: ThinkDeception 提出了一种可解释的多模态欺骗检测框架，首次将 MLLM 引入该领域，将二分类任务转化为显式认知推理过程。通过构建多模态 CoT 数据集训练基础模型，并提出 VAC-GRPO 渐进式强化学习策略（含难度分层、过程感知奖励和反思学习），在主流基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺骗检测方法多为端到端黑盒范式，缺乏可解释性，无法提供透明的推理轨迹，也难以捕捉欺骗行为中细微的跨模态不一致性。

Method: 提出 ThinkDeception 框架：(1) 构建了首个带逐步推理标注的多模态 Chain of Thought 数据集；(2) 训练 ThinkDeception Base 基础模型；(3) 核心创新 VAC-GRPO（视觉-音频一致性组相对策略优化），包含渐进式训练策略，将数据分为四个难度层级实现易到难的认知过渡，结合多维过程感知奖励机制和反思学习范式。

Result: 在主流基准上达到 SOTA，显著优于现有方法，在检测准确率和推理质量两方面均取得领先。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将欺骗检测领域推向可解释的多模态认知推理方向，验证了模态不一致性在解码欺骗中的关键作用，以及渐进式强化学习对推理质量提升的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文将多模态欺骗检测建模为显式认知推理过程，使用多模态 Chain of Thought (CoT) 数据集和渐进式强化学习框架提升推理质量，核心贡献在于多模态推理与思维链，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot"。

Importance reason: Novel application of MLLM to deception detection with progressive RL, but the topic is far from user's core priorities in continuous models and generation. Signals: Multimodal deception detection, MLLM reasoning, Chain-of-Thought dataset, Progressive reinforcement learning, VAC-GRPO

Abstract: Multimodal deception detection is critical for identifying fraudulent intentions, yet existing approaches predominantly rely on end to end black--box paradigms. These methods suffer from a severe lack of interpretability failing to provide transparent reasoning trajectories and struggling to explicitly capture the subtle, cross modal inconsistencies inherent in deceptive behaviors. To transcend these limitations, we propose ThinkDeception, a novel and interpretable multimodal deception detection framework. As a pioneering effort, it introduces Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) into this domain, transforming deception detection from a traditional binary classification task into an explicit cognitive reasoning process. Facilitated by the first meticulously annotated step--by--step multimodal Chain of Thought (CoT) dataset, we develop a foundational model, ThinkDeception Base, empirically validating the critical role of modal inconsistency in decoding deception. Building upon this foundation, our core innovation lies in proposing Visual-Audio Consistency Group Relative Policy Optimization(VAC--GRPO) equipped with a progressive training strategy. Distinct from standard GRPO, we stratify the training data into four progressive difficulty tiers, guiding the model through a psychologically grounded easy--to--hard cognitive transition. By innovatively coupling this dynamic curriculum scheduler with a multi dimensional, process aware reward mechanism and a reflective learning paradigm, we significantly elevate the model's overall reasoning quality. Extensive experiments on mainstream benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkDeception establishes a new SOTA, significantly outperforming existing methods in both detection accuracy and rationale quality. Ultimately, this work successfully drives the field of deception detection toward interpretable, multimodal cognitive reasoning.

</details>


### [63] [Rethinking Reward Supervision: Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19327)
*Siyi Gu, Jialin Chen, Sophia Zhou, Arman Cohan, Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation（RCSD）框架，通过评分准则（rubric）作为结构化细粒度反馈，对推理语言模型进行在线策略自蒸馏，替代传统的单一参考思维链蒸馏或标量奖励优化，在科学推理基准上平均超越 GRPO 1.0 分、OPSD 0.9 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型的后训练方法存在两大问题：(1) 监督蒸馏依赖昂贵的思维链标注，且标注本身可能包含噪声、不完整或部分错误，即使最终答案正确，不完美的推理过程也会干扰学习；(2) 强化学习使用标量奖励信号，将评估反馈压缩为单一数值，无法区分回答中哪些方面需要改进。因此需要一种更细粒度的、结构化的反馈机制来指导推理过程学习。

Method: 提出 Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation (RCSD) 框架，包含两阶段流程：第一阶段学习生成任务特定的评分准则（rubric），第二阶段训练评分准则引导的推理器。具体地，教师模型以准则级别的 rubrics 为条件，对学生的采样轨迹提供 token 级别的指导。该方法避免将单一参考推理过程作为唯一的监督目标，而是通过 rubrics 明确强回答应满足的标准，实现对推理过程的细粒度信用分配，优于标量奖励优化。

Result: 在多个科学推理基准上，RCSD 平均超越 GRPO 1.0 分、OPSD 0.9 分，表明 rubric 条件化的自蒸馏能有效将准则级别的标准转化为 token 级别的推理过程指导。

Conclusion: Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation 通过结构化细粒度反馈替代标量奖励和单一参考推理过程，为推理语言模型的后训练提供了一种更有效的范式，在科学推理任务上取得了显著改进。

Classification reason: 该论文关注推理语言模型的后训练，提出基于评分准则（rubric）的自蒸馏框架以改进推理过程。虽然论文主要聚焦于纯文本推理（科学推理基准），但其方法（rubric-conditioned self-distillation）与多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）方向高度相关，该方向涵盖科学推理、推理过程优化等主题。论文的核心贡献在于改进推理过程中的细粒度信用分配，属于推理增强方法，因此映射到该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Novel self-distillation framework for reasoning models, but tangential to user's focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Rubric-conditioned self-distillation, token-level guidance, reasoning language model, science reasoning benchmarks, GRPO/OPSD comparison

Abstract: Post-training of reasoning language models is commonly driven by supervised distillation and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Distillation often relies on chain-of-thought annotations that are expensive to obtain and may themselves be noisy, incomplete, or partially incorrect; even when the final solution is correct, an imperfect rationale can interfere with learning. Reinforcement learning with verified rewards, on the other hand, typically compresses evaluative feedback into a scalar signal, obscuring which aspects of a response should be improved. We propose \textbf{Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation}, a framework that incorporates rubrics as structured, fine-grained feedback for on-policy self-distillation. Our method conditions the teacher model on criterion-level rubrics and uses it to provide token-level guidance on the student's own sampled trajectories. This design avoids treating a single reference rationale as the sole supervision target. Instead, rubrics specify what a strong response should satisfy, enabling more fine-grained credit assignment over the reasoning process than scalar reward optimization. We instantiate this framework with a two-stage pipeline that first learns to generate task-specific rubrics and then trains a rubric-guided reasoner. We evaluate on a diverse suite of science reasoning benchmarks and results show that rubric-conditioned self-distillation effectively converts rubric-level criteria into token-level guidance over the reasoning process, surpassing GRPO by 1.0 points and OPSD by 0.9 points on average.

</details>


### [64] [Native Active Perception as Reasoning for Omni-Modal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19341)
*Zhenghao Xing, Ruiyang Xu, Yuxuan Wang, Jinzheng He, Ziyang Ma, Qize Yang, Yunfei Chu, Jin Xu, Junyang Lin, Chi-Wing Fu, Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (69.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniAgent 是首个原生全模态（omni-modal）智能体，将长视频理解建模为 POMDP 驱动的迭代 Observation-Thought-Action 循环，通过主动感知选择性提取音视频线索到文本记忆，解耦推理复杂度与视频时长。引入 Agentic SFT 和 Agentic RL（TAURA）训练策略，在多个长视频理解基准上达到开源 SOTA，7B 模型在 LVBench 上超越 72B 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解模型采用"全量观看"范式，计算成本随视频时长线性增长；交互式框架依赖全局预扫描，上下文成本仍与视频长度相关。作者希望实现一种原生主动感知机制，使模型能像人类一样根据问题需求选择性关注视频片段，将推理复杂度与原始视频时长解耦。

Method: (1) 将视频理解建模为 POMDP 驱动的迭代 Observation-Thought-Action 循环，智能体通过主动感知（如播放、暂停、快进等动作）选择性提取音视频线索到持久化文本记忆；(2) Agentic SFT：通过 best-of-N 轨迹合成与双阶段质量控制引导原生主动感知能力；(3) Agentic RL with TAURA：利用 turn 级熵（turn-level entropy）引导信用分配，聚焦关键发现轮次。

Result: 在 VideoMME、LVBench 等 10 个基准上达到开源模型 SOTA。在 LVBench 上，7B 模型以 50.5% 超越 10 倍大的 Qwen2.5-VL-72B（47.3%）。展现出正向测试时扩展（test-time scaling），性能随推理轮次增加而提升。

Conclusion: OmniAgent 验证了原生主动感知作为推理范式的有效性，通过迭代 Observation-Thought-Action 循环将推理复杂度与视频时长解耦，实现了正向测试时扩展，为长视频理解提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OmniAgent，将长视频理解建模为 POMDP 驱动的迭代 Observation-Thought-Action 循环，通过主动感知（active perception）逐步推理并选择性提取音视频线索到文本记忆。核心是推理时扩展（test-time scaling）和多步推理，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel POMDP-based active perception for long video understanding with strong scaling and SOTA results, but not directly on priority subtopics. Signals: active perception, long video understanding, POMDP reasoning, test-time scaling, agentic RL

Abstract: Passive models for long video understanding typically rely on a "watch-it-all" paradigm, processing frames uniformly regardless of query difficulty, causing computational cost to grow with video duration. Although interactive frameworks have emerged, they often rely on global pre-scanning, and their context cost still scales with video length. We propose OmniAgent, the first native omni-modal agent that formulates video understanding as a POMDP-based iterative Observation-Thought-Action cycle. OmniAgent executes on-demand actions to selectively distill audio-visual cues into a persistent textual memory, effectively decoupling reasoning complexity from raw video duration. To operationalize this, we introduce (1) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning to bootstrap native active perception via best-of-N trajectory synthesis with dual-stage quality control, and (2) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA (Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage), which leverages turn-level entropy to steer credit assignment toward pivotal discovery turns. Crucially, OmniAgent exhibits positive test-time scaling, where performance improves as the number of reasoning turns increases, validating the efficacy of active perception. Empirical results across ten benchmarks (e.g., VideoMME, LVBench) demonstrate that OmniAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Notably, on LVBench, our 7B agent outperforms the 10$\times$ larger Qwen2.5-VL-72B (50.5% vs. 47.3%).

</details>


### [65] [Visual-OPSD: Cross-Modal On-Policy Self-Distillation for Efficient Unified Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18974)
*Pengyu Li, Zhitao Gao, Lingling Zhang, Muye Huang, Yuanming Li, Fangzhi Xu, Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文发现统一多模态模型（UMMs）中生成的"视觉思维"（VT）虽然渲染后注意力集中在VT上，但去除或加噪VT对准确率影响很小，而VT生成路径的语义内容（而非渲染像素）编码了有用的推理信息。基于此提出Visual-OPSD，通过教师（有VT）和学生（仅文本）共享权重的在线自蒸馏，将生成式推理转移到纯文本学生模型，实现14.3倍加速并在9个基准上平均提升+3.40pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）在空间推理任务中通过生成"视觉思维"（VT）来辅助文本推理，但多步扩散生成VT带来了约一个数量级的推理开销。作者发现VT的渲染像素对最终准确率贡献有限（去除或加噪VT后准确率几乎不变），但VT生成路径的语义内容确实编码了有用信息。因此希望在不牺牲推理质量的前提下，消除昂贵的VT生成过程，实现高效的多模态推理。

Method: 提出Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation（Visual-OPSD）。教师和学生共享相同权重但上下文不同：教师看到特权VT（由扩散模型生成），学生仅看到问题。在学生的on-policy推理轨迹上进行token级JSD（Jensen-Shannon Divergence）蒸馏，将教师的推理能力转移到纯文本学生模型。训练时，教师使用VT生成完整推理，学生仅基于文本进行推理，通过KL散度诊断验证蒸馏效果。

Result: 在9个基准上，Visual-OPSD相比生成式教师模型平均提升+3.40pp，同时实现14.3倍加速（每样本10.0s vs 142.8s）。在VSP任务上比同规模VLM提升+63.83pp。高斯噪声控制实验显示真实VT带来+10.28pp提升而噪声仅+0.40pp，且KL差距闭合58.4%，证实增益来自生成路径的语义内容而非噪声。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了统一多模态模型中视觉思维生成路径的语义内容比渲染像素更重要，并提出Visual-OPSD方法通过在线自蒸馏有效利用这一发现，在显著加速推理的同时提升性能。该方法为高效多模态推理提供了新思路，表明生成式推理的语义知识可以通过蒸馏转移到纯文本推理中。

Classification reason: 论文研究统一多模态模型中的视觉推理（visual thoughts）与文本推理结合，属于多模态推理范畴。虽然涉及连续视觉token生成（扩散），但核心贡献是推理效率提升（通过自蒸馏将生成式推理蒸馏到纯文本推理），因此映射到[多模态推理与思维链]（CANONICAL）最为贴切。同时与连续多模态推理方向有交叉。

Importance reason: Directly addresses unified multimodal reasoning efficiency with a novel distillation method, aligning with user's high-weight priority on unified multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient inference, self-distillation, visual reasoning, 14.3x speedup

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) interleave generated ''visual thoughts'' (VTs) with text reasoning to improve spatial tasks. This incurs roughly an order-of-magnitude inference cost from multi-step diffusion. We find this cost yields limited direct benefit. On ThinkMorph, removing or noising VTs barely changes accuracy across nine benchmarks. Once rendered, attention concentrates on the VT regardless of content. Yet a KL diagnostic shows that conditioning on a privileged VT trace shifts the model's completion distribution. This suggests the generation pathway encodes useful reasoning beyond the rendered pixels. Motivated by this gap, we propose Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation(Visual-OPSD). Teacher and student share identical weights but differ in context: the teacher sees privileged VTs while the student sees only the question. Token-level JSD distillation on on-policy student trajectories transfers the teacher's reasoning to a text-only student. Across nine benchmarks, Visual-OPSD improves over its generative teacher by $+3.40$pp with $14.3\times$ speedup (10.0s vs. 142.8s per sample) and outperforms same-scale VLMs by $+63.83$pp on VSP. A Gaussian-noise control ($+0.40$pp vs. $+10.28$pp for real VTs) and $58.4\%$ closure of the KL gap confirm that gains come from the semantic content of the generation pathway.

</details>


### [66] [Seeing Before Reasoning: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Shortcut-Resilient Multimodal On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19120)
*Sihan Wang, Xiyao Liu, Lianqing Liu, Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViGOS 提出了一种视觉锚定的在线自蒸馏框架，通过解耦感知与推理阶段（先写视觉描述再推理），并引入图像专属感知教师和特权推理教师，有效缓解了 MLLM 自蒸馏中文本参考目标导致的视觉捷径问题，在多个基准上提升了图像依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏（OPSD）在 LLM 推理中效果显著，但直接扩展到 MLLM 时会产生捷径：特权目标（privileged target）可能主要基于文本参考目标而非图像来引导 token 生成，导致模型忽视视觉输入。需要一种方法让 MLLM 在自蒸馏过程中真正依赖视觉信息。

Method: 提出 ViGOS 框架，包含：(1) 解耦的推理流程——学生模型先写视觉描述（感知阶段），再基于描述推理出最终答案（推理阶段）；(2) 图像专属感知教师（image-only perception teacher）监督视觉描述；(3) 特权推理教师（privileged reasoning teacher）在相同学生前缀上监督推理和最终答案；(4) 参考教师（reference teacher）仅在无效 rollout 时用于恢复输出格式。

Result: 在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间定位和视觉语言先验等基准上，ViGOS 保持了 OPSD 的主要优势，并在捷径易发场景中显著提升了图像依赖行为，减少了模型对文本先验的过度依赖。

Conclusion: ViGOS 通过解耦感知与推理阶段，并引入专门的感知教师和推理教师，有效解决了 MLLM 在线自蒸馏中的视觉捷径问题，为多模态模型的后训练提供了一种更鲁棒的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ViGOS 框架，通过先写视觉描述再推理的方式解耦感知与推理，解决 MLLM 在自蒸馏中的捷径问题。核心贡献在于多模态推理的改进，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel self-distillation method to fix visual shortcut in MLLM reasoning, aligning with multimodal understanding and reasoning priorities. Signals: multimodal reasoning, self-distillation, visual shortcut, perception-reasoning decoupling, MLLM post-training

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a model on its own rollouts and uses a frozen copy to provide dense token-level targets conditioned on a reference target. This works well for LLM reasoning, but a direct extension to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can create a shortcut: the privileged target may guide tokens mainly based on the text reference target rather than the image. We propose ViGOS, a visually grounded OPSD framework for MLLM post-training. The student first writes a visual description and then reasons toward the final answer. For valid rollouts, an image-only perception teacher supervises the description, while a privileged reasoning teacher supervises the reasoning and final answer on the same student prefix. A reference teacher is used only for invalid rollouts to recover the output format. Across general vision-language, expert reasoning, visual math, spatial grounding, and visual-language-prior benchmarks, ViGOS keeps the main benefits of OPSD and improves image-grounded behavior in shortcut-prone settings.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像描述方法难以生成包含对象属性、事件背景和深层意义的丰富描述，尤其对于视觉线索无法直接观察到的细节。需要借助外部知识来增强描述的信息量和上下文丰富度。

Method: 提出层次化多模态文章检索增强框架：(1) 层次化多模态文章检索机制，考虑文章结构感知特征（标题、正文等加权文本组件和视觉布局模式）以及多面相似度计算（内容-视觉、视觉-视觉、语篇定位）；(2) 上下文相关性精炼阶段增强检索信息；(3) 生成阶段：VLM生成简洁图像描述 → 基于描述从检索文章中分割相关信息 → LLM利用描述和提取知识生成全面上下文丰富的描述。

Result: 在ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025 Challenge的OpenEvent-V1数据集私有测试集上取得第5名，总体得分为0.2824。

Conclusion: 层次化多模态检索结合结构感知特征和多面相似度计算能有效提升知识增强的新闻图像描述生成质量，验证了检索增强生成在新闻图像描述任务中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出层次化多模态文章检索机制用于知识增强的新闻图像描述生成，核心是跨模态检索（文本-视觉、视觉-视觉）与检索增强生成，属于多模态检索方向。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on a niche retrieval-augmented captioning task for news images, which is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Multimodal retrieval, News image captioning, VLM+LLM pipeline, Challenge participation, Low personal direction weight

Abstract: Traditional image captioning methods often struggle to generate comprehensive, context-rich descriptions, especially for details not directly observable from visual cues. To overcome this, we propose a novel retrieval-augmented image captioning framework that generates captions with deeper insights, such as object attributes, event context, and underlying significance, by leveraging external knowledge. Our approach features a hierarchical multi-modal article retrieval mechanism that moves beyond monolithic text entities. This retrieval considers article structure-aware features, including weighted textual components (e.g., headlines, body sections) and visual placement patterns, alongside multi-faceted similarity computations (content--visual, visual--visual, and discourse positioning). A subsequent contextual relevance refinement stage further enhances the retrieved information. The retrieved articles then serve as the knowledge base for caption generation: first, a VLM generates a concise image description; second, we segment relevant information from the retrieved articles based on this description; and finally, an LLM utilizes both the description and extracted knowledge to generate a comprehensive, contextually detailed caption. We participated in the ACM Multimedia EVENTA 2025 Challenge and achieved 5th place with an overall score of 0.2824 on the private test set of the OpenEvent-V1 dataset. Source code is publicly released at .

</details>


### [68] [LARE: Low-Attention Region Encoding for Text-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18885)
*Abdulmalik Alquwayfili, Faisal Almeshal, Jumanah Almajnouni, Leena Alotaibi, Faisal Alhajari, Mohammed Alkhrashi, Alreem Almuhrij, Abdullah Aldwyish, Raied Aljadaany, Huda Alamri, Muhammad Kamran J. Khan*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LARE 提出低注意力区域编码框架，通过双编码策略并行编码图像的完整区域和低注意力区域，生成更丰富多样的图像嵌入，提升拥挤场景下的文本-图像检索性能。同时构建了 Dense-Set 子集用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉编码器存在显著性偏差，倾向于关注图像中的主导物体而忽略低注意力区域，这在拥挤场景的细粒度检索中尤为不利。现有检索模型无法捕捉这些被忽略的视觉线索。

Method: LARE 采用双编码策略：一个编码器处理完整图像，另一个编码器专门编码低注意力区域（通过注意力图识别出的被忽略区域），两者并行编码后融合得到更丰富多样的图像嵌入。同时构建了 Dense-Set 子集（从 COCO 和 Flickr30K 中筛选），为拥挤场景图像提供更丰富的描述。

Result: 实验表明，LARE 在 Dense-Set 和标准检索基准上均提升了检索性能，通过保留非主导的细微视觉线索在共享潜空间中实现了更好的文本-图像匹配。

Conclusion: 显式建模低注意力区域可以有效缓解视觉编码器的显著性偏差，提升拥挤场景下的细粒度图像检索能力，双编码策略是一种简单有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本-图像检索（text-image retrieval），属于跨模态检索任务，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on text-image retrieval with low-attention encoding, which is not aligned with user's priority on generation and world models. Signals: text-image retrieval, low-attention region encoding, crowded scenes, dual-encoding, Dense-Set benchmark

Abstract: Image retrieval in crowded scenes is particularly challenging due to the salience bias of conventional visual encoders, which tend to focus on dominant objects while neglecting low-attention regions that are often crucial for fine-grained retrieval. We propose LARE (Low-Attention Region Encoding), a framework that explicitly models these overlooked regions. LARE adopts a dual-encoding strategy that encodes low-attention regions of an image and the full image in parallel, leading to more diverse and informative image embeddings. To evaluate image retrieval performance in challenging crowded scenes, we introduce Dense-Set, a challenging subset derived from COCO and Flickr30K. In this subset, images are re-captioned to provide richer descriptions of low-attention or previously overlooked regions. This dataset highlights the limitations of existing retrieval models and enables a more rigorous evaluation under densely crowded scene conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves retrieval performance by preserving subtle, non-dominant visual cues within the shared latent space.

</details>


### [69] [Show, Don't Ask: Generative Visual Disambiguation for Composed Image Retrieval with Turn-Valid Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18992)
*Amsisan Tran, Baogh Le, Tuan Kiet Pham, Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLARA，一种通过展示视觉候选图像而非文本提问来解决组合图像检索中用户意图歧义性的框架。CLARA 利用 conformal prediction 保证多轮交互的覆盖有效性，并通过用户直接选择视觉原型来消除歧义，在开放域和时尚检索基准上优于文本提问基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合图像检索（CIR）中，用户的查询（参考图像+文本修改）往往对应多个可能的图像，存在歧义。现有方法通过 conformal prediction 估计歧义并让用户回答文本问题来澄清，但存在两个局限：(1) 覆盖保证仅在第一轮交互有效；(2) 文本问题难以解决细粒度视觉差异（如外观、属性、视角）。因此需要一种更有效的多轮交互消歧方法。

Method: CLARA 框架的核心思想是"展示而非提问"：向用户展示一小批视觉候选图像（prototype panel），用户直接选择最接近目标的图像。具体包括：(1) 利用 likelihood ratio 对校准集重新加权，以在多轮交互中维持 conformal 覆盖保证；(2) 展示的原型被约束为当前候选集的代表，并映射到真实语料库图像，避免生成图像人为提升覆盖；(3) 通过用户选择提供直接的视觉信号，无需模型预测用户答案。

Result: 在开放域和时尚检索基准上，CLARA 在单轮检索中达到 SOTA 水平；在多轮交互中维持名义覆盖保证；相比强文本提问基线，CLARA 用更少的轮次找到目标图像。尤其在涉及视角或细粒度属性的歧义场景中，视觉消歧显著优于文本提问。

Conclusion: CLARA 通过视觉展示替代文本提问，有效解决了组合图像检索中的歧义问题，并在多轮交互中保持了统计覆盖保证。该方法在细粒度视觉差异场景下优势明显，为交互式多模态检索提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究组合图像检索（Composed Image Retrieval, CIR），属于跨模态/多模态检索范畴，与现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on composed image retrieval with visual clarification, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: composed image retrieval, visual disambiguation, conformal prediction, multi-turn interaction, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) uses a reference image and a text modification to search for a target image. However, such queries often describe several possible images rather than one exact target, making the user's intent ambiguous. Recent methods address this by using conformal prediction to estimate ambiguity and by asking users clarifying text questions. However, these methods have two limitations: their coverage guarantee only holds at the first interaction, and text questions are often insufficient for resolving fine-grained visual differences such as appearance, attributes, or viewpoint. We propose CLARA, a clarification framework that resolves ambiguity by showing users a small panel of visual alternatives. Instead of answering text questions, the user simply selects the prototype image closest to the intended target. This provides a direct visual signal and avoids relying on a model to predict the user's answer. To maintain valid conformal guarantees across multiple interaction rounds, CLARA reweights calibration using the likelihood ratio induced by the user's selection. The displayed prototypes are also constrained to represent the current candidate set and are snapped to real corpus images, ensuring that generated images cannot artificially improve coverage. Experiments on open-domain and fashion benchmarks show that CLARA matches single-turn state-of-the-art retrieval performance, maintains nominal coverage across interaction rounds, and finds the intended target in fewer rounds than strong text-question baselines. Its advantage is especially clear when ambiguity involves viewpoint or fine-grained attributes, where visual clarification is more effective than textual questioning.

</details>


### [70] [DREAM: Extending Vision-Language Models with Dual-Objective Encoding for Cross-Modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19062)
*Kaleem Ullah, Altaf Hussain, Muhammad Munsif, Sung Wook Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DREAM 提出了一种双路径表征增强与对齐模型，通过混合语言建模（masked + permuted LM）和层级视觉编码器（cascaded group attention）来改进视频-文本跨模态检索，在 MSRVTT、MSVD、LSMDC 上取得了新的 SOTA R1 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频检索系统依赖手工特征或浅层跨模态映射，难以捕捉复杂语义和时序动态；大规模视觉语言模型虽改善了跨模态对齐，但在细粒度时序依赖和语言结构建模方面仍存在不足。

Method: DREAM 包含两个核心设计：(1) 混合语言建模策略，结合 masked language modeling（捕捉局部语义）和 permuted language modeling（捕捉全局语义）来增强文本编码；(2) 层级视觉编码器，采用级联分组注意力（cascaded group attention），通过多阶段 token 交互和由粗到细的注意力精炼来整合时空信息。

Result: 在 MSRVTT、MSVD 和 LSMDC 基准上分别取得 49.4%、49.7% 和 27.3% 的 R1 分数，均达到新的 SOTA。定性分析显示模型能够在帧间保持连贯注意力，并将复杂查询与动态视频内容对齐。

Conclusion: 层级注意力和双目标文本建模在鲁棒的上下文感知视频检索中有效，为跨模态表征学习的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DREAM 模型，通过双目标编码（masked + permuted language modeling）和层级视觉编码器（cascaded group attention）提升视频-文本跨模态检索性能，在 MSRVTT、MSVD、LSMDC 上取得 SOTA。这属于多模态检索（跨模态检索）方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_retrieval"（多模态检索）。

Importance reason: Focuses on video-text retrieval with hybrid LM and hierarchical vision encoder, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: video-text retrieval, cross-modal alignment, masked+permuted LM, cascaded group attention, SOTA on MSRVTT/MSVD/LSMDC

Abstract: In today's media-driven world, the exponential growth of video content across domains such as surveillance, education, and entertainment has made retrieving semantically relevant videos via natural language queries increasingly critical. Early video retrieval systems relied on handcrafted features or shallow cross-modal mappings, limiting their ability to capture complex semantics and temporal dynamics. While large-scale vision-language models have improved cross-modal alignment, challenges remain in modeling fine-grained temporal dependencies and nuanced linguistic structures. In this paper, we introduce DREAM: Dual-path Representation Enhancement and Alignment Model, a novel multimodal framework that addresses these limitations through enhanced visual and textual encoding. DREAM incorporates a hybrid language modeling strategy that combines masked and permuted language modeling objectives to capture both local and global linguistic semantics. On the visual side, we design a hierarchical vision encoder with cascaded group attention, which integrates spatial and temporal information through multi-stage token interaction and coarse-to-fine attention refinement. We validate DREAM through comprehensive evaluations on the widely-used MSRVTT, MSVD and LSMDC benchmark datasets, where it achieves new state-of-the-art R1 scores of 49.4%, 49.7% and 27.3%, respectively. Qualitative analyses further show the model's ability to maintain coherent attention across frames and align complex queries with dynamic video content. These findings underscore the effectiveness of hierarchical attention and dual-objective textual modeling in enabling robust, context-aware video retrieval, and pave the way for future research in advancing cross-modal representation learning.

</details>

### [71] [SAMA: Semantic Anchor-aligned Augmentation for Unified Low-Resource Multimodal Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18780)
*Quanjiang Guo, Chong Mu, Jiazhou Pan, Ming Jia, Ling Tian, Hui Gao, Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMA（语义锚点对齐增强），一个统一的多模态数据增强框架，用于低资源多模态信息抽取（MIE）。SAMA通过构建结构化语义锚点指导协作多专家MLLM生成文本样本，并采用锚点保持扩散机制合成图像，再经双约束过滤模块筛选高质量样本。在MNER、MRE、MEE任务上，SAMA在全监督和低资源设置下均优于现有增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态信息抽取（MIE）任务（如MNER、MRE、MEE）面临严重的数据稀缺问题。现有数据增强方法存在跨模态对齐粗糙、任务特定设计碎片化、未能利用共享语义知识等局限，亟需一个统一的高保真数据增强框架。

Method: SAMA框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 语义锚点构建——从真实标签中提取结构化语义锚点；(2) 协作多专家MLLM（CME-MLLM）——包含通用适配器（共享语义）和任务特定适配器，生成多样且约束合规的文本样本；(3) 锚点保持扩散机制——使用锚点加权提示和潜在条件控制，在多样化视觉上下文的同时保持关键语义锚点；(4) 双约束过滤模块——基于跨模态一致性和锚点保真度筛选合成样本。

Result: 在MNER、MRE、MEE多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SAMA在全监督和低资源设置下均一致优于现有最先进的数据增强基线方法，验证了其通用性、鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: SAMA通过语义锚点对齐和协作多专家MLLM，实现了统一的高保真多模态数据增强，有效缓解了MIE任务中的数据稀缺问题，为低资源多模态信息抽取提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SAMA数据增强框架用于低资源多模态信息抽取（MNER/MRE/MEE），属于多模态理解与生成方向。虽然论文核心是数据增强方法，但其在多个MIE基准数据集上进行评测，且论文本身涉及多模态信息抽取任务的评测协议，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题相关——该主题涵盖多模态评测、新基准和评估协议。论文在多个标准基准上评估了SAMA的性能，属于该主题范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on low-resource multimodal information extraction, not aligned with user's priority on generation and world models. Signals: Multimodal information extraction, data augmentation, low-resource, MNER/MRE/MEE, not generation or world model

Abstract: Multimodal Information Extraction (MIE)-covering tasks such as Multimodal Named Entity Recognition (MNER), Relation Extraction (MRE), and Event Extraction (MEE)-is essential for understanding multimedia content but remains constrained by severe data scarcity. Although data augmentation is a promising remedy, existing approaches are impeded by coarse cross-modal alignment and fragmented, task-specific designs that fail to exploit shared semantic knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Semantic Anchor-aligned Multimodal Augmentation (SAMA), a unified framework for generating high-fidelity, task-aware synthetic data. SAMA constructs structured semantic anchors from ground-truth labels to guide a Collaborative Multi-Experts Multimodal Large Language Model (CME-MLLM), which integrates a Universal Adapter for shared semantics with Task-Specific Adapters to produce diverse yet constraint-compliant textual samples. For image synthesis, SAMA employs an Anchor-Preserving Diffusion mechanism that uses anchor-weighted prompts and latent conditioning to maintain critical semantic anchors while diversifying visual contexts. To eliminate the need for manual verification, SAMA further introduces a Dual-Constraint Filtering module that selects synthetic samples based on both cross-modal consistency and anchor fidelity. Extensive experiments across benchmark datasets for MNER, MRE, and MEE demonstrate that SAMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art augmentation baselines under both fully supervised and low-resource settings, underscoring its versatility, robustness, and effectiveness.

</details>


### [72] [DeFAb: A Verifiable Benchmark for Defeasible Abduction in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18557)
*Patrick Cooper, Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.LO, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DeFAb 是一个基于形式逻辑的可废止溯因推理基准，利用四十年积累的知识库生成 37 万+实例，通过多项式时间可验证的推导、保守性和最小性约束来评测基础模型的创造性理论推理能力。实验表明，前沿语言模型在该基准上表现不佳（最高 65%，渲染鲁棒评估下仅 23.5%），且思维链方差超过模型间差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评测模型的模式匹配和流畅文本生成能力，缺乏对形式化、可验证的创造性理论推理（如可废止溯因推理）的系统评测。作者希望构建一个逻辑严谨的基准，用可验证的符号标准来衡量模型的"创造力"和理论推理能力。

Method: 将 OpenCyc、YAGO、Wikidata 等分类层次与 ConceptNet、UMLS 等行为属性图结合，生成 18 个来源的 3375 万条物化规则，构建 372,648+ 个实例。每个实例要求模型通过推翻默认规则来解释异常现象，同时保留无关预期。所有假设必须通过多项式时间可验证的推导、保守性和最小性检查。还发布了 DeFAb-Hard（235 个高难度实例）和 CONJURE（基于 Lean 4/Mathlib 的核验证创造性变体）。

Result: 符号逻辑求解器在 50 微秒内以 100% 准确率解决所有实例；最佳前沿语言模型达到 65%，在渲染鲁棒评估下降至 23.5%。Level 2 渲染鲁棒准确率仅 7.8-23.5%，思维链方差（~36 个百分点）超过任何模型间差距。污染控制实验发现 Level 3 存在 +19.4 个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: 前沿语言模型未能可靠地内化可废止溯因推理能力，在逻辑严谨的推理基准上表现远逊于符号方法。DeFAb 提供了一个可验证的评测工具，其验证器还可作为偏好优化（DPO、RLVR/GRPO）的精确奖励信号。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 DeFAb 基准，用于评测基础模型在可废止溯因推理（defeasible abduction）上的能力，属于多模态/语言模型推理能力的评测基准，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on formal logic benchmark for defeasible abduction, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: Formal logic benchmark, low frontier model performance, no continuous token or generation focus, negative keywords absent, direction weight moderate

Abstract: A rule-based logic solver resolves every instance in our benchmark in under 50 microseconds with 100% accuracy; the best frontier language model reaches 65% at best and drops to 23.5% under rendering-robust evaluation (worst case over four surface renderings). We introduce DeFAb (Defeasible Abduction Benchmark), a dataset and generation pipeline that converts four decades of publicly funded knowledge bases into formally grounded instances for defeasible abduction: constructing hypotheses that explain anomalies by overriding defaults while preserving unrelated expectations. Because every hypothesis must pass polynomial-time checks for valid derivation, conservativity, and minimality, DeFAb makes logical rigor the instrument for measuring creativity and theoretical reasoning, scoring the disciplined construction of theory revisions rather than fluent but theory-destroying prose. The pipeline pairs taxonomic hierarchies (OpenCyc, YAGO, Wikidata) with behavioral property graphs (ConceptNet, UMLS) to produce 372,648+ instances across 33.75M materialized rules from 18 sources, in three levels with polynomial-time verifiable gold standards. Four frontier models do not reliably internalize defeasible reasoning: rendering-robust Level 2 accuracy is 7.8-23.5%; chain-of-thought variance (~36 pp) exceeds any inter-model gap; and a matched contamination control isolates a +19.4 pp Level 3 gap. We further release DeFAb-Hard (a 235-instance Level 3 difficulty variant; best model 53.3% vs 100% symbolic) and CONJURE (a kernel-verified transformative-creativity variant of 560 Lean 4/Mathlib instances whose gold answers are definitions the proof kernel did not previously contain, judge-free verifier; a pilot finds zero novel concepts). The same verifier doubles as an exact reward for preference optimization (DPO, RLVR/GRPO). Released under MIT at .

</details>

TL;DR: ForecastBench-Sim 是一个基于 Freeciv 游戏模拟环境的预测评测基准，通过游戏回放生成结构化世界状态和未来预测问题，支持连续/二值预测、条件因果问题以及罕见事件评估，为通用AI系统的概率推理能力提供可控、即时可解的评测平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界预测基准存在结果解析慢、尾部事件稀少、反事实问题难以评分等局限性，需要一种可控、即时可解的评测环境来评估AI系统的概率推理能力。

Method: 基于 Freeciv 回合制策略游戏的模拟环境，通过游戏回放生成结构化世界报告（当前游戏状态的快照），构建关于隐藏未来状态的预测问题；继续模拟并评分预测结果。支持连续/二值预测问题、任意时间跨度的预测、条件/因果干预世界以及罕见事件样本。

Result: 发布了完整的基准管线、问题族、评分协议和数据集，并报告了模型评估和匿名人类试点实验的验证结果。

Conclusion: ForecastBench-Sim 通过模拟世界的方式弥补了现实世界预测基准的不足，为研究动态世界状态下的概率推理提供了可控、可重复、即时可解的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个基于模拟世界（Freeciv游戏）的预测评测基准，属于多模态/通用AI系统的评测与基准构建，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The benchmark focuses on forecasting in a simulated game world, which is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: simulated forecasting benchmark, Freeciv game environment, probabilistic reasoning evaluation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics, no direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation

Abstract: Forecasting benchmarks for general-purpose AI systems usually inherit the constraints of the real world: outcomes resolve slowly, tail events are rare, and counterfactual questions are difficult to score. We introduce ForecastBench-Sim, a simulated-world forecasting benchmark built on game rollouts from Freeciv, a turn-based strategy game modelled on the Civilization series. Forecasters receive a fixed world report (a structured snapshot of the current game state) and answer questions about hidden future states; the benchmark then continues the simulation and scores forecasts. Because the world is simulated, the same setup can generate continuous or binary forecasting questions at arbitrary time horizons, paired intervention worlds for conditional or causal questions, and resolved examples of rare or disruptive outcomes. We describe the benchmark pipeline, question families, scoring protocol, and release artifacts, and report validation slices from model evaluations and an anonymized human pilot. ForecastBench-Sim is intended to complement real-world forecasting benchmarks by providing controlled, immediately resolvable tasks for studying probabilistic reasoning under dynamic world states.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时提取滑坡的视觉特征和高层地学语义，通用 VLM 在复杂地质场景中存在感知局限和领域幻觉，亟需面向滑坡灾害的专用智能识别框架。

Method: 1) 构建 LandslideBench 多模态细粒度数据集，通过多 VLM 交叉验证和交互式标注获得七种子类型标签、高分辨率图像、像素级掩码和文本描述；2) 基于 LandslideBench 对 VLM 进行 LoRA 微调得到 LandslideVLM，增强地质语义理解；3) 设计 LandslideAgent，以 LandslideVLM 为认知骨干，采用双规则控制器（结构化报告元数据约束和交叉验证识别约束）调控自动化工具调用。

Result: LandslideBench 为五种主流模型提供了细粒度分类和语义分割的基线；LandslideVLM 在滑坡判别、细粒度分类和语义描述质量上分别提升 10.96%、32.87% 和 15.91%；LandslideAgent 实现了自主多源空间数据推理的全流程智能化。

Conclusion: 所提出的领域规则增强智能体框架有效提升了滑坡识别与分析的自动化水平和准确性，为地质灾害智能解译提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 LandslideBench 多模态滑坡数据集（含七种细粒度子类型标注、高分辨率图像、像素级掩码和文本描述），属于多模态评测基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Specialized landslide application with no direct alignment to your priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: domain-specific VLM, landslide benchmark, rule-based agent, no continuous/autoregressive/diffusion focus, low overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Intelligent landslide hazard interpretation is critical for disaster prevention, yet current paradigms struggle to simultaneously extract visual features and high-level geoscientific semantics, while general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from perceptual limitations and domain hallucinations in complex geological scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose an instruction-driven agentic framework comprising three components. First, LandslideBench, a multimodal fine-grained dataset with seven subtype labels, high-resolution imagery, pixel-level masks, and high-quality textual descriptions, is constructed via multi-VLM cross-validation and interactive annotation. Then, LandslideVLM, a landslide-oriented VLM, is fine-tuned via LoRA on LandslideBench to enhance geological semantic understanding. Finally, LandslideAgent, a domain rule-enhanced agent taking LandslideVLM as its cognitive backbone, employs a dual-rule controller incorporating structured report metadata constraints and cross-validation identification constraints to regulate automated tool invocation. Experiments demonstrate that LandslideBench provides effective baselines across five mainstream models on fine-grained classification and semantic segmentation. LandslideVLM achieves accuracy improvements of 10.96%, 32.87%, and 15.91% on landslide discrimination, fine-grained classification, and semantic description quality, respectively. LandslideAgent further enables autonomous multi-source spatial data inference, realizing full-process intelligence for landslide identification and analysis.

</details>

*Linghao Feng, Yinqian Sun, Dongqi Liang, Sicheng Shen, Chenfei Yan, Yuxuan Peng, Yilin Zhao, Haibo Tong, Kai Li, FeiFei Zhao, Yi Zeng*

Method: 从显式风险维度和科学学科两个互补视角构建基准，覆盖 7 个学科、31 个子学科和 10 个风险维度，对主流 LLM 和科学专用 LLM 进行跨维度、跨学科的细粒度评估。

Conclusion: SciRisk-Bench 为 AI4Science 安全性评估提供了系统化的风险维度框架，有助于推动更安全的科学 AI 系统开发。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SciRisk-Bench，一个面向 AI4Science 安全性的评测基准，覆盖多学科与风险维度。这属于多模态/LLM 评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI safety benchmarking for science, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: AI4Science safety, risk dimension benchmark, LLM evaluation, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in AI for Science (AI4Science) workflows, from scientific question answering and literature analysis to laboratory planning and autonomous discovery. This progress creates an urgent need for safety benchmarks that evaluate not only scientific competence, but also whether models recognize and avoid risks in high-stakes scientific contexts. Existing AI4Science safety datasets cover several disciplines and task formats, leaving the underlying risk dimensions underspecified. We introduce \textbf{SciRisk-Bench}, a benchmark designed to evaluate AI4Science safety from two complementary perspectives: explicit risk dimensions and scientific disciplines. SciRisk-Bench covers 7 disciplines, 31 subdisciplines and 10 risk dimensions. In the experimental section, we evaluate both mainstream LLMs and science-oriented LLMs across risk dimensions, disciplines, and sub-disciplines, enabling fine-grained diagnosis of where scientific models remain unsafe.

</details>

*San Kim, Daechul Ahn, Reokyoung Kim, Hyeonbeom Choi, Seungyeon Jwa, Jonghyun Choi*

TL;DR: RTSGameBench 是一个基于即时战略游戏 Beyond All Reason 构建的 VLM 战略推理评测基准，包含多样化对战、诊断性小游戏和自演化生成框架，实验表明现有 VLM 在需要紧密协作和多智能体协调的场景下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在战略推理（即预测和影响其他智能体行为）方面能力不足，而现有的 RTS 基准存在评估范围有限、缺乏系统性能力诊断、场景覆盖固定等问题，亟需一个更全面、可扩展的评测基准。

Result: 多个 SOTA VLM 在需要紧密协调、多智能体协作以及任务规模增大时表现不佳，揭示了当前 VLM 在战略推理方面的局限性。

Conclusion: RTSGameBench 有效诊断了 VLM 在战略推理上的不足，为未来提升 VLM 的长期规划、多智能体协调和适应性能力提供了评测基础。

Importance reason: Focuses on RTS game benchmark for VLM strategic reasoning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: RTS benchmark, VLM strategic reasoning, multi-agent coordination, diagnostic mini-games, self-evolving generation

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with strategic reasoning, i.e., anticipating and influencing other agents' actions, under uncertainty in competitive and cooperative settings. Real-time strategy (RTS) games can be a natural testbed for diagnosing this limitation, as they demand coordination with allies, adaptation to opponents' strategy, and long-horizon planning under partial observability. However, existing RTS benchmarks offer limited evaluation scope, lack systematic competency diagnosis, and remain fixed in the pre-designed scenario coverage. To address these limitations, we present RTSGameBench, which is built on Beyond All Reason, a large-scale RTS game with an expanded battlefield that demands broader strategy diversity than the existing testbeds. The proposed benchmark provides evaluations through diverse gameplay across various matchup structures, diagnostic assessment via mini-games, each targeting an individual strategic competency, and extensible coverage via a self-evolving generation framework that converts free-form queries into new mini-games, improving over successive cycles. Additionally, for VLMs to operate in large-scale RTS games, we provide RTSGameAgent that manages units by an FSM with agentic memory. We empirically validate that multiple state-of-the-art VLMs do not perform well when matchups demand tighter coordination, multiagent coordination and when task scale increases.

</details>


### [77] [IndicContextEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Context Utilisation in Audio Large Language Models Across 8 Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19157)
*Sakshi Joshi, Dhruv Subhash Rathi, Sanskar Singh, Eldho Ittan George, R J Hari, Kaushal Bhogale, Mitesh M. Khapra*

TL;DR: 本文提出 IndicContextEval，一个覆盖8种印度语言、555位说话人、23个专业领域的56小时多语言音频基准，通过7级提示框架系统评估 AudioLLM 对上下文信息的利用能力，发现不同模型在上下文利用行为上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AudioLLM 虽然支持文本提示条件下的语音识别，但无法判断模型是真正利用了上下文信息还是依赖预训练中的参数化知识。现有基准缺乏对上下文利用能力的显式评估。

Method: 构建了包含555位说话人、8种印度语言、23个专业领域的56小时自然语音数据集，并设计了7级提示框架（从无上下文到对抗性错误实体提示），逐步引入元数据、自然语言描述、英文/本地文字实体列表及对抗性提示，系统评估模型上下文利用能力。

Result: 评估了5个模型（包括 Whisper、Qwen2-Audio、SALM、USM、SeamlessM4T），发现不同模型在上下文利用行为上存在显著差异，部分模型对对抗性提示敏感，揭示了 AudioLLM 在上下文利用方面的不足。

Conclusion: AudioLLM 的上下文利用能力需要显式评估，IndicContextEval 为这一方向提供了系统化的评测框架，有助于推动更可靠的上下文感知语音识别模型的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个针对音频大语言模型（AudioLLM）上下文利用能力的多语言评测基准 IndicContextEval，属于多模态（音频+文本）评测范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio LLM evaluation for Indic languages, which is outside the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: AudioLLM benchmark, Indic languages, context utilization, prompting framework, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: AudioLLMs enable speech recognition conditioned on textual prompts such as domain descriptions or entity lists. However, it remains unclear whether these models genuinely utilise such context or rely on parametric knowledge learned during pretraining. Existing benchmarks cannot answer this question because they evaluate transcription under fixed prompting conditions and rarely include explicit contextual inputs. We introduce IndicContextEval, a 56-hour multilingual benchmark of natural speech from 555 speakers across 8 Indian languages and 23 professional domains. We design a 7-level prompting framework that progressively introduces contextual signals, including metadata, natural-language descriptions, entity lists in English and native script, and adversarial prompts with incorrect entities. Evaluating five models reveals substantial differences in context utilisation behaviour, highlighting the need for explicit evaluation of contextual grounding in AudioLLMs.

</details>


### [78] [From Bounding Boxes to Visual Reasoning: An On-Policy Data Annotation Tool for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18846)
*Like Zhang, Runliang Niu, Shiqi Wang, Xiyu Hu, Qianli Xing, Pan Wang, Qingzu He, Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScreenAnnotator，一个面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的开放源码数据标注工具。它通过统一的标注原子模式（将空间、语义和结构信息绑定）、基于贝叶斯标注验证器（BAV）的在策略标注循环，以及模板驱动的多任务数据合成流程，解决了现有标注工具表达能力有限、标注-训练解耦严重、数据复用性差三大瓶颈。实验表明，该工具在流程图场景下标注接受率接近100%，GUI截图场景下达77%，且随着标注数据积累，单图标注时间稳步下降；微调后的VLM在流程图任务上平均准确率达76.1%，绝对提升35.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据标注工具无法满足VLM进行精细化结构化视觉推理所需的数据需求，存在三大系统瓶颈：表达能力有限（无法统一空间坐标、开放词汇描述、结构化属性和拓扑关系）、标注-训练严重解耦（标注过程不利用模型反馈）、数据复用性差（不同任务需重复标注）。

Method: 1) 定义统一的标注原子模式（annotation atom schema），将空间、语义和结构基元绑定为单一单元；2) 实现在策略标注循环（on-policy annotation loop），嵌入贝叶斯标注验证器（BAV）来利用模型反馈提升标注质量；3) 设计模板驱动的多任务数据合成流程，将静态原子动态转换为多样化的多维度推理任务，避免重复标注。

Result: 在流程图场景下，标注接受率接近100%，GUI截图场景下达77%；随着标注数据积累，单图标注时间稳步下降；在流程图场景下，微调VLM后平均准确率达76.1%，相比基线绝对提升35.1个百分点。

Conclusion: ScreenAnnotator通过统一标注模式、在策略验证循环和模板驱动合成，有效解决了VLM精细化视觉推理数据标注的瓶颈，显著提升了标注效率和数据质量，并验证了高质量标注数据对VLM性能提升的重要作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种面向视觉语言模型的数据标注工具 ScreenAnnotator，核心贡献在于数据标注与合成流程，属于多模态数据构建与评测基础设施。虽然论文本身不是传统意义上的评测基准，但数据标注工具与多模态评测/基准密切相关（为训练和评估提供数据基础），且 multimodal_benchmark 是 [CANONICAL] 中最接近的现有主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on data annotation tooling for VLM visual reasoning, not directly on core priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM annotation tool, on-policy annotation loop, Bayesian verifier, flowchart/GUI focus, not generation or world model

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are rapidly advancing toward sophisticated grounded structured visual reasoning. Training models for such advanced capabilities demands a new genre of data that seamlessly unifies spatial coordinates, open-vocabulary descriptions, structured attributes, and topological relationships into a singular representation. However, existing data annotation tools fundamentally fail to meet these intricate demands, suffering from three systematic bottlenecks: limited expressiveness, severe annotation-training decoupling, and poor data reusability. To bridge this infrastructure gap, we introduce an open-source annotation tool, ScreenAnnotator. First, we define a unified annotation atom schema that binds spatial, semantic, and structural primitives into a single unit. Second, we implement an on-policy annotation loop embedded with a Bayesian Annotation Verifier (BAV). Finally, we design a template-driven multi-task data synthesis process dynamically transforms static atoms into diverse multi-dimensional reasoning tasks, eliminating redundant re-annotation. The on-policy loop drives the annotation accept rate to nearly 100% on flowcharts and 77% on GUI screenshots, while steadily reducing per-image annotation time as labeled data accumulate. In the flowchart scenario, fine-tuning a VLM yields 76.1% average accuracy, which is a 35.1% point absolute gain. Our code is available at: .

</details>

*Hong-Tao Yu, Chen-Wei Xie, Yuxin Peng, Serge Belongie, Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FG-BMK，一个包含101万问题和28万图像的细粒度图像评测基准，从人类导向和机器导向两个范式评估LVLM的细粒度语义识别和视觉判别能力，并诊断模型失败的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM评测多从整体或特定任务角度出发，对细粒度图像任务（计算机视觉的基础能力）的评估尚不充分，缺乏系统性的诊断分析框架。

Method: 构建FG-BMK基准，包含101万问题和28万图像，覆盖常见物体中心领域到专业领域。通过人类导向（对话级细粒度语义识别）和机器导向（特征级视觉判别性）两种范式联合评估，并设计诊断分析机制区分失败源于视觉表征不足、视觉-语义对齐弱还是细粒度知识有限。

Result: 实验表明当前LVLM在细粒度识别上仍不充分，失败原因涉及视觉表征、语义对齐、模态对齐和类别知识等多个交织的瓶颈。进一步分析了训练设计因素对细粒度能力的影响，以及视觉和语言扰动对预测的影响。

Conclusion: FG-BMK为当前LVLM在细粒度视觉任务上的局限性提供了诊断性见解，为未来数据构建和模型设计提供了指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了FG-BMK基准，用于评估LVLM在细粒度图像任务上的能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Provides diagnostic insights into LVLM fine-grained failures, but is a benchmark paper not directly advancing the user's priority subtopics. Signals: fine-grained benchmark, LVLM evaluation, diagnostic analysis, visual-semantic grounding, not priority subtopic

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal perception and reasoning capabilities. While numerous benchmarks have evaluated LVLMs from holistic or task-specific perspectives, their capabilities on fine-grained image tasks-fundamental to computer vision-remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we introduce FG-BMK, a comprehensive fine-grained evaluation benchmark containing 1.01 million questions and 0.28 million images, covering diverse scenarios from common object-centric domains to specialized domains. FG-BMK jointly evaluates dialogue-level fine-grained semantic recognition and feature-level visual discriminability through human-oriented and machine-oriented paradigms, enabling diagnostic analysis of whether LVLM failures arise from insufficient visual representations, weak visual-to-semantic grounding, or limited fine-grained knowledge. Through extensive experiments on a diverse set of representative LVLMs/VLMs, we find that current LVLMs remain inadequate fine-grained recognizers, with failures arising from intertwined bottlenecks in visual representations, semantic grounding, modality alignment, and category-level knowledge. We further analyze training design factors for improving fine-grained capabilities and examine how visual and linguistic perturbations affect LVLM predictions. These findings provide diagnostic insights into the limitations of current LVLMs and offer guidance for future data construction and model design in developing more reliable LVLMs for fine-grained visual tasks. Our code is open-source and available at .

</details>


### [80] [Beyond the Current Observation: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models in Controllable Non-Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19338)
*Shengyuan Ding, Xilin Wei, Xinyu Fang, Haodong Duan, Dahua Lin, Jiaqi Wang, Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RNG-Bench，一个用于评估多模态大模型在可控非马尔可夫博弈中重建过去观测并据此行动能力的基准套件，包含 Matching Pairs 和 3D Maze 两个互补游戏，通过 Memory Gap 指标分离遗忘与决策错误，实验表明前沿 MLLM 在困难配置下远未饱和，且微调可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么暴露完整状态（混淆隐藏状态重建与其他能力），要么仅在回合结束后测试回忆，无法有效评估多模态基础模型在闭环策略中基于不可见观测进行决策的能力。本文旨在设计一个能隔离并评估模型重建过去观测并据此行动能力的基准。

Method: 提出 RNG-Bench 基准套件，包含两个互补游戏：(1) Matching Pairs——需记忆短暂展示的卡牌位置并匹配；(2) 3D Maze——需将第一视角观测整合为空间地图。设计三个可控难度轴（网格大小、视觉模式、观测模态），引入 head-to-head 对决协议控制实例级方差，以及 Memory Gap 指标分离遗忘与决策错误。最后使用最优策略 rollout 和过滤后的模型演示微调 Qwen3.5-9B。

Result: 最困难配置需要约 128K token 和每回合 350 张图像输入，前沿 MLLM 远未饱和。Memory Gap 分析表明大部分残差错误源于遗忘早期观测而非次优决策。微调 Qwen3.5-9B 在 RNG-Bench 上性能提升，且能迁移到现有基准而不损害通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: RNG-Bench 有效评估了 MLLM 在非马尔可夫博弈中的记忆与决策能力，揭示了当前模型在长上下文记忆方面的显著不足，并为提升模型在该类任务上的表现提供了可行的微调方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RNG-Bench，一个用于评估多模态大模型在非马尔可夫博弈中重建过去观测并据此行动能力的基准套件，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLMs in non-Markov games but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM evaluation benchmark, non-Markov game, memory gap metric, controllable difficulty, fine-tuning improves performance

Abstract: Deploying multimodal foundation models as closed-loop policies increasingly requires conditioning actions on observations that are no longer visible. However, existing benchmarks either expose the full state, conflate hidden-state reconstruction with other agent skills, or test recall only after an episode has ended. We introduce RNG-Bench (Reconstructive Non-Markov Games), a benchmark suite designed to isolate a base model's ability to reconstruct past observations and act on them during multi-step interaction. RNG-Bench includes two complementary games: Matching Pairs, where card identities briefly revealed at specific locations must later be recalled, and 3D Maze, where egocentric views must be integrated into a spatial map. Both games are evaluated under a unified harness with three controlled difficulty axes: grid size, visual pattern, and observation modality. The benchmark further introduces a head-to-head duel protocol to control for instance-level variance and a Memory Gap metric that disentangles forgetting from poor action selection. The hardest configurations require contexts of roughly 128K tokens and 350 image inputs per episode, and remain far from saturated by frontier MLLMs. Memory Gap analysis shows that most residual errors stem from forgetting earlier observations rather than from suboptimal decision making. Finally, fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal-policy rollouts and filtered model demonstrations improves performance on RNG-Bench and transfers to existing benchmarks without degrading general multimodal capability.

</details>

### [81] [Moving Beyond Diversity: Visual Token Pruning as Subspace Reconstruction for Efficient VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18681)
*Jaeyeon Lee, Shunjie Wen, Dong-Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (71.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SPARE 将视觉 token 剪枝重新定义为子空间重构问题，通过迭代选择投影残差大的 token 进行重构驱动剪枝，并引入反相关性机制保留上下文信息。在 LLaVA 上可去除 94% 的视觉 token 同时保留 95% 的基线性能，完全无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多样性最大化的 token 剪枝方法使用余弦归一化相似度，丢弃了幅度信息，无法忠实近似原始特征表示，在组合多技能推理任务上表现欠佳。作者希望提出一种能最小化重构误差的 token 剪枝方法。

Method: 将 token 剪枝形式化为列子集选择问题，通过迭代选择投影残差最大的 token 进行重构驱动剪枝（SPARE）。同时发现反直觉的"反相关性"现象：图像-文本相关性得分较低的 token 反而能更好地保留上下文信息，据此将反相关性作为额外选择标准。

Result: 在多个 VLM 和基准上达到 SOTA 性能，在组合任务上提升显著。在 LLaVA 上去除 94% 视觉 token 仍保留 95% 基线性能，且完全无需训练。

Conclusion: SPARE 通过子空间重构视角重新定义了 token 剪枝问题，超越了仅依赖角度多样性的方法，为高效 VLM 提供了一种训练免费、性能优异的 token 压缩方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SPARE 方法，通过子空间重构进行视觉 token 剪枝，以降低 VLM 的计算开销。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）中的 token 压缩方向，与 canonical 条目高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in multimodal models via a novel reconstruction-driven token pruning method, aligning with high-priority model efficiency and unified multimodal subtopics. Signals: visual token pruning, subspace reconstruction, efficient VLM inference, training-free acceleration, anti-relevance mechanism

Abstract: Despite their remarkable performance, Vision Language Models (VLMs) incur substantial computational overhead due to the large number of visual tokens. While diversity maximization has become a dominant strategy for token reduction, existing methods rely on cosine-based normalized similarity that discards magnitude information, failing to faithfully approximate the original feature representation and leading to suboptimal performance, particularly on compositional multi-skill reasoning tasks. In this paper, we introduce SPARE, a subspace reconstruction method that reformulates token pruning as a column subset selection problem and explicitly minimizes reconstruction error. By iteratively selecting tokens with large projection residuals, SPARE performs reconstruction-driven pruning beyond angular diversity. Moreover, we reveal a counterintuitive anti-relevance phenomenon: tokens with lower image-text relevance score can better preserve contextual information. Based on this finding, we incorporate anti-relevance into SPARE as an additional selection criterion to promote context-aware token selection. Extensive experiments across multiple VLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that SPARE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with strong gains on compositional tasks. When applied to LLaVA, SPARE removes up to 94% of visual tokens while retaining 95% of the baseline performance, all in a fully training-free manner.

</details>


### [82] [A Unified Framework for Efficient Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering: Adapting Dual, Hybrid, and Encoder-Decoder Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19277)
*Timothy Agboada, Shikha Chandel, Yadav Raj Ghimire, Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文提出RS Adapter框架，通过向CLIP、BLIP、FLAVA三种VLM架构注入轻量级bottleneck adapter（仅训练<5%参数），实现遥感VQA的高效适配。实验表明混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理与检索间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感VQA面临高分辨率、多尺度目标分布和语义复杂性的挑战，通用基础模型直接应用存在巨大域偏移且全量微调计算成本过高，亟需高效的参数高效微调方案。

Method: 提出统一的架构适配流程，在冻结的CLIP（双编码器）、BLIP（编码器-解码器）和FLAVA（混合架构）的注意力层和MLP层中注入轻量级bottleneck adapter，仅训练不到5%的参数，在RSVQA-x数据集上进行评估。

Result: 三种适配模型均能收敛，其中混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理与检索能力上优于双编码器和编码器-解码器架构，为灾害评估和城市监测中的资源高效VQA建立了新基线。

Conclusion: PEFT策略可有效将通用VLM适配到遥感领域，混合架构在遥感VQA中展现出最佳的多模态能力平衡，为资源受限场景下的遥感智能分析提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究遥感VQA中基于PEFT（参数高效微调）的VLM适配方法，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Remote sensing VQA is a negative keyword, and the paper's focus on domain-specific PEFT does not align with the user's priority subtopics. Signals: Remote sensing VQA, Parameter-efficient fine-tuning, Bottleneck adapter, CLIP/BLIP/FLAVA adaptation, Low personal relevance due to negative keyword match

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) in the Remote Sensing (RS) domain presents unique challenges due to the high resolution, multi scale object distribution, and semantic complexity of aerial imagery. While general domain Foundation Models have achieved remarkable success, their direct application to RSVQA is hindered by massive domain shifts and the computationally prohibitive nature of full fine tuning. This study presents a comparative analysis of RS Adapter, a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) strategy, applied across three distinct Vision Language Model (VLM) architectures: the Dual Encoder CLIP, the Encoder Decoder BLIP, and the Hybrid FLAVA. We introduce a unified architectural surgery pipeline that injects lightweight bottleneck adapters into the attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with less than 5 percent of trainable parameters. Experimental results on the high resolution RSVQA x dataset demonstrate that while all adapted models achieve convergence, the Hybrid FLAVA architecture offers a superior balance of multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities compared to its unimodal counterparts. Our findings establish a new baseline for resource efficient VQA in disaster assessment and urban monitoring.

</details>

### [83] [Beyond Scalar Scores: Exploring LLM-based Metrics for Clinical Significance Evaluation in Radiology Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18797)
*Qingyu Lu, Ruochen Li, Liang Ding, Yufei Xia, Youxiang Zhu, Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (49.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究放射学报告生成的临床显著性评估问题。现有标量指标无法区分临床显著错误与无害变异。作者使用 ReEvalMed 基准评估 8 种 LLM 评估器，发现存在"歧视偏差"——模型能检测错误但过度惩罚无害改写。通过合成 4k 报告对训练轻量级可解释指标（基于 Qwen3-8B 和 MedGemma-4B），性能超越 32B 规模医学 LLM 并与闭源模型竞争。研究发现两遍推理设置并未一致提升性能，主要是在歧视与鲁棒性之间做权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成评估指标将报告质量简化为无医学依据的标量分数，无法可靠区分临床显著错误与无害变异。需要一种能评估临床显著性的指标。

Method: 使用 ReEvalMed 基准作为测试平台，从"判别力"（检测真实临床错误）和"鲁棒性"（容忍无害变异）两个维度评估指标级临床显著性。在 8 种 LLM 评估器上测试单遍和两遍推理设置。合成 4k 报告对，在 Qwen3-8B 和 MedGemma-4B 上训练轻量级可解释指标。

Result: 训练后的指标在临床显著性边界上表现更优，超越 32B 规模医学 LLM，与闭源模型竞争力相当。两遍推理设置未能一致提升整体性能，主要是在判别力和鲁棒性之间做权衡。

Conclusion: 单遍训练指标是成本敏感部署的实用选择，两遍推理适用于需要精细平衡判别力与鲁棒性的场景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究放射学报告生成的临床显著性评估指标，属于生成模型评测范畴。论文使用 ReEvalMed 基准，提出新的评估指标并训练轻量级评估模型，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical radiology report evaluation, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative keywords include clinical. Signals: LLM-based metrics, clinical significance, radiology reports, discrimination bias, lightweight trained metrics

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of generated radiology reports requires strict clinical accuracy, as omitted critical findings or mischaracterized radiographic observations can directly affect patient care. Existing metrics obscure this requirement by reducing report quality to a medically ungrounded scalar. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) possess rich medical knowledge, they likewise struggle to draw a reliable boundary between clinically significant errors and harmless variation. We study this boundary using ReEvalMed benchmark as testbed and evaluate metric-level clinical significance from detecting true clinical errors ("Discrimination") and tolerating insignificant variations ("Robustness"). Across 8 LLM evaluators under one-pass and two-pass settings, we identify a widespread discrimination bias: models effectively detect errors but also over-penalize harmless rephrasings. To mitigate this, we synthesize 4k report pairs and train lightweight interpretable metrics on Qwen3-8B and MedGemma-4B. Our trained metric sharpens the clinical significance boundary, surpassing 32B-scale medical LLMs and remaining competitive with proprietary models. Crucially, the more costly two-pass setting fails to consistently improve overall performance and mainly trades discrimination for robustness. These findings suggest one-pass trained metrics as the practical choice for cost-sensitive deployment, with two-pass inference reserved for settings where D-R balance is critical. We will release the dataset and metric.

</details>

### [84] [OneCanvas: 3D Scene Understanding via Panoramic Reprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19253)
*Bartłomiej Baranowski, Dave Zhenyu Chen, Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OneCanvas 提出一种无需复杂几何编码器的 3D 场景理解方法：将多视图 patch 特征通过深度和相机位姿反投影到 3D 世界坐标，再映射到以任意位姿为中心的全景画布上，并添加 3D 位置编码保留深度信息。预训练 VLM 可直接将其作为普通图像处理。该方法在 SQA3D 和 VSI-Bench 上达到 SOTA，且训练计算量比最强方法低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 的 3D 场景理解要么依赖复杂的模型特定几何编码器，要么需要大量训练预算来追求空间推理能力。作者希望设计一种轻量、通用的表示方法，使预训练 VLM 无需架构修改即可直接进行 3D 空间推理。

Method: OneCanvas 的核心是将所有视图的 patch 特征聚合到单个等距柱状全景画布上。具体地：1) 每个 patch 利用其深度和相机位姿反投影到 3D 世界坐标；2) 将该点相对于画布原点的连续经纬度映射到画布上，无需光栅化或跨视图聚合；3) 为每个 patch 添加其度量坐标的 3D 位置编码，以恢复将世界坐标压缩为角度坐标时丢失的深度信息。画布可围绕任意感兴趣位姿中心化，支持从特定视角进行情境推理。此外，作者还设计了空间预训练课程：通过程序化地将真实图像中物体的 patch 特征放置在空画布上的选定 3D 世界位置，生成覆盖广泛空间推理任务的监督信号。

Result: OneCanvas 在 SQA3D 和 VSI-Bench 上达到最先进准确率，在 SPBench 上泛化到分布外数据，且训练计算量比最强竞争方法低一个数量级。

Conclusion: OneCanvas 证明了一种简洁的全景重投影表示方法，无需复杂几何编码器或大规模训练即可赋予 VLM 强大的 3D 场景理解与空间推理能力，特别适用于机器人学和具身 AI 中的情境推理需求。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OneCanvas，通过全景重投影将多视图 patch 特征映射到统一等距柱状画布上，使预训练 VLM 能够进行 3D 场景理解与空间推理。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与 3D 多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为论文核心是 3D 场景理解与空间推理，且面向机器人/具身场景。

Importance reason: Novel 3D scene understanding method with SOTA results and high efficiency, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous/multimodal models and generation. Signals: 3D scene understanding, panoramic reprojection, SOTA on SQA3D/VSI-Bench, order-of-magnitude compute reduction, no geometry encoder needed

Abstract: Existing approaches to 3D scene understanding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) either rely on complex, model-specific geometry encoders or large training budgets in pursuit of spatial reasoning. Instead, OneCanvas aggregates patch features from all views onto a single equirectangular panoramic canvas. Namely, each patch is unprojected to a 3D world coordinate using its depth and camera pose, then placed on the canvas at the continuous longitude and latitude of that point as seen from the canvas origin, with no rasterization or aggregation across overlapping views. A 3D position embedding of the patch's metric coordinates is added to its feature, restoring the depth lost when collapsing the world position to an angular canvas coordinate. Patches from all frames thus share one spatial coordinate system with no fusion or major architectural modifications of the backbone. The pretrained VLM consumes this representation as if it were an ordinary image. Because the canvas can be centered on any pose of interest, the same representation directly supports situated reasoning from a specific viewpoint, a common requirement in robotics and embodied AI. Thanks to this representation, we can also introduce a spatial pretraining curriculum: by procedurally placing patch features of objects, drawn from real images, at chosen 3D world positions on an otherwise empty canvas, we generate on-the-fly supervision spanning a broad range of spatial reasoning tasks, with answer distributions controlled to reduce spatial reasoning shortcuts. OneCanvas achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SQA3D and VSI-Bench, and generalizes to out-of-distribution data on SPBench, using an order of magnitude less training compute than the strongest competing methods.

</details>

### [85] [NAVI-Orbital: First In-Orbit Demonstration of a Zero-Shot Vision-Language Model for Autonomous Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18271)
*Juan Manuel Delfa Victoria, Taran Cyriac John, Andrew W. Herson*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (83.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NAVI-Orbital 首次在低地球轨道卫星上部署了零样本视觉语言模型（Gemma 3），实现完全在轨的自主多模态推理、场景分类、文本描述生成和自然语言对话，通过语义压缩颠覆了传统"先采集再下传"的带宽模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测数据生成速度远超下行带宽和人工处理能力，导致星上采集与地面情报之间存在巨大鸿沟。传统"采集-下传-处理"模式带宽瓶颈严重，亟需在轨智能处理实现语义压缩。

Method: 在 LEO 卫星上部署 Gemma 3 视觉语言模型，使用 LangGraph 图状态机协调检测和对话两个专用智能体。系统通过自然语言提示重新配置任务，无需传统指令序列。在轨推理使用硬件加速 GPU，无需针对飞行仪器进行微调。

Result: 地面基准测试（AID 数据集 7,960 张图像）准确率 88.16%；通过 Flatsat 验证和真实在轨捕获的未见地球影像验证了可行性，包括未校正的 YAM-9 影像。

Conclusion: 在卫星级边缘计算机上运行基础模型是可行的，能够通过语义压缩实现地球观测数据的在轨智能处理，逆转传统带宽模式。

Classification reason: 该论文在轨部署了 Gemma 3 视觉语言模型进行自主多模态推理，属于通用视觉语言模型在轨应用，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general (视觉语言模型与 MLLM)" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel in-orbit deployment of a VLM for autonomous Earth observation, but the application domain (remote sensing) is a negative keyword and the core contribution is not in the user's priority subtopics. Signals: First in-orbit VLM demonstration, zero-shot multimodal inference on satellite, semantic compression for bandwidth reduction, uses Gemma 3 and LangGraph, remote sensing application

Abstract: As Earth Observation data generation outpaces downlink bandwidth and human-in-the-loop processing, a widening gap has emerged between onboard collection and actionable ground intelligence. This paper presents NAVI-Orbital, a software system deployed on a Low Earth Orbit (LEO) spacecraft. On April 16, 2026, NAVI-Orbital achieved what is, to the authors' knowledge, the first in-orbit demonstration of a vision-language model performing autonomous multi-modal inference entirely onboard. NAVI-Orbital uses a local vision-language model (Gemma 3) to classify each captured scene, produce a text description of its content and the relationships between its features, and respond to operator follow-up via natural-language dialogue. The system is re-tasked through plain-English prompts in place of conventional command sequences, and is orchestrated by a graph-based state machine (LangGraph) coordinating dedicated agents for detection and dialogue. Results across ground benchmarking (88.16% accuracy on the 7,960-image curated AID benchmark), Flatsat validation, and live in-orbit captures of newly acquired, previously unseen Earth imagery (including uncorrected YAM-9 imagery, processed onboard with hardware-accelerated GPU inference and no fine-tuning for the flight instrument) demonstrate the feasibility of running foundation models on satellite-class edge computers to invert the conventional acquire-then-downlink-everything bandwidth profile through semantic compression of Earth observations in-orbit.

</details>


### [86] [User as Engram: Internalizing Per-User Memory as Local Parametric Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19172)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文提出 User as Engram 方法，将用户个性化记忆以局部参数编辑（Engram 行）的方式存储于语言模型内部，而非外部检索或全局 LoRA 适配器。该方法将内容记忆与推理技能分离：用户事实以稀疏局部编辑写入 Engram 模型的哈希键控记忆表，推理技能由共享适配器承载。相比 per-user LoRA，该方法在间接推理准确率上平均提升 5.6 倍，内存占用减少约 33,000 倍，且多个用户的编辑可无损叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 个性化方法要么将用户事实存储在外部（记忆文件或检索索引），要么使用 per-user LoRA 将事实写入模型权重。前者在事实数量增多时检索效率下降，后者将内容与技能混入全局权重增量，会污染无关文本。受大脑记忆机制启发（海马体存储稀疏局部记忆印迹，新皮层存储共享技能），作者希望设计一种既能精确存储用户事实、又不影响模型其他能力的个性化方法。

Method: 基于 Engram 模型架构，将用户内容记忆存储为对哈希键控记忆表的局部参数编辑（Engram 行），每个事实对应一个精确的局部编辑，仅在触发位置激活查找并添加所需值，其他位置保持完全不变。推理技能则存储在一个共享的适配器中。不同用户的事实落在不相交的哈希槽中，因此多个用户的编辑可以无损叠加在同一张表中。

Result: 相比 per-user LoRA，User as Engram 在间接推理准确率上平均提升 5.6 倍，内存占用减少约 33,000 倍，且不会降低任何用户在基础模型上的推理能力。当事实数量超过约 100 条时，其检索效率超过使用 2.5 倍更大模型的检索管道。

Conclusion: User as Engram 通过将用户记忆以局部参数编辑的方式内化到模型中，实现了比 LoRA 更精确、更高效、可无损叠加的个性化记忆方案。这种内容与技能分离的设计更接近生物大脑的记忆机制，为 LLM 个性化提供了一种新的范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 User as Engram 方法，通过将用户记忆以局部参数编辑（Engram 模型）的方式内化到语言模型中，属于语言模型个性化与记忆增强方向。这与多模态理解与生成方向下的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）存在关联，因为 Engram 模型本身是一种可编辑的 LLM/MLLM 架构，且论文讨论的是 LLM 个性化记忆问题，属于通用语言模型/多模态模型架构范畴。虽然论文不直接涉及视觉，但其核心的 Engram 架构与参数编辑思想可应用于多模态模型，且现有 taxonomy 中没有更精确匹配的 subtopic。

Importance reason: Novel memory editing approach but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: local parametric edits, per-user memory, engram model, hash-keyed memory table, indirect reasoning improvement

Abstract: Personal memory in a language model is two problems: content and reasoning skill. The brain keeps the two apart (a sparse, local engram in the hippocampus for each episode, a slow neocortex for the shared skills that interpret it), so a new fact need not overwrite everything else. Most personalization today keeps a user's facts outside the weights, in a natural-language memory file or a retrieval index. When facts are written into the model instead, the standard recipe is the per-user LoRA adapter, which does the opposite of the brain, folding content and skill into one global weight delta. Writing a user's facts as a LoRA contaminates text unrelated to them; writing the same facts as local Engram rows leaves it mathematically untouched, resulting in a roughly 33,000x smaller memory footprint. We therefore propose User as Engram: store a user's content as surgical edits to the hash-keyed memory table of an Engram model, and carry the reasoning skill in one shared adapter. This layered design matches per-user LoRA's direct recall while delivering 5.6x higher indirect-reasoning accuracy on average, and never makes a single user worse at reasoning than the untouched base. The edit is a glass box: writing a fact switches on its lookup at exactly the trigger, adds the value the answer needs, leaves every other position unchanged to the last bit, and fails if written into the wrong layer. Because different users' facts land in disjoint hash slots, their edits compose: many users live in one shared table at once, stacking additively and losslessly, where a per-user LoRA, a single global weight delta, admits only one. Upon retrieval, a per-user Engram table does not grow with the population the retriever must search, so past ~100 facts it overtakes a retrieval pipeline on a 2.5x larger model.

</details>


### [87] [AMALIA-VL: A Native European Portuguese Open-Source Vision and Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19100)
*Diogo Glória-Silva, João Cardeira, Manuel Letras da Luz, Afonso Simplício, Gonçalo Vinagre, Diogo Tavares, Rafael Ferreira, Inês Calvo, Inês Vieira, David Semedo, João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AMALIA-VL 是首个面向欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的开源指令微调视觉语言模型，采用高分辨率视觉编码器与动态图像分块，通过三阶段训练（视觉-语言对齐、通用视觉指令微调、偏好优化）和 pt-PT 多模态数据混合构建，为欧洲葡萄牙语多模态模型建立了强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源多模态模型严重缺乏对欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的支持，要么将其与巴西葡萄牙语混为一谈，要么在训练数据中严重不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建首个原生支持 pt-PT 的开源 LVLM。

Method: 采用高分辨率视觉编码器配合动态图像分块（dynamic image tiling），通过可学习的连接器与完全开放的 pt-PT 优化语言模型对接。设计了三阶段训练流程：1）视觉-语言对齐；2）通用视觉指令微调；3）偏好优化。同时构建了以 pt-PT 为中心的多模态数据混合，包括策划和翻译的公开数据集以及新创建的数据集。

Result: AMALIA-VL 在多个多模态基准上建立了欧洲葡萄牙语开源模型的强基线。作者将公开模型权重、训练数据、构建流程以及机器翻译的 pt-PT 评测基准。

Conclusion: AMALIA-VL 成功填补了欧洲葡萄牙语多模态模型的空白，通过原生构建而非简单翻译的方式，为低资源语言的多模态 AI 发展提供了可复现的范式和资源。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个面向欧洲葡萄牙语的开放源码视觉语言模型（LVLM），涉及视觉编码器、语言模型连接、三阶段训练（对齐、指令微调、偏好优化）以及多模态数据构建。这属于通用视觉语言模型与MLLM的范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specific language (European Portuguese) and lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: European Portuguese LVLM, open-source, high-resolution vision encoder, dynamic image tiling, three-stage training

Abstract: Large Vision and Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced rapidly, yet European Portuguese (pt-PT) remains systematically underserved by existing open-source multimodal models, which either conflate it with Brazilian Portuguese or severely under-represent it in their training data mixes. We introduce AMALIA-VL, the first open-source instruction-tuned LVLM built natively for pt-PT, pairing a high-resolution vision encoder with dynamic image tiling and a fully open pt-PT-optimized language model via a learned connector. We contribute with a purposefully designed three-stage training process - vision-language alignment, general visual instruction tuning, and preference optimization - together with a pt-PT-centric multimodal data mix combining curated and translated public datasets with novel datasets that address the near-total absence of European Portuguese multimodal resources. Our evaluation shows that AMALIA-VL establishes a strong baseline for open-source pt-PT will release model weights, training data, and construction pipelines along with machine-translated pt-PT evaluation benchmarks to help democratize pt-PT LVLM development.

</details>

### [88] [PorTEXTO: A European Portuguese Benchmark for Visual Text Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19096)
*João Cardeira, Diogo Glória-Silva, Manuel Letras da Luz, Rafael Ferreira, Diogo Tavares, David Semedo, João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PorTEXTO 是首个面向现代欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）场景的视觉文本提取基准，涵盖合成与真实世界样本。通过前沿 LVLM 转录加母语者审核的标注流程确保质量。实验发现合成到真实样本性能显著下降，且多语言专用数据比模型规模或分辨率对 pt-PT OCR 性能影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）在现有 OCR 基准中几乎缺失，已有的少数基准仅关注历史文献，缺乏对现代 OCR 应用（如日常场景文本）的覆盖。

Method: 构建 PorTEXTO 基准，包含合成和真实世界样本；采用前沿 LVLM 转录加母语者人工审核的标注流程保证质量；在多个 OCR 模型上进行系统评测。

Result: 合成样本到真实样本性能显著下降；多语言专用数据比模型规模或分辨率对 pt-PT OCR 性能贡献更大。

Conclusion: 当前多语言专用数据是提升 pt-PT OCR 性能的关键因素，呼吁社区发布开放的 pt-PT OCR 资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的视觉文本提取基准 PorTEXTO，属于OCR/文档理解范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specific language OCR benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: European Portuguese OCR benchmark, synthetic-to-real performance gap, multilingual data importance, no connection to continuous language models or video generation

Abstract: European Portuguese (pt-PT) is largely absent from OCR benchmarks, which skew toward high-resource languages. The few benchmarks that cover pt-PT focus on historical artifacts and literature. This work addresses modern OCR applications, introducing PorTEXTO, the first benchmark for contemporary and culturally relevant pt-PT visual text extraction. To ascertain quality, we employ an annotation pipeline combining transcriptions from a frontier LVLM with exhaustive review by native speakers. We observe a sharp performance drop from synthetic to real world samples in most models, and find that, currently, specialized multilingual data is a better driver for pt-PT performance than model size or resolution budget, motivating the release of open pt-PT OCR resources.

</details>

## 视频理解与时序推理 [[Back]](#toc)

视频 LLM、视频问答、时序定位、长视频理解、第一视角视频。

### [89] [APT: Atomic Physical Transitions for Causal Video-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18586)
*Shang Wu, Haoran Lu, Songling Liu, Chenwei Xu, Lie Lu, Pranav Maneriker, Fan Du, Manling Li, Zhaoran Wang, Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (66.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Atomic Physical Transitions (APTs)概念，将视频中的物理事件分解为最小因果状态变化链（如支撑丢失→接触开始→反弹→稳定），并构建了包含14种物理过渡类型、27,303个标注实例的数据集。实验发现现有VLM对物理过渡的零样本召回率仅14%。作者提出APT-Tune方法，通过图像填充感知监督、格式条件协同训练和机制条件域到类型解码，仅用11M LoRA参数在Qwen3-VL-2B上显著提升APT检测能力，同时保持事件级视频问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型仅关注事件级标签（如"弹跳"），忽略了构成物理事件因果链的细粒度状态变化过程。作者希望让VLM能够理解"为什么"事件发生，而不仅仅是"发生了什么"。

Method: 1) 定义APT概念：最小、时序局部的状态变化，绑定可见线索、活跃物理机制和前后动力学状态。2) 构建混合源APT数据集：结合人类标注和模拟器真值，覆盖接触、重力、摩擦、旋转/稳定4大类14种过渡类型。3) 提出APT-Tune微调方法：包含图像填充感知监督（image-pad-aware supervision）、格式条件协同训练（format-conditional co-training）和机制条件域到类型解码（mechanism-conditioned domain-to-type decoding），在Qwen3-VL-2B上仅用11M LoRA参数。

Result: APT-Tune显著提升APT检测召回率，同时保持事件级视频问答性能，避免了直接微调导致的灾难性遗忘。实验表明APT不仅是新的答案格式，更是与人类对齐的因果监督信号。

Conclusion: APT提供了一种将物理事件分解为因果状态变化链的框架，可作为VLM物理视频理解的有效监督信号。APT-Tune证明了参数高效微调可以在不牺牲事件级理解的前提下，赋予模型细粒度物理因果推理能力。

Classification reason: 该论文关注视频中的因果物理状态变化（Atomic Physical Transitions），旨在提升VLM对视频中物理事件因果链的理解能力，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴。论文使用Qwen3-VL进行微调，涉及视频问答和物理事件检测，与[视频理解与时序推理]（video_understanding_temporal）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: Introduces a novel causal video understanding framework (APTs) that aligns with the user's priority on continuous multimodal reasoning and world models. Signals: causal video understanding, atomic physical transitions, continuous multimodal reasoning, world model alignment, parameter-efficient fine-tuning

Abstract: Physical events are not understood by their names alone, but by the causal state changes that compose them. A clip-level label such as "bounce" can be correct while hiding the process that makes the event physically valid, from support loss and contact onset to rebound and settling. To make this hidden process explicit, we introduce Atomic Physical Transitions (APTs): minimal, temporally localized state changes that bind a visible cue to an active physical mechanism and before/after dynamical regimes. An APT chain represents a video as an ordered causal transition sequence rather than a single aggregate event label: event labels tell what happened; APT chains explain why it happened. To make APTs learnable by VLMs, we construct mixed-source APT data from human annotations and simulator ground truth, covering 14 transition types across contact, gravity, friction, and rotation/stability, with 27,303 timed instances over 1,246 trials. Using this data, we find that current VLMs miss transition-level physics, with zero-shot recall at most 14% and errors dominated by missed transitions. Direct fine-tuning on APT chains improves transition detection but causes event-level forgetting, indicating that the model learns a specialized answer format rather than a reusable physical representation. We therefore propose APT-Tune, a parameter-efficient recipe that teaches VLMs to use causal transitions without forgetting how to answer video questions. It combines image-pad-aware supervision, format-conditional co-training, and mechanism-conditioned domain-to-type decoding to make APT learning format-robust and physically grounded. With only 11 M LoRA parameters on Qwen3-VL-2B, APT-Tune substantially improves APT recall while also improving event-level video transfer. These results show that APTs are not a new answer format, but a human-aligned causal supervision signal for physical video understanding.

</details>
